### [1] [Optimum foraging area in a three-trophic food chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18609)
*Lucas Massoni,Rafael Menezes,Marcus A. M. de Aguiar,Sabrina B. L. Araujo*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过元胞自动机和主方程（平均场与成对近似）研究三营养级捕食者-猎物模型中多觅食区域对种群动态的影响，发现最优觅食区域并不最大化种群密度，且存在多态性：一组最优策略可共存，这一范围由进化稳定策略决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物觅食策略受搜索范围与局部捕获效率之间的权衡影响，个体倾向于将觅食区域调整至最优值，从而影响种群动态。作者希望探究在多个觅食区域并存时，这种权衡如何塑造三营养级捕食者-猎物系统的种群密度与策略演化。

Method: 构建由三个营养级组成的捕食者-猎物模型，捕食者与猎物仅在捕食者有限邻域内相互作用，且适应可跨世代发生；假设局部捕食效率与觅食区域成反比。分别用元胞自动机进行数值模拟，并用主方程的平均场近似和成对近似进行解析分析；进一步采用自适应动力学（Adaptive Dynamics）框架分析策略稳定性。

Result: 平均场近似无法重现模拟中种群密度对觅食区域的依赖，而成对近似能较好再现该依赖关系。模拟表明，最优觅食区域并不使种群密度最大。此外，系统出现多态种群，即并非单一最优策略，而是一组最优策略可以共存。自适应动力学分析确认，最优区域范围并非最大化种群规模的范围，而是能够入侵其他种群且不被入侵的进化稳定策略（ESS）。

Conclusion: 在三营养级捕食者-猎物系统中，觅食区域的进化结果由进化稳定策略而非种群密度最大化决定；多态共存与成对近似的预测一致，说明空间局部相互作用和策略多样性对种群动态具有重要影响。

Abstract: Organisms' foraging strategies are shaped by a trade-off between search area and local capture efficiency. This trade-off leads individuals to adapt their foraging area to an optimal value, impacting population dynamics. Here we study the effect of multiple foraging areas in a predator-prey model composed of three trophic levels. The interactions between predators and prey occur only within a limited neighborhood of the predators, where adaptation can occur over generations. We assume a trade-off where local predation efficiency is inversely proportional to the foraging area. These dynamics were implemented computationally via cellular automata and analytically via Master Equations with mean-field and pair approximations. Unlike the mean-field approximation, the pair approximation reproduced the dependence of population density on foraging area observed in the simulations. However, the simulations showed that the optimal foraging area does not maximize population density. Moreover, we found that a polymorphic population emerged, where not a single optimal strategy but a range of optimal strategies can coexist. Using the framework of Adaptive Dynamics, we confirm that the range of optimal areas is not the one that maximizes population size, but the Evolutionary Stable Strategy that can invade a population and not be invaded.

</details>

### [2] [What does "instant thermalization" in large-$q$ SYK models mean?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17794)
*Alexander Osterkorn,Jan C. Louw*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究q/2体相互作用SYK模型的量子热化速率，发现其呈现Planckian行为但速率为Γ∼Tq⁻¹（而非此前猜测的Γ∼Tq），并指出瞬时热化仅对t₁,t₂>0的块有意义，非对角块本质上非热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受两体相互作用SYK模型中观测到的Planckian热化速率Γ∼T的启发，作者希望研究q/2体情形下的热化行为。此前研究已建立1/q领头阶的"瞬时热化"概念，并猜测热化仍是Planckian但速率发散，为Γ∼Tq，以此解释"瞬时"现象，本文旨在验证这一猜想。

Method: 选取一个可解析且可数值求解的系统，计算t=0时刻quench后的有效温度；同时对比分析因果Green函数𝒢(t₁,t₂)在两时间平面不同块中的行为，并重点研究非热关联的时间依赖性。

Result: 计算得到的有效温度确实呈现Planckian速率，但速率为Γ∼Tq⁻¹，即随q衰减，与预期的Γ∼Tq发散行为相反。而因果Green函数𝒢(t₁,t₂)在t₁,t₂>0的对角块中立即获得热形式。

Conclusion: 瞬时热化仅对t₁,t₂>0的块是有意义的概念，而t₁·t₂<0的非对角块在任何q下本质上都是非热的。由于有效温度由跨越所有块的关联决定，它继承了一个有限速率，该速率由非对角块决定。

Abstract: Motivated by the Planckian thermalization rate $Γ\sim T$ observed in the two-body interacting SYK model, we study thermalization in the $q/2$-body case. Previous studies of such systems have established the notion of instantaneous thermalization to leading order in $1/q$. It was conjectured that the thermalization may still be Planckian but with a divergent rate $Γ\sim T q$, explaining the ``instantaneous'' part. For an analytically and numerically tractable system, we calculate the effective temperatures after a quench at time $t = 0$ and indeed find a Planckian rate, albeit with the unexpected decaying behavior $Γ\sim T q^{-1} $. This is contrasted with the behavior of the causal Green's function $\mathcal{G}(t_1, t_2)$, which instantly acquires a thermal form in the two-time plane block $t_1, t_2 > 0$.
  The resulting picture is that instant thermalization is a meaningful concept only for this block, while the off-diagonal blocks $t_1 \cdot t_2 < 0$ are inherently non-thermal at any $q$. Since the effective temperature is obtained from correlations spanning all blocks, it inherits a finite rate set by the off-diagonal. We illustrate this directly by studying the non-thermal correlations' time dependence.

</details>


### [3] [Assessing the Reliability of Anomalous Hall Conductivity Extraction in GdAlSi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17872)
*Anil Kumar,Debapratim Pal,Sudhan Koirala,Muhammad Adnan,Youngsang,Ji,Prakash Regmi,Bailey S. Bouley,Ludi Miao,Yun Suk Eo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文比较了提取反常霍尔电导率的不同方法，以SrRuO₃和GdAlSi为例，指出在背景与非线性响应复杂时，数据处理方式会导致反常霍尔电导率估计出现显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常霍尔电导率的提取通常依赖对普通霍尔背景的扣除及电阻率-电导率转换，但在某些量子材料中横向响应不饱和、线性和非线性区域难以界定，方法选择可能严重影响结果可靠性，因此有必要系统比较不同处理方式。

Method: 对比在电阻率到电导率转换前后扣除普通霍尔贡献两种方式；以SrRuO₃（普通霍尔背景明确、磁电导小）与GdAlSi（横向响应无明显饱和、低场和高场区域均近似线性）为对象；分析将不同线性区域解释为普通霍尔背景，以及纵向电阻处理方式对反常霍尔电导率估计的影响；并检验非线性霍尔响应能否在普通霍尔框架内描述而不引入反常贡献。

Result: 在SrRuO₃中，无论转换前还是转换后扣除普通霍尔贡献，得到的反常霍尔电导率几乎一致。在GdAlSi中，由于横向响应不饱和且低场、高场区域均近似线性，不同线性区域归属及纵向电阻在电阻率-电导率转换中的处理会产生完全不同的反常霍尔电导率估计；非线性霍尔响应也可在普通霍尔框架下得到描述。

Conclusion: 提取量子材料中的额外霍尔贡献时需谨慎：在横向响应不饱和或背景界定不清的情况下，普通霍尔背景扣除方式与纵向电阻转换处理会显著影响反常霍尔电导率结果，应明确方法假设并评估替代解释。

Abstract: We examine the different methods of extracting the anomalous Hall conductivity using SrRuO$_3$ and GdAlSi. For SrRuO$_3$, where the ordinary Hall background is well defined and the magnetoconductance is small, subtracting the ordinary Hall contribution either before or after conversion from resistivity to conductivity yields nearly identical anomalous Hall conductivities. In GdAlSi, in contrast, the transverse response does not exhibit clear saturation, and both low- and high-field regions can appear approximately linear. We show that different interpretations of these linear regions as the ordinary Hall background, together with the treatment of the measured longitudinal resistance in the resistivity-to-conductivity conversion, can produce completely different estimates of the anomalous Hall conductivity. We also examine whether the nonlinear Hall response can instead be described within an ordinary Hall framework without invoking an anomalous contribution. Our analysis provides practical cautions for extracting excess Hall contributions in quantum materials.

</details>


### [4] [Optical investigation of the electronic structure of a ferromagnetic Weyl semimetal CeAlSi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18592)
*Shin-ichi Kimura,Yue Pan,Hiroshi Watanabe,Akimitsu Kirikoshi,Junya Otsuki,Hiroshi Tanida*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过测量CeAlSi在铁磁转变前后的温变光电导谱并与DFT+DMFT能带计算对比，发现Ce 4f态近乎局域，Weyl费米子与Ce 4f态相互作用极弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究磁性Weyl半金属CeAlSi在铁磁转变过程中电子态的行为，特别是Ce 4f态与Weyl费米子之间的相互作用强度。

Method: 测量温度依赖的光电导谱σ₁(ω)，并与DFT+DMFT能带计算进行对比；利用扩展Drude模型评估动态有效质量与散射概率。

Result: σ₁(ω)谱在铁磁有序温度T_C处无显著变化，表明Ce 4f态近乎局域；DFT+DMFT计算成功重现了谱形及跨T_C的不变谱；动态有效质量很小，计算亦可重现，但更低温度下的散射概率暗示存在铁磁涨落。

Conclusion: Weyl费米子与Ce 4f态之间的相互作用强度非常弱，与DFT+DMFT计算结果一致。

Abstract: To investigate electronic states during the ferromagnetic transition in a magnetic Weyl semimetal CeAlSi, we measured temperature-dependent optical conductivity [$σ_1(ω)$] spectra and compared them with DFT+DMFT band calculations. The $σ_1(ω)$ spectrum did not change significantly across the ferromagnetic ordering temperature ($T_C$), suggesting that the Ce 4f states are almost localized. DFT+DMFT calculations with almost localized Ce 4f states successfully reproduced the spectral shape and the unchanged $σ_1(ω)$ spectra across $T_C$. The dynamic effective mass evaluated from the extended Drude model is very small, which DFT+DMFT calculations also reproduce, but the scattering probability at even lower temperatures suggests ferromagnetic fluctuations. These results suggest that the interaction intensity between the Weyl fermions and Ce 4f states is very weak, as reproduced by DFT+DMFT calculations.

</details>


### [5] [Orbital-Induced Peierls Transitions: How Orbitals Orchestrate Lattice Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18614)
*T. Mizokawa,S. V. Streltsov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了轨道自由度诱导的高维Peierls不稳定性，梳理了其物理机制、典型材料、高温未畸变相特征及局域效应（如分子轨道形成）的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知将Peierls转变视为一维材料特有的现象，但轨道自由度能在更高维度中诱导该不稳定性，这一机制缺乏系统性梳理与总结，因此需要一篇综述来整合相关物理图像与实验材料。

Method: 采用综述方式，归纳两类主要机制：（1）p、d轨道各向异性导致电子能谱的"有效一维化"；（2）轨道自由度通过局域或能带Jahn-Teller效应使能带相对移动、解除简并，从而改变费米面嵌套；并结合配位八面体的共边（常见）与共面（较少见）几何进行材料分类讨论。

Result: 指出轨道诱导Peierls效应最常出现在过渡金属周围配体八面体共边的化合物中，共面几何中较少见；并总结了高温未畸变相的性质与局域效应（如分子轨道形成）的作用。

Conclusion: 轨道自由度可将Peierls不稳定性推广到高维体系，其发生受轨道形状各向异性、Jahn-Teller效应及配体八面体连接方式共同控制，局域效应在理解该现象中起重要作用。

Abstract: The Peierls transition is typically regarded as a phenomenon inherent to one-dimensional (1D) materials. However, orbital degrees of freedom can induce this instability even in higher dimensions. Two mechanisms are primarily responsible. First, the anisotropic shape of $p$ and $d$ orbitals can lead to effective "1D-zation" of the electronic spectrum. Second, orbital degrees of freedom can lift band degeneracy by shifting bands relative to each other via the local or band Jahn-Teller effect, thereby affecting the nesting of the Fermi surface. The orbital-induced Peierls effect is most commonly observed when ligand octahedra surrounding transition metals share edges, and less frequently in face-sharing geometries. In this review, we discuss the underlying physical mechanisms, the materials in which this phenomenon occurs, the characteristics of the high-temperature undistorted phase, and the role of local effects such as the formation of molecular orbitals.

</details>


### [6] [Electronic correlations shape the low-energy optical response of the kagome antiferromagnets Mn$_3$Sn and Mn$_3$Ge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18744)
*R. Mathew Roy,Bo Tai,Maxim Wenzel,Achyut Tiwari,Mykhaylo Ozerov,Chandra Shekhar,Claudia Felser,Artem V. Pronin,Xiaolong Feng,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过光谱与DFT+U计算，揭示Mn₃Sn的光学性质受强电子关联主导，其光学有效质量约为Mn₃Ge的三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn₃Sn作为kagome反铁磁体，其电子关联效应对拓扑金属性质的影响尚不明确，需厘清光学响应来源。

Method: 光学光谱测量结合密度泛函理论（DFT+U，Lichtenstein形式，U=4 eV，J=0.25 eV），并施加高达17 T磁场对比Mn₃Sn与同构Mn₃Ge。

Result: Mn₃Sn低能带间跃迁仅由关联修正的能带结构产生，光学有效质量约为Mn₃Ge三倍；Mn₃Sn需Hubbard修正拟合，Mn₃Ge无需（U,J=0）；σ₁(ω)线性区来自多条带间跃迁叠加，非Weyl锥光学特征；17 T内光学响应不变。

Conclusion: 确立Mn₃Sn与Mn₃Ge的强关联图像，为其他关联拓扑金属提供研究模板。

Abstract: Using optical spectroscopy and density functional theory, we provide evidence that electron-electron interactions strongly affect the properties of the kagome antiferromagnet Mn$_3$Sn, since its low-energy optical interband transitions arise exclusively from the correlation-modified electronic band structure. Mn$_3$Sn possesses an optical effective mass about three times larger than that of its isostructural analog Mn$_3$Ge. The DFT+$U$ treatment in the Lichtenstein formulation, with an on-site Coulomb repulsion $U = 4$~eV and Hund's coupling $J = 0.25$~eV, accounts for the optical transitions in Mn$_3$Sn, whereas those in Mn$_3$Ge are already reproduced without Hubbard corrections ($U, J = 0$). The near-isotropic electronic structure of Mn$_3$Sn reveals a three-dimensional metal whose electronic response is shaped prominently by the Mn $d$-states. The apparent linear regime in $σ_1(ω)$ observed in Mn$_3$Sn and Mn$_3$Ge arises from several overlapping interband transitions and therefore should not be interpreted as the optical signature of the Weyl cones. The optical response does not alter with magnetic field up to 17 T, consistent with strong free-carrier screening. Our findings establish a comprehensive-correlated picture of Mn$_3$Sn and Mn$_3$Ge that offers a template for other correlated topological metals.

</details>


### [7] [Exact and fast series expansions for quantum models with long-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18761)
*Antonia Duft,Patrick Adelhardt,Jan Alexander Koziol,Andreas A. Buchheit,Kai Phillip Schmidt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出"图zeta方法"，用确定性图zeta函数与张量网络分块求值取代高维格点求和的蒙特卡洛计算，将高阶关联簇展开加速为可在桌面硬件上分钟级完成，并应用于KTmSe₂模型的相互作用判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年，基于关联簇方法的高阶级数展开已成为研究长程相互作用能隙量子系统低能性质的重要工具，但其核心计算瓶颈在于对高维格点求和进行蒙特卡洛（MC）评估：代价高昂且存在统计噪声，限制了参数采样密度与动量分辨谱的分辨率。

Method: 以图zeta函数重新表述图嵌入求和，并按树宽tw分块：tw≤2的低树宽块给出基于Epstein zeta函数的闭式表达，tw>2的高树宽块用张量网络桶消元（bucket elimination）求值；单次计算即得完整动量依赖级数，并提供开源实现，适用于一般相互作用与大范围参数扫描。

Result: 在一维、二维、三维幂律相互作用的横场Ising模型上基准测试，复现了此前MC结果，计算代价仅为其中一小部分，同时支持更密的参数采样；应用于准二维三角晶格横场Ising反铁磁体KTmSe₂时，发现包含偶极相互作用的模型最能描述现有实验数据。

Conclusion: 图zeta方法消除了高阶关联簇展开的核心计算瓶颈，使其成为快速、确定性的定量动量分辨建模工具，适用于短程与长程量子物质。

Abstract: Over the past decade, high-order series expansions based on linked-cluster methods have become an important tool for studying low-energy properties of gapped quantum systems with long-range interactions. We introduce a deterministic framework that removes a central computational bottleneck of this method. Our graph zeta method replaces the costly and statistically noisy Monte Carlo (MC) evaluation of high-dimensional lattice sums by a systematic, high-precision computation that delivers series coefficients within minutes on standard desktop hardware. The full momentum-dependent series is obtained in a single calculation, enabling high-resolution excitation spectra throughout the Brillouin zone. Building on the companion paper [1], the method reformulates graph-embedding sums as graph zeta functions and decomposes them into blocks classified by their treewidth tw. Low-treewidth blocks (tw$\leq2$) admit closed expressions based on Epstein zeta functions, while higher-treewidth blocks (tw$>2$) are evaluated using tensor-network bucket elimination. We benchmark the approach for transverse-field Ising models with power-law interactions in 1d, 2d, and 3d, reproducing previous MC results at a fraction of the computational cost while enabling substantially denser parameter sampling. An open-source implementation makes the method directly applicable to general interactions and large parameter scans. As an application, we compare microscopic interaction models for the stacked quasi-2d transverse-field Ising triangular-lattice antiferromagnet KTmSe$_2$ and find that a model including dipolar interactions best describes existing experimental data. The graph zeta method thus turns high-order linked-cluster expansions into a practical and deterministic tool for fast quantitative momentum-resolved modeling of short- and long-range quantum matter.

</details>


### [8] [Spinon-Induced Phonon Dynamics in Chiral and $π$-Flux Quantum Spin Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19050)
*Zachary Hizon,Kristian Hauser Villegas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过研究自旋子-声子耦合对声子动力学的影响，发现声子谱不被重整化，但声衰减和声子热导率能区分不同量子自旋液体相，且手性量子自旋液体不产生声子热霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋液体因强量子涨落而回避长程有序，缺乏常规序参量，导致实验识别困难。本文旨在寻找可实验探测的手段来区分不同的量子自旋液体相。

Method: 计算声子自能，研究自旋子-声子耦合对不同量子自旋液体相声子动力学的影响，分析声衰减和声子热导率等响应函数。

Result: 声子谱不被自旋子相互作用重整化；声衰减和声子热导率表现出对底层量子自旋液体相的独特特征；手性量子自旋液体与声子耦合时，尽管显式破坏时间反演对称性，却不产生声子热霍尔效应。

Conclusion: 声子输运可作为识别和表征量子自旋液体的潜在探针；相关响应函数为区分不同量子自旋液体背景提供实验可及的指纹。

Abstract: Quantum spin liquids (QSLs) are magnetic phases that evade long-range order down to the lowest temperatures due to strong quantum fluctuations. Their lack of conventional order parameters, however, makes experimental identification challenging. In this work, we investigate how distinct QSL phases affect phonon dynamics through spinon-phonon coupling. By computing the phonon self-energy, we show that the phonon spectrum remains unrenormalized by spinon interactions, while sound attenuation and phonon thermal conductivity exhibit distinct signatures of the underlying QSL phase. These response functions therefore provide experimentally accessible fingerprints for distinguishing different QSL backgrounds. Remarkably, we find that a chiral QSL coupled to phonons does not generate a phonon thermal Hall effect despite explicitly breaking time-reversal symmetry. Our results establish phonon transport as a potential probe for identifying and characterizing quantum spin liquids.

</details>

### [9] [Self-Supervised Learning for Robust Resonance Mass Regression in Cascade Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17726)
*Ho Fung Tsoi,Alex Yang,Luis Felipe Gutierrez Zagazeta,Shion Chen,Dylan Rankin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 采用自监督预训练（VICReg + Transformer编码器）学习对多种扰动不变的嵌入，再微调用于缺失能量下重共振质量回归，在2.5–6.5 TeV、类SUSY十一体级联衰变场景中，相比同架构监督模型，预训练模型重建的共振峰更锐、在真实扰动下性能更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对撞机新物理搜索中，从含缺失能量的衰变产物重建重共振质量直接决定灵敏度。监督学习方法面对系统性不确定性与分布漂移时泛化能力差：穷举标注数据的所有变化计算代价高，而泛化失败会破坏峰搜寻所依赖的共振宽度重建。

Method: 遵循基础模型范式：先用VICReg以自监督方式预训练Transformer编码器，学习对多种corruption不变的embedding；再针对重共振质量回归进行微调。任务设定为质量范围2.5–6.5 TeV、具有类SUSY级联衰变为十一体末态的重共振。

Result: 与在相同增强数据上从头训练的、架构相同的监督模型相比，预训练模型重建的共振峰更尖锐，并在各种真实corruption下表现更稳定。

Conclusion: 自监督基础模型式预训练可提升缺失能量下重共振质量重建的鲁棒性与泛化性，为峰搜寻分析提供更可靠的质量/宽度重建，优于直接监督训练。

Abstract: Reconstructing the mass of a heavy resonance from its decay products with missing energy is one of the central tasks that directly determine the sensitivity in new physics searches at collider experiments. Supervised learning approaches to this problem often struggle to generalize well due to the presence of various systematic uncertainties and distribution shifts. Exhausting all possible variations in the labeled data can be very compute-intensive, while a failure of the model to generalize can corrupt the reconstructed resonance widths that are critical in peak-hunting analyses. In this work, following the foundation model paradigm, we use a self-supervised approach to pre-train a transformer encoder with VICReg to learn an embedding invariant to various corruptions, then fine-tune it for mass regression on a heavy resonance with masses ranging from 2.5 to 6.5 TeV and a SUSY-like cascade decay into an eleven-body final state. We show that the pre-trained model reconstructs sharper resonance peaks and has a more stable performance under various realistic corruptions, compared to a supervised model of the same architecture trained on the same augmented data from scratch.

</details>


### [10] [Similarity Pairing with Energy Mover's Distance for Self-Supervised Pre-Training at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17738)
*Ho Fung Tsoi,Dylan Rankin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 提出一种基于能量搬运距离（EMD）的数据驱动配对方法，用相似事件的配对替代手工数据增强，实现无需增强的自蒸馏预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC基础模型的自监督训练依赖数据增强来构建对物理或探测器对称性不变的表示空间，但增强集的选择自由度大且直接影响下游性能；现有实现要么修改事件从而破坏事件保真度，要么模拟更多事件变体而计算开销巨大。

Method: 利用能量搬运距离（EMD）度量两个事件之间的相似性（即把一个事件变换为另一个所需做的"功"），据此对事件进行采样与匹配，将相似但不同的事件作为学习不变性的正样本视图，从而在不引入手工扰动的前提下保持每个事件的物理内容完整。

Result: 在QCD喷注上通过自蒸馏进行预训练，验证该免增强配对方法能够产生具有语义性的喷注嵌入，其下游判别能力与基于增强的基线相当或更优。

Conclusion: 以相似性配对替代手工数据增强是一种可行且有效的自监督预训练策略，既避免了事件保真度受损，又降低了模拟开销。

Abstract: Many self-supervised methods for training foundation models at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) rely on data augmentations to encourage the model to embed events into a representation space invariant to certain physical or detector symmetries. A common challenge arises from the large freedom in choosing a proper set of augmentations on which downstream performance depends. The implementation of augmentations involves either modifying existing events, potentially breaking the event fidelity, or simulating more event variants, which is computationally intensive. In this work, we present a data-driven method of pairing events by their similarity via the energy mover's distance (EMD), which measures how similar two events are in terms of the work required to transform one into the other. With this approach, distinct events are sampled and matched by their similarity to serve as views for learning invariance, keeping the physics content of each event intact without handcrafted distortions. We demonstrate this augmentation-free pairing method by pre-training on QCD jets via self-distillation and show that it can yield semantic jet embeddings with downstream discrimination power comparable to or better than an augmentation-based baseline.

</details>


### [11] [Search for Visible Dark Photon Decays at the Future SHINE Facility with the DarkSHINE Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18534)
*Zejia Lu,Huayang Wang,Xiang Chen,Jiahui Wu,Yulei Zhang,Liang Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究DarkSHINE实验对位移暗光子衰变A'→e⁺e⁻的灵敏度，质量区间2m_e<m_A'<2m_μ，采用8 GeV电子束打钨靶。通过Geant4模拟、基于图神经网络的TrackNet算法及位移顶点筛选压制本底，预期3年（9×10¹⁴ EOT）本底为0.3事例，90%置信度下可排除暗光子质量约30–70 MeV、动能混合参数约5×10⁻⁵–2×10⁻⁴的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对暗光子可见衰变通道缺乏专门搜索策略的问题，本文旨在建立DarkSHINE实验中位移暗光子衰变的搜索方案，以补充不可见暗光子衰变的搜寻。

Method: 采用8 GeV电子束入射钨靶；暗光子信号产生用CalcHEP在树图层面计算；事例经精细Geant4探测器模拟传播；利用专用近端径迹探测器、基于图神经网络的TrackNet算法及位移顶点选择来压制对产生和光子转换引起的瞬发本底；并对残留本底顶点分布进行外推。

Result: 外推残留本底顶点分布得到3年运行（对应9×10¹⁴ EOT）的预期本底为0.3事例；在该曝光量下，90%置信度的中位预期排除区域覆盖暗光子质量约30–70 MeV、动能混合参数约5×10⁻⁵–2×10⁻⁴，且耦合边界随质量变化。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了DarkSHINE实验中可见暗光子衰变的搜索策略，与不可见暗光子衰变搜索形成互补。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of the proposed DarkSHINE experiment to displaced dark photon decays, $A'\to e^+e^-$, using an 8 GeV electron beam incident on a tungsten target. The study focuses on masses $2m_e<m_{A'}<2m_μ$ and assumes a unit branching fraction to electron and positron pairs. Signal production is calculated at tree level with CalcHEP, and the generated events are propagated through a detailed Geant4 detector simulation. A dedicated close tracker, the TrackNet algorithm based on a graph neural network, and displaced vertex selections suppress prompt backgrounds from pair production and photon conversion. Extrapolation of the residual background vertex distribution yields an expected background of 0.3 events for three years of operation, corresponding to $9\times10^{14}$ electrons on target. For this exposure, the median expected exclusion region at 90% confidence level spans dark photon masses of approximately 30--70 MeV and kinetic mixing parameters of approximately $5\times10^{-5}$--$2\times10^{-4}$, with mass-dependent coupling boundaries. This study establishes a visible decay search strategy at DarkSHINE that complements searches for invisible dark photon decays.

</details>


### [12] [After 60 years, here comes the Sun](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18799)
*Anatael Cabrera,Mark Chen,Stefano Dusini,Jeff Hartnell,Jorge Martín Camalich,Diana Navas Nicolás,Hiroshi Nunokawa,Aldo Serenelli,Javier Trujillo Bueno,Frédéric Yermia*

TL;DR: 太阳中微子正从"建立振荡"转向"探测太阳本身"：反应堆反中微子已把太阳中微子味转换相关的传播不确定性压缩到可忽略，第一个"深渊"被填平；但主流pp通量(>90%)的探测精度仍比标准太阳模型预言低一个数量级，且本征¹⁴C本底封锁pp区，需全新探测技术才能跨越，进而实现多信使"太阳层析成像"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六十年来太阳中微子主要用于建立中微子振荡，而非反哺对太阳自身的认识。随着反应堆反中微子精确测定控制太阳中微子味转换的振荡参数，传播相关不确定性趋于可忽略，使太阳中微子有望真正成为探测太阳核反应源项的工具，故作者提出将太阳中微子测量转向"解读太阳"的动机。

Method: 本文为观点/综述性论述，非实验论文：通过逻辑论证与现状对比展开分析，一是利用反应堆反中微子实验对振荡参数的精确约束评估太阳中微子传播端不确定性；二是对比当前太阳中微子探测精度与标准太阳模型对pp通量的预测精度，并剖析¹⁴C本底对pp区的封锁；三是结合日震学对太阳力学结构的独立测量，提出多信使"heliotomography"的论证框架。

Result: 反应堆反中微子已将太阳中微子味转换的振荡参数约束得非常精确，传播相关不确定性接近可忽略，第一个"深渊"基本闭合；但当前太阳中微子探测对主导的pp通量(>90%)的精度比标准太阳模型的预测低约一个数量级，且此差距任何反应堆实验都无法弥合；本征¹⁴C本底封锁了pp能区，只有新探测技术才能突破。

Conclusion: 跨越第二个"深渊"需要革命性而非渐进式的技术突破。若将精密中微子测得的核源项与日震学对太阳力学结构的测量相结合，可实现由"两侧同时解读"太阳内部的多信使太阳层析成像(heliotomography)，开启太阳研究新时代，前提是该领域必须建成与任务相匹配的探测装置。

Abstract: For sixty years, the Sun served neutrino physics more than the reverse: its neutrinos established oscillations rather than probing the star itself. That is now changing. Reactor antineutrinos have pinned down the oscillation parameters governing solar-neutrino flavour conversion so precisely that the propagation-related uncertainty is becoming negligible -- closing the first of two abysses. Solar neutrinos can, at last, be turned on the Sun. Yet a second abyss remains: today's solar-neutrino detection falls an order of magnitude short of the precision the Standard Solar Model already predicts for the dominant pp flux ($>\,$90$\%$) -- a gap no reactor experiment can close. The intrinsic $^{14}\mathrm{C}$ background walls off the pp region; only new detection techniques can breach it. Bridging this second abyss demands a revolution, not an increment. Combined with helioseismology's complementary reading of the Sun's mechanical structure, precision neutrino measurements of the nuclear source terms would enable a multi-messenger heliotomography of the solar interior -- reading our star from both sides at once. A new age of solar discoveries may follow, on one condition: that the field builds the instruments equal to the task.

</details>

### [13] [On the computational cost of Stochastic Gradient Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17750)
*Mateusz B. Majka,Tigran Nagapetyan,Łukasz Szpruch,Yue Wu,Danqi Zhuang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文比较了SGLD与Euler-Maruyama方法在有限和漂移随机微分方程中的计算代价，导出了显式依赖数据集规模m、小批量规模s与精度ε的复杂度估计，揭示了两种方法各自占优的参数区域，并用高斯贝叶斯推断实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGLD通过小批量近似替代全数据集漂移评估以降低Langevin采样的计算成本，但由此引入的子采样误差可能抵消这一计算节省。因此需要量化分析子采样误差与计算节省之间的权衡，明确SGLD相对于精确方法的适用边界。

Method: 针对具有有限和漂移的随机微分方程，在给定均方精度ε²的条件下，推导SGLD与Euler-Maruyama(EM)方法的复杂度估计，显式刻画其对数据集规模m、小批量规模s和精度参数ε的依赖关系，并据此进行两者计算代价的对比分析；同时以高斯贝叶斯推断模型开展数值实验，检验所预测的代价区域以及底层离散化误差与方差估计。

Result: 复杂度对比揭示了两种方法各自占优的清晰参数区域：EM仅在“小数据、激进子采样”区域才具有更低的主导阶代价；在其余大部分参数空间中SGLD更优。在实用相关区域s ≪ m中，两种方法之间的转换发生在m ≍ ε⁻¹这一尺度上。数值实验基于高斯贝叶斯推断模型，验证了所预测的代价区域及相关离散化误差和方差估计。

Conclusion: SGLD相对EM的计算优势并非在所有情形下成立，而是取决于数据规模、批量规模与目标精度之间的具体关系；总体而言SGLD在绝大多数实用参数区域中更经济，EM的优势仅限于小数据且强子采样的特定情形，转换尺度为m ≍ ε⁻¹。

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Langevin Dynamics (SGLD) reduces the cost of Langevin-based sampling by replacing full-dataset drift evaluations with mini-batch approximations, but the resulting subsampling error may offset this computational saving. We study this trade-off for stochastic differential equations with finite-sum drifts and compare the computational cost of SGLD with that of the Euler-Maruyama (EM) method. For a prescribed mean-square accuracy $\varepsilon^2$, we derive complexity estimates that explicitly track the dependence on the dataset size $m$, mini-batch size $s$, and accuracy parameter $\varepsilon$. The resulting comparison reveals distinct parameter regimes in which either method is preferable. In particular, EM can have lower leading-order cost only in a small-data, aggressive-subsampling regime, whereas SGLD is favoured over most of the remaining parameter space. In the practically relevant regime $s \ll m$, the transition between the two methods occurs at the scale $m \asymp \varepsilon^{-1}$. We complement the theoretical analysis with numerical experiments based on a Gaussian Bayesian inference model, which examine the predicted cost regimes together with the underlying discretisation error and variance estimates.

</details>


### [14] [Spatio-temporal Latent Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models for Reduced-order Modeling of Parametrized Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17768)
*Michiel Nikken,Nicolò Botteghi,Federico Califano,Silke Glas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的非侵入式模型降阶方法，在POD与自编码器构成的潜空间中生成参数化动力系统的时空解，并在二维绕流问题上验证了其精度、泛化与时间外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体力学、气候建模等科学问题常产生高维全阶模型（FOM），计算代价高昂，难以满足实时与多查询需求。模型降阶（MOR）可构建降阶模型（ROM），以小幅精度损失换取大幅计算成本缩减，但如何在非侵入式框架下保持解的质量与时间连贯性仍是挑战。

Method: 提出非侵入式MOR框架：采用生成式机器学习中的DDPM生成不同参数实例下动力系统的解。区别于仅在空间域操作的常规DDPM，该方法直接生成时空解以提升质量与时间连贯性；并将DDPM嵌入潜空间——该潜空间由本征正交分解（POD）与自编码器依次作用得到，从而降低数据维度与DDPM的计算开销。

Result: 在参数化二维绕障流问题上开展数值实验，结果表明潜空间DDPM能够：(i) 生成准确且时间连贯的解；(ii) 对未见参数取值具有较强泛化能力；(iii) 在时间上外推超出训练时间范围。

Conclusion: 潜空间DDPM为非侵入式MOR提供了一条有效途径，可在保证时空解精度与连贯性的同时显著降低计算成本，并具备参数泛化与时间外推潜力，适用于实时与多查询场景。

Abstract: Many scientific problems require accurate modeling of complex physical phenomena, such as fluid dynamics or climate modeling. These phenomena often result in high-dimensional and thus computationally expensive computational models that can limit their application to real-time and multi-query problems. Model-order reduction (MOR) is an approach that seeks to approximate full-order models (FOMs) using reduced-order models (ROMs), trading a minor reduction in accuracy for a major reduction in computational cost. In this work, we propose a non-intrusive MOR method using generative machine learning by means of denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPMs) for generating solutions of the dynamical systems under different instances of their parameters. Unlike conventional DDPMs, which often operate purely in the spatial domain, we aim to generate spatio-temporal solutions to improve quality and temporal coherence. In addition, we embed the DDPM in a latent space obtained by sequentially applying proper orthogonal decomposition and an autoencoder to reduce the data dimensionality and the computational cost of the DDPM. We test our approach on a parametrized 2D fluid flow around an obstacle. The numerical experiments demonstrate that our latent DDPM can (i) produce accurate and temporally coherent solutions, (ii) achieve strong generalization capabilities to scenarios involving unseen parameter values, and (iii) extrapolate in time beyond the training horizon.

</details>


### [15] [A variational mollification approach to circular deconvolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17849)
*Mirza Karamehmedović,Pierre Maréchal,Mykhailo Petrov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"磨光目标重建"的变分磨光方法求解圆周反卷积问题，证明其一致性与确定性扰动下的收敛速率（普通光滑核达代数阶最优率，超光滑核达对数阶最优率），并用合成数据与风向数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆周反卷积问题本身是不适定的，直接求解精确解在噪声存在时不稳定；因此需要一种既能稳定求解、又能保持最优收敛性质的替代重建思路。

Method: 不重建精确解，而是重建磨光后的目标对象；将问题构造为凸变分问题，其解具有显式Fourier表示，并借助该表示分析一致性及确定性数据扰动下的收敛速率。

Result: 方法随目标分辨率增大保持一致；对普通光滑核，在Besov-Nikolskii光滑性假设下达到经典的阶最优代数收敛率；对超光滑核，达到相应的阶最优对数收敛率。合成数据与风向数据的数值实验证实了理论预测。

Conclusion: 变分磨光为圆周反卷积提供了一种兼具凸性、显式Fourier解与最优收敛速率的方法，理论与数值结果一致，适用于实际圆周数据场景。

Abstract: We propose a variational mollification approach to circular deconvolution based on the reconstruction of a mollified target object rather than the exact solution itself. The method is formulated as a convex variational problem whose solution admits an explicit Fourier representation. We establish the consistency of the proposed reconstruction as the target resolution increases and derive convergence rates for deterministic data perturbations. For ordinary smooth kernels, the method achieves the classical order-optimal algebraic convergence rates under Besov-Nikolskii smoothness assumptions, while for supersmooth kernels it attains the corresponding order-optimal logarithmic rates. Numerical experiments on synthetic and wind-direction data illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach and confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [16] [A Geometric View of Adaptive Cross Approximation via Exterior Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17947)
*Trevor Loe,Longxiu Huang,Deanna Needell*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 通过外代数重构CUR残差的行列式公式，将残差分解为权重幅值、角度几何与枢轴条件数三种效应，恢复σ_{k+1}型估计，解释贪心ACA失败并提出几何感知加权质量选主元规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应交叉近似（ACA）在逐项查询廉价、但构造或反复乘全矩阵昂贵的场景下很有吸引力。但其实际性能无法由现有最坏情况界解释：这些界可能指数级大，且往往无法预测贪心最大元素选主元何时表现不佳。

Method: 将矩阵视为交叉格拉姆矩阵（cross-gram matrix），用外代数视角重新诠释CUR残差的经典行列式公式。在该表示下，每个残差项是两块(k+1)-blades之间的加权内积除以枢轴块归一化体积。该公式分离出三种效应：奇异向量行的加权幅值、其角度几何，以及所选枢轴的条​​件数。据此恢复经典σ_{k+1}型估计，刻画单个枢轴何时湮灭或近乎湮灭额外行列，得到半正定矩阵的准最优性估计，并用显式角度公式界定秩一更新。

Result: 用该框架解释了贪心ACA在非对称螺旋核示例上的失败，并据此启发为径向基伽辽金刚度矩阵设计几何感知的加权质量（weighted-mass）选主元规则。实验报告显示，加权质量管理选主元相比贪心选主元和随机枢轴Cholesky，常在Frobenius残差上更小，同时在并行硬件上保持相当运行时间。

Abstract: Latent neural operators improve the efficiency of operator learning for partial differential equations (PDEs) by performing the main computation on compact latent representations. Abstract: We propose a two-dimensional benchmark for fluid-structure-contact interaction consisting of a deformable elastic disk falling under gravity within a viscous incompressible fluid and rebounding in the vicinity of the bottom wall. The configuration is particularly challenging due to the well-known no-contact paradox, which can lead to a contactless rebound and forces numerical methods to resolve a vanishingly thin fluid layer in the near-contact region, making the dynamics highly sensitive to the spatial and temporal discretizations. In addition to no-slip boundary and interface conditions, a reduced porous modeling of surface roughness, either on the disk boundary or on the bottom wall, is also considered; this circumvents the no-contact paradox and enables genuine contact. An energy balance law is derived theoretically for all three cases. Eight numerical methodologies, developed by five research groups and spanning different model formulations, numerical methods, and codes (including both fitted and unfitted discretizations), are applied to the benchmark at several levels of spatial and temporal refinement. Quantities of interest of varying complexity are collected and compared, showing close agreement during the falling phase and increased sensitivity in the near-contact and rebound regimes. The setting and the results provide a suitable reference for the systematic assessment of fluid-structure-contact interaction solvers. The time histories of all quantities of interest for every approach and refinement level are provided as supplementary material.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文指出能量稳定性不足以保证空间非均匀 Allen–Cahn 方程的局部动力学保真度，并提出基于完整残差符号的容许类与点态单调性判据，据此对多种能量稳定格式进行分类。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 ODE 层面的研究表明，能量耗散本身并不能保证空间均匀相场模型的动力学保真度。然而，这些 ODE 论证无法直接推广到空间非均匀的 Allen–Cahn 解：扩散项进入完整残差，即使 0<u≤1 时其逐点符号也可能改变。因此需要针对这一差异建立新的分析框架。

Method: 作者引入由完整 Allen–Cahn 残差决定符号的容许类，证明精确 PDE 流保持相应的逐点单调增长或单调衰减方向。全隐式 Euler 方法在其标准唯一可解区域内继承该结构。随后考察若干常用能量稳定格式：一阶凸分裂与足够强的一阶稳定化；二阶凸分裂、稳定化 CN/AB、IEQ 和 SAV 格式，并对后者构造反例，且验证反例在充分小的光滑空间非均匀容许扰动下依然成立。

Result: 精确 PDE 流与全隐式 Euler 保持点态单调方向。一阶凸分裂和足够强的一阶稳定化能保持正确方向，但发生在缩减的有效时间尺度上，导致人为延迟或阻尼。相比之下，二阶凸分裂、稳定化 CN/AB、IEQ 和 SAV 格式存在单调数据，使得大时间步长在耗散原始或修正能量的同时产生符号错误的逐点增量；四类格式的严格反转在充分小的光滑空间非均匀容许扰动下持续存在，反例在扩散真实活跃时依然有效。数值实验验证了该分类。

Abstract: Energy stability is fundamental in the numerical approximation of phase-field models, but it does not by itself guarantee faithful reproduction of local dynamics. Recent ODE-level studies have shown that energy dissipation alone does not ensure dynamical fidelity for spatially homogeneous phase-field models (Xu and Xu, 2023; Li and Wang, 2026). However, these ODE arguments do not extend directly to spatially inhomogeneous Allen--Cahn solutions: diffusion enters the full residual $\mathcal R_\varepsilon(u):=Δu+\varepsilon^{-2}(u-u^3)$ and may change its pointwise sign even when $0<u\leq1$.
  Motivated by this distinction, we introduce sign-dependent admissible classes determined by the full Allen--Cahn residual and prove that the exact PDE flow preserves the corresponding pointwise monotone-growth or monotone-decay direction. The fully implicit Euler method inherits this structure in its standard unique-solvability regime. We then examine several widely used energy-stable schemes. First-order convex splitting and sufficiently strong first-order stabilization can preserve the correct direction, but on a reduced effective time scale, causing artificial delay or damping. By contrast, second-order convex splitting, stabilized CN/AB, IEQ, and SAV schemes admit monotone data for which large time steps generate wrong-signed pointwise increments despite dissipation of the original or a modified energy. For all four schemes, these strict reversals persist under sufficiently small smooth spatially nonhomogeneous admissible perturbations, so the counterexamples remain valid with genuinely active diffusion. Numerical experiments confirm this classification. Thus, pointwise monotonicity is a local dynamical criterion complementary to energy stability and maximum-bound preservation.

</details>

Strong RICS drag impedes the flow of electrons and positrons, particularly along extended field lines. The accelerated plasma flow from the gap becomes loaded with copious electron-positron pairs in extended RICS zones, and also carries ions extracted from the star and accelerated in the gap. When radiative drag stops the pair-loaded flow at the magnetic equator, the ion component transfers momentum through streaming instabilities, delivering a small lepton population into the opposite hemisphere and sustaining the circuit. Our results suggest that the detection or non-detection of GWs from nearby GRBs and Type Ic broad-lined SNe could help constrain the mechanisms operating in the central engine.

The outcome of simulations starting from $β_0=0.1$ (superthermal magnetic pressures) depends upon the numerical resolution as quantified by the number of grid cells per thermal scale height $H_\mathrm{th}$. While such states might be sustained in global models with even lower initial $β_0$ and/or continuous $B_y$ injection, our results indicate that they could arise purely from under-resolving the midplane MRI dynamo that would otherwise be able to generate and emanate randomized fields. Using the CR distribution functions and CSM density profiles from Inoue et al. (2021), we calculate time-dependent gamma-ray fluxes from Type II-P SNe, including both attenuation processes. Abstract: Multi-messenger astronomy (MMA), the joint study of cosmic sources through gravitational waves (GWs), electromagnetic (EM) radiation, neutrinos, and cosmic rays, is rapidly reshaping time-domain astrophysics. GPT-5.5 attains the highest event-level $F_1$ score, the harmonic mean of precision and recall, at $0.893 \pm 0.010$, and the highest GCN-level recall, $0.794 \pm 0.013$, improving on previous GPT-4.1 results by 16\% and 10\%, respectively, while Claude-Opus-4.7 achieves the highest precision, $0.978 \pm 0.014$. Second, we introduce concurrent likelihood evaluation, which speeds up the MCMC computation by a factor of $40\times$ over our previous results.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大尺度磁场主导吸积盘内区与喷流动力学，而目前公认的主要生成机制是磁旋转不稳定性（MRI）。作者希望探索是否存在其他独立且高效的磁场生成通道。

Method: 理论分析：考虑明亮致密冕产生的辐射场，论证其非保守性会产生辐射驱动的极向磁场，随后由较差转动放大，导致磁场的二次增长。

Result: 在广泛的冕光度、尺度及MRI放大位置参数范围内，该机制可在与MRI相当或更短时间内实现磁化，且从零磁场初值出发即可生效。

Conclusion: 该机制不仅是MRI的替代方案，在相当大参数空间内还是主导通道；它源于辐射场的非保守本质，是强各向异性辐射源下黑洞吸积流的必然结果，并可推广至活动星系核、伽马射线暴和潮汐撕裂事件等发光天体系统。

Abstract: Large scale magnetic fields play a crucial role in shaping the dynamics and structure of the inner parts of accretion disks and the resulting jets from the vicinity of black holes and neutron stars. Currently, the primary mechanism considered for generating and amplifying large scale magnetic fields in accretion disks is the magneto-rotational instability (MRI). Here we show that non-conservative radiation fields provide a rapid and independent mechanism for generating and amplifying magnetic fields in accretion flows. A luminous, compact corona generates a radiation-driven poloidal field that is subsequently amplified by differential rotation, yielding quadratic magnetic-field growth and, over a broad range of coronal luminosities and sizes and MRI amplification locations, magnetization on timescales comparable to or shorter than those of the MRI. The mechanism therefore provides not merely an alternative to MRI, but an additional and, over a substantial region of parameter space, dominant channel for magnetic-field generation from initial conditions with zero magnetic field. Since the radiation-driven source arises directly from the non-conservative nature of the radiation field, such magnetic-field generation is an inevitable consequence of sufficiently strong and anisotropic radiation sources in black-hole accretion flows. More generally, the mechanism requires only a non-conservative radiation field and differential plasma motion, and is therefore expected to operate in a broad range of luminous astrophysical systems, including active galactic nuclei, gamma-ray bursts, and tidal disruption events.

</details>

### [46] [Human-Centric Grasp State Assessment: Toward Transferring Subjective Evaluation to Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17540)
*Ryohei Kobayashi,Kosei Isomoto,Yuga Yano,Yuichiro Tanaka,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合VLM半自动监督生成器与轻量抓取状态预测器的框架，将人类隐性主观标准迁移至机器人，实现可变形物体抓取力的在线自适应调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体抓取中，定性的人类期望与定量的机器人测量之间存在语义鸿沟；传统深度学习方法需要大量人工标注数据，且每遇新物体都需重新标注，成本过高。

Method: 框架由两部分组成：（1）基于VLM的半自动监督信号生成器，以极少量人工标注试验作为上下文锚点，将主观标准传播至未标注数据；（2）轻量级抓取状态预测器，依据时序触觉与抓取力测量，顺序估计抓取状态，支持在线快速自适应。

Result: 在三种代表性可变形物体上进行实验，并开展25名参与者的人类评估研究，验证了该框架在给定任务设定下按人类感知的抓取适宜性调节抓取力的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够以极少的标注代价弥合定性与定量之间的语义鸿沟，实现符合人类主观标准的可变形物体抓取力调节，具备可行性。

Abstract: We propose a framework that transfers tacit human subjective criteria to robotic systems for the appropriate grasping of deformable objects. Achieving such behavior is challenging because a semantic gap exists between qualitative human expectations and quantitative robotic measurements. Conventional deep learning approaches for bridging this gap also require prohibitive amounts of manually annotated data for each newly encountered object. To address these challenges, our framework integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based semi-automated supervisor generator with a lightweight grasp state predictor, using a minimal set of human-annotated trials as contextual anchors to propagate subjective criteria to unannotated data. The prediction model then enables rapid online adaptation by sequentially estimating the grasp state from time-series tactile and grasping force measurements. Through experiments on three representative deformable objects and a human evaluation study with 25 participants, we demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed framework for adjusting grasping force according to human-perceived grasp appropriateness in the evaluated task setting.

</details>


### [47] [Real-Time Service Robot Replanning via Simple Button Interaction for Improved Task Success and User Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17541)
*Ryo Terashima,Yuga Yano,Koshun Arimura,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种实时反馈功能，让机器人通过平板单键接收人类反馈并重新规划动作，以应对任务中的失败与异常。实验表明该功能可同时提升任务成功率与用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在真实环境中需应对意外指令，但机器人无法在任务中检测所有失败与异常，因此需借助人类反馈来弥补其感知与决策的不足。

Method: 设计实时反馈功能：用户在机器人动作不正确时，通过平板上的单个按钮直观地发送反馈；机器人利用该反馈判断自身失败并重新规划合适动作以完成任务。随后开展有/无反馈功能的对比实验，并用五点李克特量表问卷评估，以检验"简单交互不会带来负面用户体验"这一假设。

Result: 加入反馈功能后，"您是否觉得机器人的行为出乎意料？"得分提升0.5分，"您是否有时对机器人的行为感到焦虑？"得分提升0.9分。

Conclusion: 结果支持研究假设，表明引入该实时反馈功能可同时提升任务成功率与用户体验。

Abstract: Service robots must respond to unexpected instructions in real-world environments. However, robots cannot detect all failures and exceptions during a task. To address these issues, we propose a real-time feedback function that enables robots to modify their behavior based on human feedback. In this system, users can intuitively send feedback to the robot by pressing a single button on a tablet when the robot fails to act correctly. Robots use this feedback to consider their failures and replan appropriate actions to complete the task. We conducted experiments with and without the feedback function to verify the following hypothesis: "Simple interactions do not cause a negative user experience." All questionnaire responses are evaluated on a five-point Likert scale. After adding the feedback function, the response score for the question "Did you feel that the robot's behavior was unexpected?" improved by 0.5 points, and that for "Did you feel anxious about the robot's behavior at times?" improved by 0.9 points. These results support the study's hypothesis and indicate that incorporating this real-time feedback function can simultaneously improve task success and the user experience.

</details>


### [48] [Predictive Varanus: Combining CSP Conformance Monitoring with Predictive LTL Runtime Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17625)
*Angelo Ferrando,Matt Luckcuck,Pedro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PREDICTIVE VARANUS 两阶段预测性运行时验证流水线，将基于 CSP 的运行时验证器 VARANUS 与 LTL 预测性监控结合，以提前给出更早的验证结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运行时验证是事后反应式的：只有在系统已执行了坏事件之后监控器才能检测到违规，在失败代价高或安全性关键的领域往往为时已晚。因此需要一种能够提前预测未来行为、尽早给出裁决的验证方法。

Method: 采用两阶段验证流水线：第一阶段用 CSP 模型作为对观测事件轨迹的一致性门控，若行为超出模型则立即拒绝；第二阶段将同一 CSP 模型翻译为 Buchi 自动机，用于约束预测性 LTL 监控器所探索的未来路径，从而将模型一致的前缀分类为已保证满足、已强制违反或仍不确定。形式化了组合监控器，并说明其实现。

Result: 在核仓储巡检机器人 rover 案例上验证了该方法，表明 CSP 验证与预测性 LTL 的结合相比标准运行时监控能更早给出裁决，并复用了已有的设计期 CSP 模型。

Conclusion: 将 CSP 一致性检查与预测性 LTL 监控相结合，可在保持模型一致性的同时实现更早的预测性裁决，且能复用既有的设计期 CSP 模型，适用于对失败敏感的自主与机器人系统。

Abstract: Runtime Verification is well suited to autonomous and robotic systems because it checks the behaviour that is actually observed during execution. Its main limitation, however, is that it is usually reactive: the monitor detects a violation only after the system has already performed a bad event. This can be too late in domains where failures are costly or unsafe. In this paper we present PREDICTIVE VARANUS, a two-stage verification pipeline that combines VARANUS, a runtime verifier that uses models written in the process algebra Communicating Sequential Processes (CSP), with predictive runtime verification for LTL. A CSP model is first used as a conformance gate over the observed event trace; the same model is then translated into a Buchi automaton that constrains the futures explored by a predictive LTL monitor. In this way, out-of-model behaviour is rejected immediately, while model-consistent prefixes can be classified as already guaranteeing satisfaction, already forcing violation, or still being inconclusive for the monitored temporal property. We formalise the combined monitor, explain its implementation, and illustrate the approach on a robotic rover for nuclear-store inspection. The case study shows how the combination of CSP validation and predictive LTL can provide earlier verdicts than standard runtime monitoring while reusing an existing design-time CSP model.

</details>


### [49] [Flexible-body Modeling, Kinematic Identification, and Assembly Accuracy of Overconstrained Spatial Linkages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17627)
*Daniel Huczala,Michael Pieber,Johannes Gerstmayr,Andreas Mair,Frederik Schulte,Silvia Glas,Tomas Postulka,Ales Vysocky,Martin Pfurner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于浮动参考系公式的柔性多体建模框架，对过约束单环连杆机构进行精度分析与实验验证，揭示其自装配倾向，并提供适用于病态雅可比机构的运动学参数辨识方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过约束有理单环连杆机构高效、紧凑、低成本，适合定制化制造，但其工业应用受限。主要障碍在于：刚体建模因冗余约束在仿真中失效，且该类机构对制造误差敏感，理论上轴不对齐时无法装配。因此需要能够处理结构柔性的建模与验证方法。

Method: 采用基于浮动参考系公式（FFRF）的柔性多体建模框架，对装配后的连杆样机进行整体精度分析；以3D打印PLA的Bennett四杆机构及其故意引入关节轴线失配的变体为对象进行实验验证；提出针对该类病态雅可比机构的运动学参数辨识方法；并用纸管与竹签等低成本非传统材料搭建机构进行定性演示。

Result: 仿真与实验结果吻合，表明过约束机构具有自装配倾向：结构柔顺性驱动装配趋向理想几何构型，并将约束应力分布到整个结构中；即使按刚体理论无法装配的失配变体，物理样机仍可装配；低成本、制造精度有限的纸管与竹签也能实现功能机构。

Conclusion: 柔顺性使过约束连杆机构具备自装配能力，可放宽制造精度要求并支持低成本材料制造；所提建模流程完全算法化，为未来设计优化奠定基础。

Abstract: Overconstrained rational single-loop linkages are efficient, compact, and low-cost custom mechanisms, yet their deployment in industrial settings is limited. In simulations, rigid body formulations fail due to redundant constraints. This study presents a flexible multibody modeling framework based on the floating frame of reference formulation, and delivers an overall accuracy analysis of assembled linkages prototypes. The approach is validated against 3D-printed PLA prototypes of a Bennett four-bar mechanism, including variants with intentional joint-axis misalignment, which theoretically, from the rigid body point of view, cannot be assembled. A supplementary contribution is delivered in the form of a kinematic parameter identification methodology suited for this type of mechanism with ill-conditioned Jacobian. The experimental and simulation results are compared and reveal that these overconstrained mechanisms exhibit a self-assembling tendency -- structural compliance drives the assembly toward the ideal geometric configuration, distributing constraint stress throughout the structure. Additional qualitative demonstrations using cardboard tubes and bamboo sticks as link building blocks confirm that functional mechanisms can be realized from low-cost, unconventional materials with limited manufacturing accuracy. The proposed modeling pipeline is fully algorithmic and enables design optimization in the future.

</details>


### [50] [LEAP: Learning Emergent Active Perception for Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17628)
*Ü. Bora Gökbakan,Stéphane Caron,Philippe Souères*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LEAP方法，在不增加任务目标的情况下，通过地形课程训练让主动感知（注视控制）自然涌现，实现面向危险地形的目标导航，成功率92.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动感知能让智能体自主选择视角以减少环境不确定性，但现有学习系统通常依赖人工设计的代理目标（如覆盖度、好奇心奖励），这些目标可能与实际任务冲突。作者希望在不修改任务目标的前提下，让主动感知行为自发涌现。

Method: 将问题建模为危险地形上、目标需视觉发现的目标导向导航任务；提出带有主动感知的导航策略架构，在渐进式地形课程上训练，仅靠任务压力驱动注视控制涌现；核心是采用注视不变表示（gaze-invariant representation），将深度图像整合进自我中心信念地图。

Result: 在留出评估场景中达到92.7%成功率，显著高于脚本式感知的74.2%和被动感知的34.5%，并接近特权oracle仅差4.6个百分点。此外，未经修改的LEAP导航策略可直接迁移至物理仿真中的四足运动策略转向控制。

Conclusion: 任务压力本身足以诱导主动感知行为涌现，无需额外代理目标；基于注视不变表示的架构有效，且所学导航策略具备良好的可迁移性，可复用于运动控制。

Abstract: Active perception allows autonomous agents to select their viewpoints rather than passively process the viewpoints given to them, enabling them to target where to reduce uncertainty about their environment. Learned systems typically encourage this behavior with hand-designed proxy objectives, such as coverage or curiosity bonuses, that may conflict with the task. In this work, we propose a method to learn emergent active perception (LEAP) without augmentation of the task objective. We formulate the problem of goal-oriented navigation over hazardous terrains with goals that must be discovered visually. We then propose an architecture for navigation policies with active perception, and train them on a terrain curriculum where task pressure alone leads to the emergence of gaze control. Key to this emergence, LEAP works on a gaze-invariant representation that integrates depth images into egocentric belief maps. We validate its performance in held-out evaluation scenarios, where it achieves a 92.7% success rate, compared to 74.2% for scripted or 34.5% for passive perception, and comes within 4.6 points of a privileged oracle. We validate that LEAP navigation policies, unchanged, can be directly applied to steering quadrupedal locomotion policies in physics simulation.

</details>


### [51] [Vision-Language Grounded Task-Context-Aware Imitation Learning for Robotic Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17714)
*Jeon Ho Kang,Igal Tamarkin,Ethan Niu,Ian Novales,Satyandra K. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出结合层级任务选择与任务上下文感知模仿学习的框架，通过语言注入任务上下文，为技能选择提供显式结构并将语言指令与视觉目标关联，实现长时序机器人拆解任务，且无需显式物体标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人拆解需要长时序执行（单一场景内多部件有序操作）。多重有效任务目标与多样装配构型使模仿策略难以仅凭原始观测推断意图技能，尤其在训练数据无法覆盖真实配置与零件几何的组合多样性时。

Method: 方法是引入语言形式的任务上下文，提供技能选择的显式结构，并将语言指定任务与视觉场景中对应操作目标关联。具体框架结合了层级任务选择（hierarchical task selection）与任务上下文感知模仿学习（task-context-aware imitation learning），把语言指令落地到空间视觉表征以支撑拆解，且不需要显式物体标注。

Result: 在多样的连接件几何与装配构型上具备泛化能力；端到端任务成功率较扩散策略（diffusion policy）基线提升35个百分点，较此前任务上下文感知基线提升75个百分点。

Conclusion: 语言提供的任务上下文能缓解长时序拆解中的技能选择歧义与目标关联问题，并使策略在组合多样性的真实配置下具备泛化性。该方法无需显式物体标注即可取得显著性能提升，验证了任务上下文感知模仿学习在机器人拆解中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world robotic disassembly requires long-horizon execution, where robots must perform ordered sequences of manipulation tasks across multiple parts within a single scene. Multiple valid task goals and diverse assembly configurations make it difficult for imitation policies to infer the intended skill from raw observations alone, particularly when training data cannot cover the combinatorial diversity of real-world configurations and part geometries. We show that incorporating task context through language alleviates these challenges by providing explicit structure for skill selection and associating language-specified tasks with their corresponding manipulation targets in the visual scene. The proposed framework combines hierarchical task selection with task-context-aware imitation learning to ground language instructions in spatial visual representations for robotic disassembly. The resulting framework generalizes across diverse connector geometries and assembly configurations without requiring explicit object annotations. Our method improves end-to-end task success by 35 percentage points over the baseline diffusion policy and by 75 percentage points over the previous task-context-aware baseline.

</details>


### [52] [RAF-VLA: Representation Alignment with the Future for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17728)
*Dogun Kim,Yongjae Lee,Joonhee Lim,Yeina Lee,Junhyeok Park,Moogeun Park,Dongsuk Kum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAF-VLA，通过对齐隐状态与未来帧表示来塑造规划相关的内部表征，从而避免世界模型VLA的显式未来生成带来的训练负担与推理延迟，在NAVISM上以极少训练样本和开销取得竞争力规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向自动驾驶的VLA模型通过显式预测未来驾驶场景进行世界建模，虽能提供密集监督、帮助策略学习有利于规划的丰富内部表征，但依赖显式未来生成，带来额外训练负担与推理延迟两大限制。

Method: 提出RAF-VLA框架，采用未来对齐监督微调（Future-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning）：在学习驾驶动作的同时，用简单正则项将策略的隐状态与预训练世界编码器得到的未来帧表示对齐，以此直接引导表征学习，无需生成未来帧。

Result: 在NAVISIM基准上，RAF-VLA以显著更少的训练样本量达到与最先进VLA规划器相当的规划性能；仅增加3.8%训练开销和可忽略的1 ms推理开销。

Conclusion: 表征对齐可替代显式未来生成作为世界建模监督的有效途径，在保持规划性能的同时大幅降低训练与推理成本，为高效VLA自动驾驶提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving have incorporated world modeling by predicting future driving scenes alongside driving actions, demonstrating strong planning performance. Future driving scenes are utilized as dense supervision, encouraging the policy to learn rich internal representations useful for planning. However, these World-Modeling VLAs rely on explicit future generation to learn such representations, thereby introducing two key limitations: additional training burden and inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose RAF-VLA (Representation Alignment with the Future), a VLA-based autonomous driving framework that shapes planning-relevant internal representations through direct guidance from future-frame representations. RAF-VLA employs Future-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning, in which a straightforward regularization aligns the policy's hidden states with future-frame representations obtained from a pretrained world encoder while learning driving actions. This simple alignment allows RAF-VLA to avoid the training burden and inference latency associated with future generation. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark show that RAF-VLA achieves competitive planning performance against state-of-the-art VLA planners with substantially fewer training samples seen. Moreover, RAF-VLA incurs only 3.8% training overhead and a negligible 1 ms inference overhead.

</details>


### [53] [CALOS: Control-Affine Lyapunov On-manifold Safety Layer for Safe Deep Reinforcement Learning for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17758)
*Fabrizio Cesareo,Sebastiano Mengozzi,Nicola Mimmo,Andrea Acquaviva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CALOS：一种无需修改学习算法的运行时安全层，通过求解控制仿射Lyapunov二次规划，对四旋翼姿态施加硬约束，实时性强、可并行，训练中零违例并提升跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽在四旋翼控制中表现优异，但其学习策略在训练与部署阶段均无法保证满足安全约束，存在姿态等安全性风险。

Method: 将四个倾角不等式与Lyapunov下降条件统一构建为单个二次规划，求解对策略名义力矩输出的最小范数修正；在三维力矩空间通过活跃集枚举精确求解，计算开销低，可在数千个并行仿真环境中实时执行。

Result: 在NVIDIA Isaac Lab的轨迹跟踪任务中，相对无约束PPO基线，横向跟踪误差降低55–60%，训练轨迹上姿态约束违例为零；同时加速训练收敛、提升数据效率，且未产生次优策略。

Conclusion: CALOS可在不改变底层学习算法的前提下，为四旋翼提供实时、可并行的姿态安全保障，并通过限制探索至安全状态区域，实现安全性与学习效率、性能的兼顾。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning has demonstrated remarkable capability in quadrotor control, yet learned policies offer no guarantee of respecting safety constraints during training or deployment. We present CALOS (Control-Affine Lyapunov On-manifold Safety), a runtime safety layer that enforces attitude constraints on a quadrotor without modifying the underlying learning algorithm. CALOS formulates four tilt-angle inequalities and a Lyapunov descent condition as a single quadratic program whose solution is the minimum-norm correction to the nominal torque output of the policy. The quadratic program is solved exactly via active-set enumeration over the three-dimensional torque space, with a computational cost low enough to enforce constraints in real time across thousands of parallel simulation environments, as required by modern massively parallel Deep Reinforcement Learning training. Evaluated on trajectory-tracking tasks in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, CALOS reduces lateral tracking error by 55-60% relative to an unconstrained Proximal Policy Optimization baseline while achieving zero attitude-constraint violations on the training trajectory. By restricting exploration to safe regions of the state space, the safety layer also accelerates training convergence and improves data efficiency without producing suboptimal policies.

</details>


### [54] [DRT&R: Direct Radar Teach & Repeat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17766)
*Alexander Krawciw,Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DRT&R：将直接雷达处理与局部建图结合，最大化保留信息，在道路与非道路环境中实现SOTA旋转雷达导航定位与闭环跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达导航对雨雪、沙尘、雾霾等导致激光雷达失效的恶劣条件具鲁棒性。已有的基于整幅雷达扫描（而非稀疏点）的直接法提升了道路全局定位性能，但尚未用于非道路环境或闭环系统；且直接全局地图会平滑掉依赖视角的雷达伪影，在沿相似轨迹建图与定位时丢失可提供位姿信息的关键线索。

Method: DRT&R（Direct Radar Teach & Repeat）：将直接雷达处理与局部建图相结合的旋转雷达导航栈，通过局部建图保留依赖视角的雷达伪影等信息；与MPC控制器构成闭环，在Clearpath Warthog非道路车辆上实时运行。

Result: 使用344 km道路数据与20 km非道路数据，多数道路与非道路场景定位误差在4 cm以内，几何退化及稀疏环境下为12 cm；闭环自主运行超过10 km，实时性满足，非道路雷达导航跟踪性能达SOTA。

Conclusion: 直接雷达处理结合局部建图可显著提升信息保留量，DRT&R在道路与非道路环境的定位及闭环跟踪上均取得SOTA，验证了旋转雷达在复杂非结构化场景导航中的实用性与实时性。

Abstract: Radar-based navigation is appealing for its robustness to adverse conditions involving airborne particles, such as precipitation, dust, fog, and smoke, that can cause lidar-based systems to fail. Recently, direct methods that retain and use the entire radar scan rather than sparse points have improved on-road global localization performance. However, they have yet to be deployed in off-road environments or in closed-loop systems. Additionally, even direct global maps may lose information: their global nature leads to a smoothing out of viewpoint-dependent radar artifacts, which can provide pose information when mapping and localization occur along similar trajectories. This paper introduces Direct Radar Teach & Repeat (DRT&R): a direct spinning radar-based navigation stack that maximizes the amount of retained information by combining direct radar processing with local mapping. DRT&R yields state-of-the-art (SOTA) localization performance in both on-road and off-road environments. Using 344 km of on-road data and 20 km of off-road data, DRT&R is able to localize to within 4 cm in most on-road and off-road conditions, and 12 cm in geometrically degenerate and sparse environments. DRT&R is also evaluated autonomously in closed loop with an MPC controller for more than 10 km using a Clearpath Warthog off-road vehicle, demonstrating that it runs in real time and achieves SOTA tracking performance for off-road radar navigation.

</details>


### [55] [HINT-Plan: Human Intention-Aware Robot Task Planning in Context-Rich Environments using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17771)
*Yuchen Liu,Luigi Palmieri,Lujun Li,Radu State,Ilche Georgievski,Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HINT-Plan，将视觉语言模型（VLM）预测的人类高层意图转化为目标状态，结合分层场景图与形式化任务规划，实现人机协同的任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有将人类意识引入移动机器人决策的研究多聚焦于低层运动规划中的避障，忽视了人类存在带来的高层行为挑战。为填补这一空白，需要把人类意图预测整合进机器人任务规划层面。

Method: HINT-Plan 使用 VLM 从第三人称图像观测中预测高层人类意图，将其转换为目标状态；采用分层场景图（SG）作为环境高层表示以捕捉丰富的上下文信息；并将环境拓扑与可操作知识翻译为形式化规划语言，以求解联合任务规划问题并保证计划可执行。

Result: 在照片级真实感仿真中，HINT-Plan 的人机联合任务规划总体成功率达 69.71%，较基线最高提升 35.29%，同时减少了功能冲突。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将推断出的人类意图显式纳入形式化多智能体任务规划，对实现主动式人类感知机器人决策是有效的。

Abstract: Approaches to incorporating human awareness into mobile robot decision-making mainly focus on collision avoidance in low-level motion planning, often overlooking the challenges posed by human presence and high-level behavior. To address this vacancy, we present HINT-Plan, a novel approach to integrate human intention prediction into robot task planning. HINT-Plan employs Vision Language Models (VLMs) to anticipate high-level human intentions from third-person image observations, convert them into goal states, and solve joint task-planning problems. To effectively enable scene awareness in context-rich environments, we use hierarchical Scene Graphs (SGs) as high-level representations of the environment, and translate environmental topology and actionable knowledge into formal planning language to ensure executable plans. Evaluated in a photorealistic simulation, HINT-Plan achieves an overall success rate of 69.71% in joint human-robot task planning, substantially outperforming the baselines by up to 35.29%, while also reducing functional conflicts. The results show the effectiveness of explicitly incorporating inferred human intentions into formal multi-agent task planning for proactive human-aware robot decision-making.

</details>


### [56] [SPROUT: The Open-Source Soft Growing Robot for Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17781)
*Antonio Alvarez Valdivia,Ciera McFarland,Robert Reeve,Ankush Dhawan,Chad Council,Megan Richardson,Margaret McGuinness,Nathaniel Hanson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SPROUT（Soft Pathfinding Robotic Observation Unit）软体机器人系统，旨在弥合软体机器人从实验室到野外应用的差距，并提供开源硬件与软件方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人系统长期被认为是搜救作业的理想候选方案，但从实验室走向现场仍存在显著障碍，缺乏可复制、可靠的软体机器人系统。

Method: 面向专业城市搜救团队多年互动中积累的需求，设计了SPROUT系统，目标是在多尘、潮湿和孤立环境中作业；采用模块化硬件与基于ROS 2的软件栈，支持任务专用载荷与控制功能，并提供代码、部件清单、CAD装配件与搭建说明等补充材料。

Result: 在急救人员使用的坍塌结构训练场地中，SPROUT完成了多种具有挑战性的检查与通行任务；其模块化设计也使其可适配基础设施检测、考古等搜救之外的领域。

Conclusion: SPROUT通过开源、模块化和可靠的设计，推动了软体机器人从实验室向现场应用的转化，并为更广泛的机器人研究社区提供了可复现的构建与扩展平台。

Abstract: Soft robotic systems have long been theorized as ideal candidates for use in search and rescue operations; however, there have been significant barriers to entry in graduating soft robotic systems from the laboratory to the field. To address this gap in replicable, reliable soft robot systems, we present the designs for SPROUT, the Soft Pathfinding Robotic Observation Unit. The system has matured over years of interaction with professional urban search and rescue communities, with the goal of operating in dusty, wet, and isolated conditions. This manuscript contains supplementary material, including code, parts manifests, CAD assemblies, and build instructions to allow the broader robotics research community to build their own SPROUT systems. The modular hardware and ROS 2-based software stack support task-specific payloads and control functions, allowing SPROUT to be adapted for applications beyond search and rescue, including infrastructure inspection and archaeology. Here, we demonstrate SPROUT performing a variety of challenging inspection and traversal tasks in collapsed structure training sites used by first responders. The main project page is available online at https://sprout-mitll.github.io/sprout/.

</details>


### [57] [Learning Multi-Humanoid Pickup and Transport via Decentralized Object-Centric Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17824)
*Bikram Pandit,Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于"抓取附着区域"的物体中心式去中心化控制抽象，统一实现单机抓取、多机协同搬运及机器人间交接，仿真与真机验证了人形机器人团队对变尺寸/重量/几何物体的协同操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机协同搬运需要适应不同尺寸、重量与几何形状的物体，且所需机器人数量随任务变化，导致控制策略需针对不同任务反复设计；作者希望找到一种不依赖逐任务重设计的通用控制抽象，以统一单机抓取、多机协同与机器人间交接。

Method: 采用去中心化的物体中心控制：为每个机器人分配共享物体上的局部附着区域，通过无夹爪的双臂夹持学习完成抓取与搬运；该"基于附着"的接口作为通用控制抽象，无需逐任务重设计。同时对比仅在单机抓取数据上训练的策略与显式多机训练策略的表现。

Result: 仅在单机抓取任务上训练的策略对协同场景具有良好的非平凡迁移能力，说明该抽象已捕捉到协调所需的大量结构信息；显式多机训练进一步提升性能，表明共享物体耦合带来的协调动力学值得直接学习。仿真中在不同团队规模与物体几何下均验证有效，并完成真机sim-to-real迁移，使真实人形机器人执行协同操作任务。

Conclusion: 基于附着区域的物体中心控制是统一单机抓取、多机协同搬运与机器人间交接的有效抽象，兼具良好的迁移性与可扩展性，并通过真机实验证明了其现实可行性。

Abstract: We study cooperative multi-humanoid pickup and transport of objects with varying size, weight, and geometry, requiring robot teams of different sizes. Our approach uses decentralized object-centric control, where each humanoid is assigned a local attachment region on the shared object and learns to realize pickup and transport through gripperless bimanual pinching. This attachment-based interface provides a common control abstraction spanning single-robot pickup, cooperative multi-robot transport, and robot-to-robot handover, without per-task redesign. We find that policies trained only on single-robot pickup already transfer nontrivially to cooperative settings, suggesting that this abstraction captures much of the structure needed for coordination. At the same time, explicit multi-robot training further improves performance, showing that shared-object coupling introduces coordination dynamics that are beneficial to learn directly. We validate the approach in simulation across varying team sizes and object geometries, and demonstrate sim-to-real transfer on hardware, where the learned controllers enable real humanoids to perform cooperative manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [58] [Adaptive-MHE : A Sampling-Based Adaptive MPC for Legged Loco-Manipulation via Moving Horizon Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17832)
*Hossein Keshavarz,Alejandro Ramirez-Serrano,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Adaptive-MHE——一种基于移动地平线估计（MHE）的在线采样式系统辨识框架，用于腿足机器人loco-manipulation，可在线估计物体与环境地形参数（如质量、摩擦）并与采样型MPC耦合，实现自适应控制，在仿真与硬件实验中优于基线并接近使用真值参数的控制器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人虽能穿越复杂地形，但loco-manipulation行为生成困难。核心难点在于物体与地形参数对机器人未知，且与仿真参数不匹配造成sim-to-real gap，劣化控制性能。经典系统辨识（Sys-ID）依赖可微动力学假设，而接触丰富的腿足系统不满足该假设；采样式Sys-ID虽规避此限制，但多为离线应用，无法随环境条件变化而自适应。

Method: 提出Adaptive-MHE，一个基于移动地平线估计（MHE）的在线采样式Sys-ID框架：通过大规模并行rollout匹配仿真与记录的状态轨迹，在线估计物体与地形物理参数（质量、摩擦等），并将该估计与采样型模型预测控制器（MPC）耦合，实现面向变化与不确定环境的自适应loco-manipulation。

Result: 在仿真与硬件实验中，该框架一致优于基线方法，并达到与可访问真值参数的控制器相当的性能。

Conclusion: 在线采样式Sys-ID（Adaptive-MHE）可有效缓解参数失配导致的sim-to-real gap，为变化与不确定环境下的腿足机器人loco-manipulation提供自适应能力，且无需依赖可微动力学假设。

Abstract: Legged robots have demonstrated a remarkable ability to traverse various terrains, yet generating effective loco-manipulation behaviors remains challenging. A key difficulty is that object and terrain parameters are typically unknown to the robot, and mismatches between these parameters and their simulated counterparts introduce a sim-to-real gap that degrades control performance. Classical system identification (Sys-ID) methods often assume differentiable dynamics, an assumption that does not hold for contact-rich legged systems. Sampling-based Sys-ID avoids this restriction by directly matching simulated and recorded state trajectories through massively parallel rollouts, but existing approaches are typically applied offline and do not adapt as environmental conditions change. We present Adaptive-MHE an online sampling-based Sys-ID framework, based on moving horizon estimation (MHE), that estimates the physical parameters of objects and terrain in the environment (e.g., mass, friction) and couples this estimate with a sampling-based model predictive controller, enabling adaptive loco-manipulation in changing and uncertain environments. In simulation and hardware experiments, our framework consistently outperforms baselines and matches the performance of a controller with access to ground-truth parameters.

</details>


### [59] [VCTP: Vehicle-Conditioned Terrain Planning for Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17834)
*Akshay Naik,Ramavarapu S. Sreenivas,Dustin Nottage,Ahmet Soylemezoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出车辆条件地形规划（VCTP），通过在八个朝向上评估共享的高程与地表ID图层，将车辆朝向、车身几何、载荷车轮接触与地形代价及俯仰/横滚预测统一建模，并用于双向路径规划与增量更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆朝向同时影响轮胎下方的地表以及车身的俯仰和横滚，传统地形规划往往忽略这种朝向-接触-姿态之间的耦合关系，因此需要一种能保留这些关系的规划方法。

Method: VCTP在八个朝向上评估共享的高程与地表ID图层；以车身几何约束可通行性，以载荷车轮接触确定建模地表代价及预测的俯仰与横滚；结合D* Lite与车辆状态搜索规划含前进和倒车的路线；观测变化时重算所有受影响的车身或接触查询，并采用选择性更新。

Result: 在完全观测的双履带仿真中，于车轮接触处采样相比车辆中心采样使建模地表代价降低39.3%且路径更短；在RGator记录的离线规划中，VCTP相对以距离为中心的规划降低了已识别地表上的建模代价与观测支撑，但不完整覆盖导致整体路线排序尚无法确定；在474次比较中，选择性更新与完全重算结果一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明车轮接触位置与车辆朝向何时影响路线选择，验证了车辆条件建模在代价降低、路径缩短和增量更新效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: A vehicle's heading affects both the surfaces beneath its tires and its pitch and roll. We present Vehicle-Conditioned Terrain Planning (VCTP), which retains these relationships by evaluating shared elevation and surface-ID layers at eight headings. Body geometry constrains admissibility, while loaded wheel contacts determine modeled surface cost and predicted pitch and roll. Established D* Lite and vehicle-state search use these evaluations to plan routes with forward and reverse motion. When observations change, VCTP recomputes every affected body or contact query. In fully observed two-track simulations, sampling at wheel contacts rather than at the vehicle center lowers modeled surface cost by 39.3% while shortening the route. In offline planning on RGator recordings, VCTP also reduces modeled costs over identified surfaces and observed support relative to distance-focused planning, although incomplete coverage leaves full-route rankings unresolved. Selective updates match full recomputation in all 474 comparisons using recorded map changes. These results identify when wheel-contact placement and vehicle heading affect route choice.

</details>


### [60] [Timely Activation of Safety Filters via One-Step Reachability Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17904)
*Javier Borquez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对离散时间执行下最小限制性安全过滤器失效的问题，提出一步扩展BRT方法，将扩展边界作为触发条件以保证离散执行下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Hamilton-Jacobi可达性的最小限制性安全过滤器依赖连续时间假设，仅在系统到达不安全集（BRT）边界时才覆盖标称控制器。但实际机器人系统以离散采样间隔施加控制，导致更新间隙系统可能跳入不安全BRT，产生理论上可避免的失效。

Method: 引入一步扩展BRT（one-step expanded BRT），即预测所有能在单个时间步内到达真实BRT的状态集合。将该扩展集形式化为一个修改后的可达性问题，并使用标准连续时间求解器计算；以扩展边界作为安全过滤器的激活条件。

Result: 所提方法使安全干预足够提前，从而在离散时间执行下保证正确性，解决了连续时间保证与离散采样之间的不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 通过一步扩展BRT作为激活条件，可在离散时间执行下维持安全过滤器的正确性，且无需专用求解器，可复用标准连续时间可达性工具。

Abstract: Least-restrictive safety filters based on Hamilton-Jacobi reachability provide strong safety guarantees by overriding a nominal controller only when the system reaches the boundary of the set of unsafe states defined as a Backward Reachable Tube (BRT). These guarantees, however, rely on the continuous-time nature of the underlying formulation. In practice, robotic systems apply control at discrete sampling intervals, which creates a mismatch where the system may jump into the unsafe BRT between updates, allowing failures that are theoretically avoidable. This work introduces a principled solution based on a one-step expanded BRT that predicts all states capable of reaching the true BRT within a single timestep. By using this expanded boundary as the activation condition for the safety filter, safety interventions occur early enough to ensure correctness under discrete-time execution. We formulate this expanded set as a modified reachability problem and compute it using standard continuous-time solvers.

</details>


### [61] [Map2Route: Benchmarking Compositional Language-Grounded Route Planning over Semantic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17910)
*Muyi Bao,Hang Xu,Jingfan Tang,Zihan Liu,Yuxin Cai,Chen Lv,Wenshan Wang,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Map2Route——一个基于预构建语义地图、用于组合式语言锚定路径规划的人工标注基准，并提出了Grounding2Route方法，在七个基线中全面领先，但仍与人类示范存在较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作对语言锚定路径规划中复杂指令的覆盖不足，缺乏能同时考察关系/比较/嵌套描述、必经区域、避让要求、多种软偏好以及空间与阶段作用域的统一基准，因此需要构建更具挑战性的评测平台并推动方法进步。

Method: 构建Map2Route基准：包含40个场景、1,000个episode，指令采用关系、比较、嵌套描述来指认与路径相关的物体和区域，并规定有序必经区域、避让要求、五类软偏好以及空间与路径阶段作用域。提出Grounding2Route方法，融合可执行代码即锚定（executable code-as-grounding）、验证引导修复与作用域感知规划。

Result: 在七个代表性适配基线上，Grounding2Route在所有指标上显著优于现有方法；但与人类示范相比仍存在明显差距。

Conclusion: Map2Route具有较高难度，任务仍有相当大的提升空间；Grounding2Route验证了代码即锚定与验证修复结合的有效性，同时表明语言锚定组合式路径规划仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We introduce Map2Route, a human-curated benchmark for compositional language-grounded route planning over pre-built semantic maps. Map2Route contains 1,000 episodes across 40 scenes, where instructions use relational, comparative, and nested descriptions to identify route-relevant objects and regions, while specifying ordered must-pass regions, must-avoid requirements, five categories of soft preferences, and spatial and route-stage scopes, which is partially tested by existing works. Alongside Map2Route, we propose Grounding2Route, which combines executable code-as-grounding with verification-guided repair and scope-aware planning.Across seven representative adapted baselines, Grounding2Route substantially outperforms existing methods in all metrics. Despite these gains, a substantial gap to human demonstrations remains, highlighting the difficulty of Map2Route and the considerable headroom for future progress. Additional qualitative results and resources are available on https://anonymous.4open.science/w/Map2Route-F05F/.

</details>


### [62] [Multi-Session Multimodal Underwater Mapping with Acoustic and Optical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17929)
*Precious Philip-Ifabiyi,Valerio Franchi,Fausto Ferreira,Nuno Gracias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于因子图优化的多会话、多模态水下建图框架，联合优化轨迹、3D地标、传感器外参与会话间对齐，在真实数据上提升地图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海底精确建图对海洋科学、考古与环境监测至关重要，但不同传感器（侧扫声呐与光学相机）跨独立测量会话的数据融合受定位漂移与传感器外参偏移困扰，难以直接对齐。

Method: 基于因子图优化，联合优化载体轨迹、3D地标位置、传感器外参以及每会话的全局对齐变换；将刚性会话间校正与局部轨迹形变相结合，同时补偿会话间偏移与会话内因累积导航误差产生的畸变。

Result: 在加泰罗尼亚海岸采集的真实数据集上验证，地图一致性在所有指标（含像素精度与平均交并比）上均优于未优化与刚性对齐基线；像素精度较未优化基线提升3.4%，相当于约14700 m²区域的语义标注改善；定性结果显示在显著轨迹畸变与会话间错位下，声呐与光学地图仍保持一致配准。

Conclusion: 该框架能够从异构水下测量数据中生成连贯的多模态海底地图，展现其在多会话多模态海底建图中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate seafloor mapping is essential for marine science, archaeology, and environmental monitoring. However, integrating data from different sensors, such as side-scan sonar and optical cameras, collected across separate survey sessions, remains challenging due to positioning drift and sensor offsets. This paper presents a multi-session, multimodal underwater mapping framework based on factor graph optimization. The method jointly optimizes vehicle trajectories, 3D landmark positions, sensor extrinsics, and per-session global alignment transformations. By combining rigid inter-session corrections with local trajectory deformations, it compensates for both inter-session offsets and intra-session distortions from accumulated navigation errors. The proposed methodology was validated on real-world datasets collected along the Catalan coast. Results show measurable improvements in map consistency over both unoptimized and rigid-alignment baselines across all metrics, including Pixel Accuracy and mean Intersection over Union. The method achieves a 3.4% improvement in pixel accuracy over the unoptimized baseline, corresponding to improved semantic labelling across approximately 14700 $\text{m}^2$ of mapped area. Qualitative results further show consistent co-registration between sonar and optical maps, even in the presence of significant trajectory distortions and inter-session misalignments. These findings demonstrate the potential of the proposed framework to generate coherent multimodal seafloor maps from heterogeneous underwater surveys.

</details>


### [63] [Feedback-Modulated Harmonic Policies for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17946)
*Yixuan Jia,Steven Roche,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种命令条件化的傅里叶级数关节轨迹表示，由上下文网络生成谐波系数与反馈网络权重，在线调整关节偏移、谐波增益、频率与相位，并在仿真与Unitree Go2真机上验证其周期结构与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见学习型四足运动策略直接将观测映射为关节级动作，使运动的周期结构隐含在策略中，缺乏显式表征，难以分析和利用步态的周期性。作者希望探索一种显式表达关节周期轨迹的替代表示方式。

Method: 将每条关节轨迹表示为命令条件化的傅里叶级数，并结合机器人状态反馈在线修改；上下文网络输出傅里叶系数以及每步反馈网络的权重，其输出在执行中调整关节偏移、谐波增益、频率和相位。仿真中与直接输出关节目标的MLP策略进行对比，分析隐层激活；对选定MLP rollout做动态模式分解（DMD），以识别主导激活模态及其与足高振荡频率的关系。

Result: 谐波波形随命令速度改变频率与形状；DMD显示MLP激活的主导模态集中在足高振荡频率及其二次谐波附近，表明即使无显式傅里叶生成器也存在周期结构。在Unitree Go2上，仿真训练的谐波控制器取得暂定机载估计峰值速度3.67 m/s，并在不同试验中承载最高5.883 kg附加载荷。

Conclusion: 显式傅里叶级数表示为四足运动策略提供了可解释的周期结构，并与隐式MLP策略中出现的周期性激活模态相呼应；该表示在真实四足机器人上实现了较高速度与负载能力，表明显式频率结构是可行的运动控制替代方案。

Abstract: Learned quadruped locomotion policies commonly map observations directly to joint-level actions, leaving the periodic structure of locomotion implicit in the policy. We investigate an alternative representation in which each joint trajectory is expressed as a command-conditioned Fourier series and modified online using feedback from the robot state. A context network generates the Fourier coefficients and the weights of a per-step feedback network, whose outputs adjust joint offsets, harmonic gains, frequency, and phase during execution. In simulation, we examine this explicit frequency structure alongside the hidden activations of an MLP policy that directly outputs joint targets. The harmonic waveforms change frequency and shape with commanded speed. Dynamic mode decomposition of selected MLP rollouts reveals dominant activation modes near the foot-height oscillation frequency and its second harmonic, showing periodic structure without an explicit Fourier generator. On a Unitree Go2, the simulation-trained harmonic controller records a provisional onboard-estimated peak speed of 3.67 meter per second and carries added loads up to 5.883 kilogram in separate trials.

</details>


### [64] [Causal-History Test-Time Scaling for Failure Recovery in Autoregressive World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18016)
*Lin Li,Long Chen,Kwunhang,Wong,Jiaming Lei,Song Jin,Shucheng Du,Chuhan Zhang,Songchen Ma,Weihao Zhang,Jun Xiao,Kwang-Ting,Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 WAM 失败恢复框架，将恢复问题重构为"因果历史上的测试时缩放"，通过触发、历史前缀恢复与假设验证三阶段提升机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）主要在成功轨迹上训练，当真实执行偏离学习到的动力学时容易失败；自回归 WAM 中执行误差会进入因果历史并持续影响后续预测，放大该问题。

Method: 免训练框架，把失败恢复形式化为因果历史上的测试时缩放，分解为三个耦合决策（何时修正历史、从哪里恢复可靠前缀、哪种历史配置最优），对应三阶段：(1) 进度感知恢复触发器，检测持续无进展并在状态允许干预时才触发；(2) 历史前缀恢复，定位不可靠历史后缀，检索与当前物理状态匹配的历史锚点，在保留前缀上重建因果 KV 状态并以最新真实观测为条件；(3) 假设验证，比较完整历史、恢复前缀与完全重置三种假设的未来推演并提交最优者。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界操作环境中任务成功率一致提升；消融实验验证了各恢复阶段的贡献。

Conclusion: 无需额外训练，通过对因果历史的测试时缩放即可有效实现自回归 WAM 的失败恢复，且各阶段设计均不可或缺。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation by jointly modeling future visual dynamics and robot actions. However, existing WAMs are trained predominantly on successful trajectories, making them prone to failure when real-world execution diverges from the learned dynamics. This issue is amplified in autoregressive WAMs, where execution errors become part of the causal history and continue to influence subsequent predictions. To this end, we introduce \method{}, a training-free framework that reformulates failure recovery as \emph{test-time scaling over causal histories}. This formulation decomposes recovery into three coupled decisions: \emph{when} to revise the causal history, \emph{where} to recover a reliable history prefix, and \emph{which} history configuration best supports subsequent execution. Specifically, \method{} realizes these decisions through three stages: 1) \textbf{Progress-Aware Recovery Trigger} detects persistent non-progress and triggers recovery only when the current execution state permits intervention; 2) \textbf{History-Prefix Recovery} identifies the unreliable history suffix, retrieves a historical anchor matching the current physical state, and reconstructs the causal KV state from the retained prefix while conditioning on the latest real observation; and 3) \textbf{Hypothesis Verification} compares the future continuations induced by complete-history, recovered-prefix, and full-reset hypotheses, and commits the best-supported hypothesis. Experiments in both simulated and real-world manipulation settings demonstrate consistent improvements in task success, while ablations confirm the contribution of each recovery stage.

</details>
