### [65] [Online Multimodal Workload Assessment in Contact-Rich Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18031)
*Yanyi Chen,Fan Yang,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线多模态评估框架，融合交互力、TCP运动学与皮肤电导水平（SCL），构建四个可解释的工作负荷相关因子，并利用路径曲率和任务进程动态调整其权重，在接触丰富的物理人机交互中实现连续、透明的工作负荷评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的物理人机交互对物理交互、运动调节和生理响应提出时变需求，因此需要连续评估交互工作负荷。然而现有学习型方法虽性能强，却往往缺乏透明度，而固定权重方案又难以适应运动需求与生理状态的时间变化。

Method: 框架将交互力旋量、平面工具中心点（TCP）运动学以及皮肤电导水平（SCL）整合为四个可解释的工作负荷相关因子。各因子的相对贡献由路径曲率调节以反映运动需求变化，并由任务进程调节以考虑随时间的渐进生理变化。实验纳入24名被试，在温度、声学噪声、照度构成的18种受控组合下、两种导纳控制模式中进行评估。采用严格的留一被试（LOSO）评估，以标准化瞳孔直径（PD_z）作为独立生理参考，并与静态变体及代表性学习型基线进行比较。

Result: 该框架在30秒分块上与生理参考的群体平均Spearman相关系数为ρ30=0.308，24名被试中有23名呈现个体层面的正相关。整体性能与最先进的学习型基线相当，同时通过显式工作负荷相关因子和明确权重规则保持评估过程透明，并优于对应的固定权重形式。在两种测试的导纳控制模式下性能保持一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该框架为接触丰富物理人机交互中的连续交互工作负荷评估提供了一种透明且可解释的方法，兼顾了与学习型基线相当的性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Contact-rich physical human--robot interaction (pHRI) imposes time-varying demands associated with physical interaction, motor regulation, and physiological response, motivating continuous assessment of interaction workload. This paper presents an online multimodal assessment framework that integrates interaction wrench, planar tool-center-point (TCP) kinematics, and skin conductance level (SCL) into four interpretable workload-related factors. Their relative contributions are adjusted using path curvature to reflect changes in motion demand and task progression to account for gradual physiological variation over time. The framework was evaluated with 24 participants across 18 controlled combinations of temperature, acoustic noise, and illuminance under two admittance-control modes. Strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluation used standardized pupil diameter ($\mathrm{PD}_z$) as an independent physiological reference and included comparisons with static variants and representative state-of-the-art learning-based baselines. The proposed framework achieves a cohort-mean $30\,\mathrm{s}$ block-wise Spearman correlation of $ρ_{30}=0.308$ with the physiological reference, with positive subject-level correspondence in 23 of 24 participants. Its overall performance is comparable to the state-of-the-art learning-based baseline. At the same time, our framework keeps the assessment process transparent through explicit workload-related factors and defined weighting rules, while outperforming the corresponding fixed-weight formulation. The framework also maintains consistent performance across the two tested admittance-control modes. These results support a transparent and interpretable approach to continuous interaction workload assessment in contact-rich pHRI.

</details>


### [66] [Geometric Shortcuts for Complex Trunk Postures: Dual-Helicity Coupling Enables Low-Dimensional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18050)
*Huishi Huang,Danlu Chen,Matteo Lo Preti,Jun Liu,Marcelo H. Ang,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 大象象鼻的复杂姿态可能并非完全依赖精细的分节肌肉激活，而源于对侧手性斜肌之间的"双螺旋耦合"这一低维几何捷径。作者用简化软体机器人样机验证：仅调节两个几何参数即可生成丰富的大象式姿态库，提示存在双层控制架构，并对连续体机器人设计与生物学假说具有启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 象鼻能在缺乏（或无需）逐节精细激活的前提下产生高度多样的姿态，这一现象背后的机制尚不明确。作者试图寻找一种可解释"以少控多"的生成机制，并借此为连续体机器人设计提供新的思路与生物学假设。

Method: 提出"双螺旋耦合"假设：由方向相反的斜向肌肉（左手性与右手性）相互耦合形成低维几何捷径。构建简化的软体机器人样机，将控制变量压缩为两个几何参数，通过改变这两个参数生成姿态库并考察其是否覆盖大象式姿态范围。

Result: 仅变化两个几何参数即可生成一大类与大象高度相似的姿态，表明复杂的姿态多样性可由低维参数空间涌现，而非必须依赖分节级的独立精细激活。

Conclusion: 象鼻（及类似连续体结构）可能采用"双层控制架构"：上层以少数几何参数设定整体手性与耦合模式，下层由该几何约束生成姿态细节。该结论为连续体机器人设计提供简化控制策略，同时为生物力学假说提供可检验的工程验证途径。

Abstract: How do elephant trunks generate complex postures without relying solely on fine segmental activation? We propose that part of this complexity arises from a low-dimensional geometric shortcut: dual-helicity coupling between opposite-handed oblique muscles. In a simplified soft-robotic prototype, varying only two geometric parameters generates a broad library of elephant-like postures, suggesting a dual-layer control architecture with implications for continuum robot design and biological hypotheses.

</details>


### [67] [CLASP: A Cluster-Level Autonomous Selective Picking Robot with a Soft Rolling-Band Gripper for Fresh-Market Blueberry Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18051)
*Yixuan Xia,Yilin Cai,Natalia Belen Espinoza,Changying Li,Zilfina Rubio Ames,Xin Zhang,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CLASP——一种面向鲜食蓝莓的簇级自主选择性采摘机器人，配备软体主动滚动带夹爪（SARB-Gripper），通过全局-局部感知管线与闭环拉力控制实现簇级选择性采摘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲜食蓝莓需要选择性、轻柔采摘，但人工成本高；跨行机械采收快却无选择性，易损伤混熟果实，只能进入加工市场；现有选择性采摘机器人多针对单果而非果簇，对小型密集簇状蓝莓效率低。

Method: 设计SARB-Gripper：两条柔性带包裹果簇并相对果实滚动，顺序摘下成熟果实，同时闭环调节拉力使其低于未成熟果实脱落阈值；构建全局-局部感知管线，用手眼分离（eye-to-hand）相机进行全局簇检测与目标选择，用眼在手上（eye-in-hand）相机进行局部定位与果簇朝向估计。

Result: 田间测量确认成熟与未成熟果实的脱落力存在明显区分；SARB-Gripper的拉力控制误差在3.7%以内；端到端田间试验中，CLASP自主抓取25个果簇中的23个，成功率达92%；单机组件成本约3326美元。

Conclusion: CLASP实现了簇级选择性采摘，为鲜食蓝莓提供了一种可扩展的低成本选择性采收方案。

Abstract: Fresh-market blueberries require selective, gentle picking, which is labor-intensive and expensive. Over-the-row machine harvesters are fast but non-selective, bruising mixed-ripeness fruit and limiting yield to the processing market. Selective robotic harvesters typically target individual fruits rather than fruit clusters, which limits harvesting efficiency for small, densely clustered blueberries. This paper presents CLASP, a Cluster-Level Autonomous Selective Picking robot with a Soft Active Rolling-Band Gripper (SARB-Gripper). Two compliant bands envelop the cluster and roll against the fruit, drawing mature berries off in sequence, while closed-loop regulation of the pulling force keeps the applied load below the immature detachment threshold. A global-to-local perception pipeline pairs an eye-to-hand camera for global cluster detection and target selection with an eye-in-hand camera for local localization and cluster orientation estimation. Field measurements confirm a clear detachment-force separation between mature and immature fruit, and the SARB-Gripper reproduces a commanded pulling force to within \SI{3.7}{\percent}, enabling selective harvesting at the cluster level. In end-to-end field trials, CLASP autonomously grasped 23 of 25 presented clusters (\SI{92}{\percent}). With the component cost of approximately \$3326 per unit, CLASP offers a scalable approach to selective cluster-level harvesting for fresh-market blueberries.

</details>


### [68] [An Efficient Algorithm for Minimum-Pressure Growth Planning of Vine Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18070)
*Andres C. Torres,Tobia Marcucci,Elliot W. Hawkes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出藤蔓机器人绕多面体障碍生长的最小压力路径规划算法，2D全局最优、3D近似最优，通过仿真与硬件实验验证，并开源VinePlanner。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藤蔓机器人依靠尖端延伸在杂乱环境中导航，但其生长规划（寻找最优生长路径）研究不足；现有规划器未考虑跟随给定路径所需的生长压力，压力过高会导致机器人爆裂，因此需要寻找最小压力路径。

Method: 首先推导任意形状藤蔓机器人的生长压力方程；据此证明总存在一条仅在障碍物特定点处弯折的分段线性最小压力路径；将生长规划问题转化为带时变权的最短路径问题，并用改进的Dijkstra算法高效求解。

Result: 算法在2D保证全局最优，在3D给出近似解且误差随离散化参数趋于零而消失；数值仿真表明方法兼具速度与可扩展性；硬件实验验证了算法有效性；提供开源高性能Python实现VinePlanner。

Conclusion: 该工作为藤蔓机器人提供了考虑生长压力的最优路径规划框架，兼顾最优性保证与计算效率，并通过开源实现推动实际应用。

Abstract: Vine robots navigate cluttered environments by extending from their tip. Although their ability to operate in such environments has been extensively demonstrated, little work has addressed growth planning, i.e., finding optimal growth paths. Moreover, existing planners do not account for the growth pressure necessary to follow a given path, which can cause the robot to burst when it is too high. In this paper, we address the problem of finding minimum-pressure paths for vine robots growing around polytopic obstacles. We propose an efficient algorithm that is guaranteed to find globally optimal solutions in 2D and approximate solutions in 3D, with an error that vanishes as a discretization parameter approaches zero. First, we derive a growth pressure equation for vine robots of arbitrary shape, which we use to show that there always exists a minimum-pressure path that is piecewise-linear and can bend only at specific points on the obstacles. We then leverage this observation to reduce the growth-planning problem to a shortest-path problem with time-dependent weights, which we efficiently solve using a modified Dijkstra's algorithm. We demonstrate the speed and scalability of our approach through numerical simulations. We also validate our algorithm with hardware experiments and provide an open-source and high-performance implementation in the Python package, VinePlanner: https://github.com/Ahsoka/VinePlanner.

</details>


### [69] [Characterizing Refraction-Induced Ranging Bias in Underwater Collaborative Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18073)
*Timothy Kogucki,Alan Papalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作通过仿真研究折射引起的声学测距偏差对千米尺度多智能体协同定位的影响，发现声速梯度剧烈区域轨迹估计显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体测距辅助导航是大尺度水下定位的有前景方案，但其精度高度依赖测距质量。声速变化会导致声线折射弯曲，而现有传感器融合流程出于算法可解性假设直线传播，从而系统性高估距离。然而，这种偏差对千米尺度多智能体协同定位的影响尚未被探索。

Method: 开展一系列仿真实验，多个智能体在千米尺度上运行。仿真使用HYCOM再分析数据重建真实海洋条件，用射线追踪生成含折射的测距值，并采用集中式多智能体因子图估计器量化测距偏差对轨迹估计的影响。

Result: 初步结果表明，折射引起的偏差可导致估计轨迹显著退化，在声速梯度剧烈区域尤为明显。同时开源了仿真环境以支持后续研究。

Conclusion: 折射导致的测距偏差在千米尺度多智能体协同定位中不可忽略，尤其在强声速梯度区域，需在传感器融合中加以建模与补偿。

Abstract: This work studies how refraction-induced bias on acoustic ranging affects multi-agent collaborative localization in a range of oceanographic conditions and spatial scales. While multi-agent range-aided navigation, which uses range measurements to either fixed infrastructure or other agents, is a promising solution to the challenges of large-scale underwater localization, its accuracy depends strongly on the quality of range measurements. Sound speed variability induces refraction (bending) of acoustic rays, yet, for algorithmic tractability, standard sensor fusion pipelines assume straight-line propagation. This refraction systematically biases range measurements to be longer than the straight-line assumption predicts. However, the effects of this bias on multi-agent collaborative localization on kilometer scales remains unexplored. We present a series of simulated experiments with several agents operating over kilometer scales. The simulation uses HYCOM reanalysis data to recreate realistic oceanographic conditions, ray tracing to generate refraction-informed ranges, and a centralized multi-agent factor graph estimator to quantify the resulting measurement bias on estimated trajectories. Preliminary results indicate that refraction-induced bias can induce significant degradation of estimated trajectories, particularly in regions with sharp sound-speed gradients. We also share the simulation environment to support further studies https://github.com/UMich-RobotExploration/manta-ray.

</details>


### [70] [Not All Layers Need Tuning: Diagnosing and Directing Adaptation in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18084)
*Shahram Najam Syed,Arthur Jakobsson,Prayuj Sachdev,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文发现VLA模型的适配成本在不同网络区域间高度结构化，提出"观察—诊断—分配—适配"流水线，用极少量可训练参数实现超越均匀LoRA甚至全量微调的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调VLA模型到新部署环境代价高昂，但现有方法对所有网络区域施加同等容量的适配器，隐含假设每个区域需要等量调整，这一假设缺乏验证且可能造成参数浪费。

Method: 在五种架构各异的VLA（OpenVLA-OFT、π0、SmolVLA、DTP、Octo，93M–7B）上，以区域隔离微调下的归一化参数位移度量区域适配成本；提出"观察—诊断—分配—适配"流水线：仅凭十个无标注目标观测、无需微调，通过无参考梯度信号、蒙特卡洛Dropout信号及与缓存源参考的CKA分数估计各区域成本；分配器在参数预算下生成变秩LoRA适配器并冻结校准良好的区域，随后用标准LoRA微调。

Result: 诊断方法在各部署内对区域排序的中位Spearman为0.91；分配策略在LIBERO与CALVIN上于所有测试预算下匹配或超过均匀LoRA。在xArm-7实体平台上，指令措辞偏移下以0.04%可训练参数匹配全量微调；在五个未重训的留出场景中领先所有基线，30次rollout中成功11–23次，而最强参数高效基线为8–18次（预算相等或更大），全量微调为2–11次。

Conclusion: VLA的适配成本结构足以在微调开始前被测量和利用，据此进行非均匀参数分配可在极低参数预算下取得更优泛化性能。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model for a new deployment environment is expensive, yet most methods apply uniform-capacity adapters to every network region as if every region requires equal adjustment. This paper tests that assumption on five architecturally diverse VLAs (OpenVLA-OFT, $π_0$, SmolVLA, DTP, Octo; 93M-7B parameters). Measuring per-region adaptation cost as normalized parameter displacement under region-isolated fine-tuning reveals an adaptation spectrum in which appearance shifts concentrate cost in the vision encoder, instruction shifts in the language backbone, and novel-object shifts in the vision encoder together with the action head, across all five architectures. To exploit this structure, we introduce a pipeline that observes, diagnoses, allocates, and adapts. From ten unlabeled target observations and without fine-tuning, the diagnostic estimates per-region cost by combining reference-free gradient and Monte Carlo Dropout signals with a Centered Kernel Alignment score against a cached source reference; the allocator converts the estimates into variable-rank LoRA adapters under a parameter budget and freezes well-calibrated regions; and standard LoRA fine-tuning trains the resulting adapters. The diagnostic ranks regions within each deployment at a median Spearman of 0.91, and the allocation matches or exceeds uniform LoRA at every budget we tested on LIBERO and CALVIN. On a physical xArm-7, the pipeline matches full fine-tuning under an instruction-wording shift with 0.04% of its trainable parameters, and on five held-out scenes evaluated without retraining it leads every baseline, with 11-23 successes of 30 rollouts against 8-18 for the strongest parameter-efficient baseline at equal or larger budgets and 2-11 for full fine-tuning. These results suggest that adaptation cost in VLAs is structured enough to measure before fine-tuning begins.

</details>


### [71] [ReRadar: Robust Radar Global Localization via Rotation-Equivariant Descriptor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18092)
*Duc Manh Nguyen,Truong Giang Dao,Gia Nghiem Luong,Viet Trung Hoang,Anh Quang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReRadar 毫米波雷达全局定位流程，利用可操纵卷积提取旋转等变特征并结合群池化与 NetVLAD 生成旋转不变描述子，配合地标配准实现 3-DoF 位姿估计，在多个数据集上取得高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描式毫米波雷达全局定位困难，现有场景识别描述子常丢弃精确位姿检索所需的空间结构信息。

Method: 使用可操纵卷积神经网络（steerable CNN）提取旋转等变中间特征；通过群池化（group pooling）与 NetVLAD 聚合形成旋转不变描述子；将描述子检索与基于地标的匹配相结合，估计机器人 3 自由度位姿。

Result: 固定数据库—查询设置下，ReRadar 经目标数据集适配后在 OORD Bellmouth 达 99.37% Recall@1，Mulran DCC01 达 91.44% Recall@1、F1_max 80.99%，falling-snow Boreas 序列达 99.38% Recall@1；无目标数据集数据时跨数据集模型在 OORD 达 98.07% Recall@1，与所评估的 SOTA 方法性能相当。

Conclusion: ReRadar 通过保留空间结构的旋转等变特征与鲁棒聚合，实现了高精度毫米波雷达全局定位，并在目标域适配与跨数据集场景下均表现优异。

Abstract: Global localization with scanning millimeter-wave radar remains challenging because place-recognition descriptors often discard spatial structure needed for accurate pose retrieval. We present ReRadar, a radar global localization pipeline that extracts rotation-equivariant intermediate features using steerable convolutional neural networks, forms rotation-invariant descriptors through group pooling and NetVLAD aggregation, and combines descriptor retrieval with landmark-based matching to estimate the robot's three-degree-of-freedom (3-DoF) pose. Across fixed database-query evaluations, ReRadar with target-dataset adaptation achieves 99.37% Recall@1 on OORD Bellmouth, 91.44% Recall@1 with 80.99% F1_max on Mulran DCC01, and 99.38% Recall@1 on falling-snow Boreas sequence. Without target-dataset data, the cross-dataset model reaches 98.07% Recall@1 on OORD, performing comparably to the evaluated state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond Pixel Similarity: Task-Aware Evaluation of GAN-Based Synthetic Sonar Data for Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18100)
*Hannan Ejaz Keen,Muhammad Moazam Fraz,Karsten Berns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究GAN生成合成声呐数据的图像保真度指标是否能反映下游感知性能，发现像素级指标与目标检测性能存在不一致，PatchGAN配置检测性能好但像素相似度并非最高，主张采用任务感知评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可降低机器人感知中数据采集与标注成本，但生成能保留下游感知相关特征的传感器观测（尤其声呐图像）仍具挑战。作者怀疑传统图像保真度指标能否充分反映GAN合成声呐数据的下游感知性能。

Method: 采用Pix2Pix条件GAN，配置四种不同感受野的判别器：PixelGAN、PatchGAN-16、PatchGAN-70、ImageGAN。使用两个数据集的声呐图像训练，并用SSIM、PSNR、MSE等图像保真度指标评估。同时为补充像素级度量，仅在真实声呐图像上训练YOLOX-S、YOLOX-L和Faster R-CNN检测器，随后在各判别器配置生成的图像上使用相同测试样本与标注进行评估。

Result: 图像保真度与下游目标检测性能之间存在差异：SSIM、PSNR、MSE最优的配置并不总能带来最佳检测性能。特别是PatchGAN配置虽未取得最高像素级相似度分数，却实现了较强的下游检测结果。

Conclusion: 在所考虑的数据集和模型范围内，仅凭像素级图像保真度指标可能无法一致地刻画合成声呐观测中与任务相关的真实性，这促使人们为面向机器人感知的合成传感器数据采用任务感知评估。

Abstract: Synthetic data can reduce the cost of collecting and annotating training data for robotic perception, but generating sensor observations that preserve the characteristics relevant to downstream perception remains challenging, particularly for sonar imagery. In this work, we investigate whether conventional image-fidelity metrics adequately reflect the downstream perception performance of GAN-generated synthetic sonar data. We employ a Pix2Pix conditional generative adversarial network with four discriminator configurations characterized by different receptive fields: PixelGAN, PatchGAN-16, PatchGAN-70, and ImageGAN. The models are trained using sonar imagery from two datasets and evaluated using conventional image-fidelity metrics, including Structural Similarity Index (SSIM), Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), and Mean Squared Error (MSE). To complement these pixel-level measures with task-oriented evaluation, YOLOX-S, YOLOX-L, and Faster R-CNN detectors are trained exclusively on real sonar imagery and subsequently evaluated on the GAN-generated images using identical test samples and annotations across all discriminator configurations. The results reveal a discrepancy between image-fidelity and downstream object-detection performance: the configuration achieving the best SSIM, PSNR, and MSE does not consistently yield the best detection performance. In particular, PatchGAN configurations achieve strong downstream detection results despite not achieving the highest pixel-level similarity scores. These findings suggest, for the datasets and models considered, pixel-level image-fidelity metrics alone may not consistently capture the task-relevant realism of synthetic sonar observations and motivate the use of task-aware evaluation for synthetic sensor data intended for robotic perception.

</details>


### [73] [Technical Report: One-Step Drifting Action Heads for GR00T N1.7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18108)
*Xihe Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将GR00T N1.7的迭代扩散Transformer动作头替换为一步漂移动作头后，动作头前向时间从约45.3ms降至5.0ms、主干加动作头从约70.0ms降至30.6ms，但LIBERO任务成功率系统性下降（Spatial 64.0±4.0%、Goal 52.0±1.0%、Long 26.0±2.6%），作者将其报告为速度—成功率权衡而非整体改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步动作生成能显著降低VLA策略的推理成本，但其对闭环任务成功率的影响尚不明确，本文旨在系统研究这一权衡关系。

Method: 在GR00T N1.7变体上，把迭代式扩散Transformer动作头替换为一步漂移（drifting）动作头，并加入面向异步chunk替换的重叠条件（overlap-conditioned）扩展；所有多种子漂移实验在两张NVIDIA A800 GPU上训练，在LIBERO上做同步闭环评测。

Result: 动作头平均前向时间由约45.3ms降至约5.0ms，主干加动作头由约70.0ms降至约30.6ms；但成功率下降：LIBERO-Spatial 64.0±4.0%、LIBERO-Goal 52.0±1.0%、LIBERO-Long 26.0±2.6%，且种子间方差小，说明退化不能仅由随机初始化解释。

Conclusion: 该结果应被视为速度—成功率权衡而非整体提升；可能成因包括确定性一步模式平均、依赖批次的几何估计、较长的开环chunk执行，以及同步LIBERO评测未启用异步重叠路径。

Abstract: One-step action generation can substantially reduce the inference cost of vision-language-action (VLA) policies, but its effect on closed-loop task success remains an open question. This technical report studies a GR00T N1.7 variant in which the iterative diffusion-transformer action head is replaced by a one-step drifting action head, together with an overlap-conditioned extension for asynchronous chunk replacement. All multi-seed drifting runs were trained on two NVIDIA A800 GPUs. On LIBERO, the action head reduces the mean model-forward time of the action head from approximately $45.3\,\mathrm{ms}$ to $5.0\,\mathrm{ms}$, while the measured backbone-plus-head time falls from approximately $70.0\,\mathrm{ms}$ to $30.6\,\mathrm{ms}$. However, this speedup is accompanied by a systematic reduction in task success. Across three drifting seeds, success is $64.0\pm4.0\%$ on LIBERO-Spatial, $52.0\pm1.0\%$ on LIBERO-Goal, and $26.0\pm2.6\%$ on LIBERO-Long. The low seed variance indicates that the degradation is not explained by random initialization alone. We report the result as a speed--success trade-off rather than an overall improvement, and discuss likely contributing factors including deterministic one-step mode averaging, batch-dependent geometry estimation, long open-loop chunk execution, and the fact that synchronous LIBERO evaluation does not exercise the asynchronous overlap path.

</details>


### [74] [A Comprehensive Review of Generative Physical Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18111)
*Satyam Gaba,Krutiksinh Rana,Siva Sai,Vinay Chamola,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述"生成式物理人工智能"（GPAI），提出五类方法分类体系（RFM、VLA、LBM、DPM、WFM），分析架构基础、应用与局限，并指出数据高效学习、仿真到现实迁移、边缘兼容架构与安全框架四个方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型与物理实体结合推动了机器人学的进展，形成GPAI智能体系统，能够在复杂真实环境中自主感知、推理与行动。但该领域缺乏对架构基础、现有应用及关键局限的系统性梳理，本文因此展开全面综述。

Method: 采用综述与分类学方法，构建五类方法体系：机器人基础模型（RFM）用于跨平台技能迁移；视觉—语言—动作模型（VLA）用于端到端多模态感知与控制；大型行为模型（LBM）用于类人运动生成；扩散策略模型（DPM）用于基于扩散模型的时间连贯动作生成；世界基础模型（WFM）用于物理合规的仿真与数据生成。并分析各类方法间的互补关系。

Result: 通过对自动驾驶、工业自动化、医疗机器人和人形系统等场景的案例分析，识别出显著的性能提升。方法间互补性体现在：WFM为VLA与DPM生成训练数据，RFM支持所学策略的跨平台部署，LBM提供自然行为的运动先验。

Conclusion: 论文总结了四个有前景的研究方向：数据高效学习、仿真到现实迁移、边缘兼容架构与安全框架，为IoT连接环境中智能体与网络化传感器、执行器及边缘设备交互的具身AI发展提供洞见。

Abstract: The integration of large-scale foundation models with physical embodiments has led to significant advancements in robotics known as Generative Physical Artificial Intelligence (GPAI). These agentic AI systems autonomously perceive, reason, and act in complex real-world situations. This survey comprehensively analyzes GPAI systems, focusing on their architectural foundations, current applications, and key limitations. We introduce a taxonomy of five distinct approaches: Robot Foundation Models (RFMs) for cross-platform skill transfer; Vision-Language Action (VLA) models for end-to-end multi-modal perception and control; Large Behavior Models (LBMs) for human-like movement generation; Diffusion Policy Models (DPMs) for diffusion model-based temporally coherent action generation; and World Foundation Models (WFMs) for physics-compliant simulation and data generation. We examine how these approaches complement each other: WFMs generate training data for VLAs and DPMs, RFMs enable cross-platform deployment of learned policies, while LBMs provide motion priors for natural behavior. Through examples across autonomous vehicles, industrial automation, healthcare robotics, and humanoid systems, we identify significant performance improvements and summarize promising research directions in data-efficient learning, sim-to-real transfer, edge-compatible architectures, and safety frameworks. These insights advance embodied AI for IoT-connected environments where intelligent agents interact with networked sensors, actuators, and edge devices.

</details>


### [75] [OpenDexGrasp: Open-vocabulary Task-Oriented Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18117)
*Jiyao Zhang,Junhan Wang,Tianyu Wang,Zeyuan Chen,Anthony Bolten,Yitong Peng,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenDexGrasp，一个面向开放词汇任务导向灵巧抓取生成的统一数据与生成建模框架，并配套构建 OpenDexVerse 数据集，实现无需独立可供性到位姿推断阶段的、任务一致且可执行的高自由度抓取直接生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取合成虽能生成稳定且物理合理的姿态，但真实操作需要抓取保留任务所隐含的功能意图。开放词汇任务导向的灵巧抓取生成要求机器人从自由语言中推断功能意图，并将其落地到多视角视觉观测与物体几何上，进而生成可执行的高自由度抓取，这一设定此前缺乏统一的数据与建模方案。

Method: 提出统一的数据与生成建模框架 OpenDexGrasp，并构建 OpenDexVerse 数据集，按 Coverage-to-Alignment（C2A）配方组织双源监督：OpenDex-Scale 通过自动抓取合成与视觉-语言标注提供大规模语义与几何覆盖；OpenDex-Align 通过人类遥操作与类别级迁移提供高质量具身对齐。模型学习共享的感知-动作潜在表示，将开放词汇视觉-语言上下文与灵巧动作生成耦合；可供性 grounding 与抓取生成在该潜在空间上提供互补监督，从而无需单独的可供性到位姿推断阶段即可直接生成任务一致的灵巧抓取。

Result: 大量仿真与真实机器人实验表明，方法在功能对齐性、物理可行性、对未见类别的泛化能力以及真实世界执行成功率方面均有提升。项目页面与视频见 https://opendexgrasp.github.io/。

Conclusion: 通过覆盖到对齐的双源数据组织与共享感知-动作潜在表示学习，可统一实现开放词汇任务导向的灵巧抓取生成，省去分离的可供性推理阶段，并在仿真与真机中验证功能对齐、物理可行性与泛化性能。

Abstract: Dexterous grasp synthesis has advanced rapidly in generating stable and physically plausible hand poses, but real-world manipulation requires grasps that preserve the function implied by the task. We study open-vocabulary task-oriented dexterous grasp generation, where a robot must infer functional intent from free-form language, ground it in multi-view visual observations and object geometry, and generate an executable high-degree-of-freedom grasp. We present OpenDexGrasp, a unified data and generative modeling framework for this setting. OpenDexVerse provides dual-source supervision organized by the Coverage-to-Alignment (C2A) Recipe: OpenDex-Scale offers large-scale semantic and geometric coverage through automatic grasp synthesis and vision-language annotation, while OpenDex-Align supplies high-quality embodied alignment through human teleoperation and category-level transfer. OpenDexGrasp learns a shared perception-action latent representation that couples open-vocabulary vision-language context with dexterous action generation. Affordance grounding and grasp generation provide complementary supervision over this latent space, enabling direct generation of task-consistent dexterous grasps without a separate affordance-to-pose inference stage. Extensive simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate improved functional alignment, physical feasibility, generalization to unseen categories, and real-world execution success. Additional details and videos are available at https://opendexgrasp.github.io/.

</details>


### [76] [Fetch My Beer: Synthetic-to-real Hierarchical Policy for Smooth Pick-and-place](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18119)
*Yingyue Li,Chenyangguang Zhang,Ruida Zhang,Bowen Fu,Guangyao Zhai,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出合成到真实的框架，将物理验证的数据生成与分层扩散控制器结合，实现液体的稳定运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态敏感的机器人操作（如运输液体容器）要求全程保持物体动力学稳定，而现有系统存在局限：流体仿真对于在线强化学习过于昂贵；人类遥操作会引入意外加速度而诱发晃动；当前策略流水线只优化任务完成而非动态稳定性。

Method: 合成到真实框架：1）可扩展数据流水线合成抓取、用视觉-语言模型过滤不稳定姿态、通过流体仿真验证运输轨迹；2）分层扩散策略，高层模块将语言与视觉观测转换为SE(3)控制目标，潜在扩散控制器先在紧凑潜在空间高效规划，再解码稠密动作块以支持高控制频率。

Result: 大量实验表明，系统在运输平滑性和动态稳定性上优于当前最先进的机器人操作策略。

Conclusion: 耦合物理验证的数据生成与分层扩散控制，可有效解决液体运输中的晃动抑制与防溢出问题，实现平滑稳定的动态敏感操作。

Abstract: Many real-world robotic applications require dynamically sensitive manipulation, where success depends not only on reaching a target state but on maintaining stable object dynamics throughout execution. We study the stable transport of liquid-filled containers, where a robot must move objects to target locations while suppressing sloshing and preventing spillage. Unlike conventional pick-and-place, this task imposes stringent requirements on motion smoothness and trajectory-level stability, exposing clear limitations in existing systems. Specifically, fluid simulation remains too costly for online reinforcement learning; human teleoperation introduces unintended accelerations that induce sloshing during imitation learning; and current policy pipelines optimize for task completion rather than dynamic stability. We propose a synthetic-to-real framework coupling physically validated data generation with a hierarchical, diffusion-based controller. The scalable data pipeline synthesizes grasps, filters unstable poses via a vision-language model, and validates transport trajectories through fluid simulation. The policy is organized with a high-level module that translates language and visual observations into SE(3) control targets, and a latent diffusion controller that first plans efficiently in a compact latent space and then decodes dense action chunks, enabling the high control frequency needed for smooth and stable motion. Extensive experiments show our system outperforms state-of-the-art manipulation policies in transport smoothness and dynamic stability. Our project page: https://fetch-my-beer.github.io/

</details>


### [77] [Prior Evolution and Task Alignment for Aerial Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18153)
*Weiliang Deng,Zhengyang Dang,Yao Mu,Ximin Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在解析规划器内引入学习机制，先学习轨迹先验，再通过CEM流程迭代优化初始化，并利用执行感知Critic评估轨迹的可执行性与作为可微抓取代价，提升空中抓取优化的可靠性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受猛禽空中捕食启发，现有轨迹优化方法存在两大局限：一是优化问题高度非凸、对初始化敏感，有限计算预算下难以获得高质量解；二是人工设计的目标函数仅用有限的可数学处理量描述成功抓取，可能无法完全刻画决定任务成败的因素。

Method: 在解析规划器框架内引入学习：1）从优化运动轨迹中学习轨迹先验；2）采用基于CEM的过程，用部署的优化器评估采样初始化并保留有利者作为新监督以演进先验；3）设计Execution-Aware Critic，从接触、提升和完成结果中学习评估优化轨迹在实际物理执行中成功的可能性，其冻结能量可作为可微抓取代价，使执行数据直接塑造轨迹生成。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，该方法在优化可靠性、轨迹一致性和抓取性能方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 学习可有效弥补解析规划器在空中抓取中的初始化敏感与目标函数抽象化局限，Execution-Aware Critic的可微代价进一步打通执行数据与轨迹生成之间的闭环，验证了学习增强解析规划的可行性。

Abstract: Aerial grasping is a remarkable capability exhibited by predatory birds, allowing them to capture prey through highly coordinated maneuvers in flight. Inspired by this capability, researchers have developed various formulations to reproduce such maneuvers through trajectory optimization. However, two limitations remain in practice. First, the resulting optimization problem is highly nonconvex and sensitive to initialization, making high-quality solutions difficult to obtain under a limited computational budget. Second, prescribed numerical objectives are human-designed abstractions that describe successful grasping through a limited set of mathematically tractable quantities and may not fully capture what determines task success. We investigate how learning can address these limitations within an analytical planner. Accordingly, a trajectory prior is first learned from optimized motions and then evolved through a CEM-based process that evaluates sampled initializations with the deployed optimizer and retains favorable ones as new supervision. An Execution-Aware Critic learns from contact, lift, and completion outcomes to assess whether the optimized trajectories are likely to succeed in physical execution. Its frozen energy can further serve as a differentiable grasping cost, allowing execution data to directly shape trajectory generation. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved optimization reliability, trajectory consistency, and grasping performance.

</details>


### [78] [Energy-Regularized Imitation Learning for Force- and Work-Aware Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18164)
*Toshiki Otani,Hiromu Taketsugu,Norimichi Ukita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出工作感知的策略微调框架：从关节力矩与角位移定义关节空间机械功代理量，训练可微能量预测器将其转为可微正则项，用于微调预训练操作策略，在RLBench 12项任务上平均机械功降低2.1%、成功率略升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略通常只优化任务成功率，忽视运动过程中的物理能耗，导致动作存在物理低效。而仿真器侧的机械功等物理量本身不可微，无法直接作为正则项嵌入策略梯度微调。作者希望在不引入显式可微动力学模型的前提下，实现能量感知的策略优化。

Method: 1) 从关节力矩与关节角位移定义关节空间机械功代理量，作为能量度量；2) 训练一个可微能量预测器，从机器人状态与动作估计该机械功，从而把仿真器侧不可微物理量转化为可微正则项；3) 用该正则项微调预训练操作策略；4) 以RVT-2为基座策略，在RLBench上实例化，覆盖物体接触、关节运动、放置、推动、扫掠等12项任务。

Result: 微调后平均机械功从208.8J降至204.4J，降低2.1%；平均任务成功率从86.2%小幅提升至86.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，工作感知的策略优化能够在不需要显式可微动力学模型的情况下抑制物理低效运动，同时不损害甚至略提升任务成功率，验证了将能量正则化引入操作策略微调的可行性。

Abstract: This paper studies energy-aware manipulation as a physically grounded learning problem. We define a joint-space mechanical-work proxy from joint torque and angular displacement, and train a differentiable energy predictor that estimates this work from robot states and actions. The predictor converts a non-differentiable simulator-side physical quantity into a differentiable regularizer for fine-tuning a pretrained manipulation policy. We instantiate the framework with RVT-2 on RLBench and evaluate 12 manipulation tasks involving object contact, articulated motion, placement, pushing, and sweeping. The proposed fine-tuning reduces the average mechanical work from 208.8J to 204.4J (i.e., 2.1% reduction), while the mean task success rate also increases slightly from 86.2% to 86.9%. These results show that work-aware policy optimization can suppress physically inefficient motion without requiring an explicit differentiable dynamics model.

</details>


### [79] [LUMO: Designing Luminous Contact Morphology for Repeatable Whole-Finger Contact Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18165)
*Dong Ho Kang,Youngsu Ko,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LUMO通过嵌入式LED与柔性硅胶垫的形变光导，使接触位置可从手指侧面光学读取，从而与关节力矩结合解算接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低阻抗机器人手指只能通过关节力矩感知负载大小，但相同力矩可能来自指尖附近的小力或关节附近的大力，因此必须知道接触发生在手指何处才能解算力。

Method: 嵌入式LED照射柔性硅胶垫，接触使垫形变并改变侧面出光模式；同一结构既承载接触载荷又传导光。对垫轮廓、刚性载体及侧向空腔的截面进行多目标优化，兼顾低预载下顺应形变与高载下受限形变，以及不同接触位置在侧面的可分离光学响应。采用刚-柔接触仿真、光线追踪与多目标贝叶斯优化。

Result: 在两种硅胶、六个接触位置以及10 mm和30 mm球形压头条件下，优化后的形貌使相邻位置分离度相对重新建立接触的变异提升15%–59%。利用已知LED间距从光学响应估计接触位置，并与关节力矩融合，在931个样本上得到1.44 N的法向力MAE。在两指手抓取中，多个指节可同时出现局域化侧面响应。

Conclusion: LUMO将接触位置外部可观测化，为低阻抗手指提供了位置-力联合感知方案，并在抓取场景中展现出多指节同时感知的潜力。

Abstract: A low-impedance robot finger reports through joint torque how strongly it is loaded, but the same torque can arise from a small force near the fingertip or a large force near the joint. Resolving the force therefore requires knowing where along the finger contact occurred. LUMO makes that location externally observable. Embedded LEDs illuminate a compliant silicone pad, and contact deforms the pad so that light emerging from the finger's side changes in a pattern set by where the load acts. Because the same structure also carries the contact load, we optimize its cross-section, including the pad profile, rigid carrier, and lateral void, for two behaviors at once. Mechanically, the pad conforms under low preload while the carrier increasingly restricts further deformation as load rises. Optically, different contact locations produce separated responses on the finger's side. The search uses rigid--soft contact simulation, ray tracing, and multi-objective Bayesian optimization. Across two silicones, six contact locations, and 10- and 30-mm spherical indenters, the optimized morphologies improve neighboring-location separation relative to variation from re-establishing contact by \(15\)--\(59\%\). Estimating contact location from the optical response using the known LED spacing and combining it with joint torque gives \(1.44~\mathrm{N}\) normal-force MAE over 931 samples. In a two-finger hand, localized side responses appear on several links simultaneously during grasps.

</details>


### [80] [Characterizing Replay Retention Under Dynamics Shift in Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18167)
*Everest Yang,Skye Thompson,George D. Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在持续基于模型的强化学习中，是否保留动力学变化前的回放数据需权衡"变化幅度"与"年龄-陈旧度AUC"两个量：大幅永久变化后遗忘陈旧数据有利，但动力学复现时保留旧数据更有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动力学变化后需从新数据学习且不丢弃可能仍有效的经验。持续基于模型的RL中，变化前的回放会拖慢适应，但直接删除会不必要地减少训练数据，尤其当早期动力学再次出现时代价更高。因此需研究何时近期transition优于完整回放历史，并预测旧数据何时有益或有害。

Method: 用两个量刻画权衡：变化幅度，以及年龄-陈旧度AUC（衡量transition年龄区分陈旧与新鲜数据的能力）。在两种运动形态、两种基于模型的RL算法及Real-World RL基准扰动上进行测试；由于部署机器人上无真值陈旧标签，评估由交互数据构建的估计器能否在永久变化后提供选择回放策略所需的量。

Result: 回放保留策略取决于变化幅度以及动力学的演化方式：大幅永久偏移后遗忘陈旧数据有益；当动力学复现、旧数据重新有用时遗忘则有害。基于交互数据的估计器可在缺乏真值标签时提供选择策略所需量。

Conclusion: 回放策略的选择依赖于对旧数据何时有益或有害的预测，由变化幅度与动力学演化共同决定；在部署场景中可由交互数据的估计器替代真值标签支持该决策。

Abstract: Adapting to changes in robot dynamics requires learning from new data without discarding experience that may still be useful. In continual model-based reinforcement learning (RL), replay collected before a dynamics change can slow adaptation, while removing it unnecessarily reduces available training data and can be especially costly if earlier dynamics return. We study when recent transitions are preferable to the full replay history. Two quantities characterize this trade-off: change magnitude and age-staleness area under the curve (AUC), measuring how well transition age separates stale from fresh data. Forgetting stale data helps after large permanent shifts but hurts when dynamics recur and older data becomes useful again. Choosing a replay strategy therefore depends on predicting when older data will help or hurt. We test these effects across two locomotion morphologies, two model-based RL algorithms, and Real-World RL benchmark perturbations. Because ground-truth staleness labels are unavailable on deployed robots, we evaluate whether an estimator built from interaction data can still provide the quantities needed to choose a replay strategy after permanent changes. Our results show that replay retention depends on change magnitude and on how the dynamics evolve.

</details>


### [81] [Information-Based Trajectory Planning for Spacecraft-to-Spacecraft Tracking and Navigation in Cislunar Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19012)
*Trevor N. Wolf,Brandon A. Jones,Jay W. McMahon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种平衡信息采集与控制消耗的轨迹规划方法，用于提升地月空间航天器间绝对跟踪性能，在DRO光学观测场景下使预期导航与跟踪误差改善近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地月空间活动扩展背景下，需减少对地面测控支持的依赖，发展自主导航与跟踪手段。航天器间跟踪利用相对测量中隐含的非线性动力学信息推断观测器与目标的绝对状态，而二者间几何关系显著影响跟踪性能，因此需通过轨迹设计优化该几何条件。

Method: 将信息增益（信息论指标）纳入最优控制问题，设计观测器轨迹，同时权衡控制消耗与预期航天器间跟踪性能；方法支持多种传感模态的多个低推力观测平台，并可在规划中纳入多个空间目标。

Result: 针对运行于远距离逆行轨道（DRO）的光学观测器，利用信息增益优化后，预期导航与跟踪误差改善近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 验证了面向当前及未来地月任务的信息最优低推力航天器轨迹设计的可行性与价值。

Abstract: We present a trajectory planning method that balances information collection and control effort to improve cislunar spacecraft-to-spacecraft absolute tracking. Expanding use of cislunar space requires alternative navigation and tracking procedures that minimize reliance on ground-based support. Among efforts to address this need, spacecraft-to-spacecraft tracking exploits nonlinear dynamical tracers encoded in a series of relative measurements to infer absolute states of both an observer and a target. The geometry between spacecraft operating under this mode can significantly influence tracking performance. This work considers this geometrical impact by designing observer trajectories that jointly balance control effort and an information-theoretic quantification of the expected spacecraft-to-spacecraft tracking performance. Our methods are designed for multiple low-thrust observation platforms of various sensing modalities and can incorporate multiple space object targets in planning. By leveraging information gain in the optimal control problem, we report almost an order of magnitude improvement in the expected navigation and tracking errors for an optical observer operating in a Distant Retrograde Orbit (DRO). This work demonstrates the feasibility and value of information-optimal low-thrust spacecraft trajectory design for current and upcoming cislunar missions.

</details>


### [82] [Approximating High Dimensional Self-Motion Manifolds via Deep Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18169)
*Haitao Gao,Yang Song,Liao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出概率视角恢复自运动流形（SMM），将其视为给定目标位姿下构型条件后验的支撑集，通过采样与聚类分离连通分量，可处理任意冗余维度，首次近似7R机械臂的4-D SMM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零空间延拓与学习方法均假设SMM为曲线，无法扩展到更高冗余阶数，限制了高冗余机械臂的可行且全局最优运动规划。

Method: 采用概率视角，将SMM视为给定目标位姿下构型条件后验分布的支撑集，恢复SMM转化为从学习到的分布中采样，并通过聚类分离其不相交的连通分量；公式化与流形维度无关，冗余阶数增长时无需改变网络架构。

Result: 近似1-D SMM时性能与最新零空间延拓及学习方法相当；首次实现7R机械臂位置任务中高冗余4-D SMM的近似。

Conclusion: 该概率框架统一处理不同维度SMM，突破曲线假设限制，为高冗余机械臂的运动规划提供了可扩展的流形恢复方案。

Abstract: Self-motion manifold (SMM) characterizes the geometric structure of the infinite inverse kinematic solutions set of a redundant manipulator at a fixed end-effector pose, and its efficient recovery underpins feasible and global optimal motion planning. Existing methods such as null-space continuation and learning-based methods are formulated around the assumption that an SMM is a curve, and do not extend to higher redundancy orders. We instead adopt a probabilistic view: SMMs are the support of the conditional posterior over configurations given a target pose, so that recovering it reduces to sampling from a learned distribution and separating its disjoint components by clustering. The formulation is independent of the manifold dimension and requires no architectural change as the redundancy order grows. In this work, we demonstrate that our method can approximate 1-D SMMs with performance comparable to the latest null-space continuation and learning-based approach, and that it is the first method capable of approximating highly redundant 4-D SMMs in a 7R manipulator for position tasks. Project website: \href{https://github.com/accuracy-maker/high-dimenstional-self-motion-manifold-approximation}{https://github.com/accuracy-maker/high-dimenstional-self-motion-manifold-approximation}

</details>


### [83] [ElastiQP: An Always-Feasible QP Solver for Constrained Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19080)
*Daniel Morton,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ElastiQP，一种改进的对偶主动集QP求解器，通过对每条不等式约束施加精确的逐约束l1罚项实现软约束松弛，同时保持等式（动力学）约束为硬约束；在机器人控制基准上达到微秒级性能，并在不可行问题上优于现有求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人能力增强，基于二次规划（QP）的控制器需纳入越来越多的约束以保证安全可靠运行。每增加一条约束都会提高出现瞬时冲突的概率；当QP求解器返回"不可行"状态时，控制器将无可行解可执行，因此需要一种能在不可行情况下仍给出可用解的求解器。

Method: 提出ElastiQP，一种改进的对偶主动集（dual active-set）QP求解器。对每条不等式约束施加精确的逐约束l1罚项以松弛约束，同时保持等式约束（动力学约束）为硬约束。关键技巧在于将松弛变量解析地折叠进求解器，从而保持压缩线性系统规模不变。

Result: 在机器人控制基准测试中，ElastiQP达到微秒级性能，在可行问题上匹配或优于主流现代求解器；在不可行问题上能优雅处理，将违反量严格限制在冲突的不等式项上，返回可用解的速度比最优替代求解器快至多40倍。

Conclusion: ElastiQP为约束日益增多的机器人控制器提供了一种高效且鲁棒的QP求解方案。该工作以开源C++ header-only库形式发布，并提供Python与JAX接口，地址为https://github.com/StanfordASL/elastiqp。

Abstract: As robot capabilities increase, quadratic programming (QP)-based controllers must account for a similarly increasing number of constraints to ensure safe, reliable operation. Yet, with each added constraint, this introduces more chances of momentary conflict: in which case, a QP solver that returns an "infeasible" status leaves the controller with nothing to execute. To address this, we introduce ElastiQP, a modified dual active-set QP solver that relaxes every inequality constraint with an exact, per-constraint l1 penalty while keeping equality constraints (dynamics) hard. Notably, ElastiQP does so by folding the slack variables into the solver analytically, maintaining a constant size of the condensed linear system. On a suite of robot control benchmarks, ElastiQP achieves microsecond-level performance, matching or outperforming leading modern solvers on feasible problems. On infeasible problems, ElastiQP handles these gracefully, confining violations to strictly the conflicting inequality terms, returning a usable solution up to 40x faster than the best alternative solvers. ElastiQP is available as an open-source C++ header-only library, with Python and JAX interfaces, at https://github.com/StanfordASL/elastiqp.

</details>


### [84] [TacBPM: A Tactile-conditioned Behavior Prior Model for Dexterous Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18174)
*Jie Yin,Wanli Xing,Zeyuan Zhao,Xuezhou Zhu,Zhijie Deng,Kaifeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种触觉条件化的行为先验模型，将多尺度球形专家蒸馏进潜在控制器，下游策略通过残差潜在动作复用固定先验，加速灵巧手重定向训练并实现稳定策略及sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手内操作需要协调高自由度关节，通过间歇性、富接触的交互完成；除目标朝向跟踪外，策略还须发现能保持物体稳定的手指步态，并适应几何、各向异性、位姿、接触与传感变化，现有从原始关节命令探索的方法效率低、易失败。

Method: 提出触觉条件化的行为先验模型：将多尺度球形专家策略蒸馏为潜在控制器，下游策略通过残差潜在动作复用该固定的触觉先验，从而减少从原始关节命令重新探索的成本；先验以触觉-本体感知历史为条件，使潜在行为反映当前手-物交互状态。

Result: 在各项异性物体的任意位姿迁移、指定轴旋转，以及臂-手Grasp-to-AnyPose任务（应对新工具几何与泛化放置的抓取、抬起、搬运与到达目标位姿）上评估；实验表明该方法加快训练，在匹配的原始动作PPO接近失败处仍能获得稳定策略，并成功实现手内与臂-手任务的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 触觉条件化的行为先验与残差潜在动作机制可有效提升灵巧重定向策略的训练效率与稳定性，并具备向真实机器人迁移的能力。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires policies that coordinate high-DoF hand joints through intermittent, contact-rich interaction. Beyond target-orientation tracking, such policies must discover finger gaits that preserve object stability while adapting to geometry, anisotropy, pose, contact, and sensing changes. We propose \method, a tactile-conditioned behavior prior model for dexterous reorientation. \method distills multi-scale sphere specialists into a latent controller and lets downstream policies reuse the fixed tactile prior through residual latent actions, reducing renewed exploration from raw joint commands. The prior conditions on tactile-proprioceptive history so latent behavior reflects the current hand-object interaction. We evaluate arbitrary-pose transfer across anisotropic objects, commanded-axis rotation, and an arm-hand Grasp-to-AnyPose task in which the robot must grasp, lift, transport, and reach goal poses for novel tool geometries and generalized placements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method accelerates training and enables stable policies where matched raw-action PPO remains near failure, with successful sim-to-real transfer in in-hand and arm-hand tasks.

</details>


### [85] [OmniRisk: Omnidirectional Trajectory-Risk Learning for Agile Quadrotor Dynamic Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18191)
*Yifan He,Yang Liu,Wenhao Zhao,Hai Lin,Deping Zhang,Mingze Ma,Fei Gao,Huan Yu,Zipeng Dai,Ziming Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniRisk：一种离线学习轨迹级风险的全向规划框架，实现四旋翼对高速移动障碍物的高效机载规避。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷四旋翼规避快速移动障碍物需要在极短反应窗口内预测碰撞并选择可行机动。现有方法存在两大痛点：稀疏测距观测无法直接揭示障碍物运动；在线轨迹优化要么随障碍物数量扩展性差，要么在密集高速场景中以牺牲可靠性换取效率。

Method: 提出OmniRisk全向规划框架，离线学习轨迹级风险用于高效机载逃逸。1）用固定维度张量联合表示几何与运动，融合LiDAR距离全景图、动态掩码与笛卡尔表面速度。2）构建与障碍物速度对齐的非对称风险场，强化接近交互、衰减远离交互，并沿预测相对轨迹累积风险以提供稠密监督、抑制障碍通过后的不必要犹豫。3）采用双分支循环卷积网络，在全向锚点格上单次前向传播预测候选基元的终端边界状态与动态风险，随后进行选择与闭式重建。该设计消除在线沿轨迹风险累积，使风险推断成本与障碍物数量无关。

Result: 实现高效机载规避；真实飞行实验在相对遭遇速度高达15 m/s下完成连续规避机动，且无需微调。

Conclusion: OmniRisk通过离线学习轨迹级风险与固定维度多模态张量表示，实现风险推断成本与障碍物数量解耦，兼顾效率与可靠性，适用于密集高速遭遇场景，代码已开源。

Abstract: Agile quadrotor avoidance of fast-moving obstacles requires anticipating collisions and selecting feasible maneuvers within short reaction windows. Reliable predictive avoidance remains challenging because sparse range observations do not directly reveal obstacle motion, while online trajectory optimizers either scale poorly with obstacle count or remain efficient at the expense of reliability in dense, high-speed encounters. We present OmniRisk, an omnidirectional planning framework that learns trajectory-level risk offline for efficient onboard evasion. A fixed-dimensional tensor combines LiDAR range panoramas, dynamic masks, and Cartesian surface velocities to represent geometry and motion jointly. We formulate an asymmetric risk field aligned with obstacle velocity that emphasizes approaching interactions and attenuates receding ones. Accumulating this risk along predicted relative trajectories provides dense supervision and discourages unnecessary hesitation after obstacles pass. A dual-branch circular convolutional network predicts terminal boundary states and dynamic risks for candidate primitives over an omnidirectional anchor lattice in a single forward pass, followed by selection and closed-form reconstruction of the selected candidate primitive. This formulation removes online risk accumulation along trajectories and makes risk-inference cost independent of obstacle count. OmniRisk enables efficient onboard avoidance, with real-world flights demonstrating consecutive evasive maneuvers at relative encounter speeds up to 15 m/s without fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/VANdexj/OmniRisk.

</details>


### [86] [WAVE-Go: World-Model Navigation with Adaptive Execution for Wheel-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18193)
*Mingyi Li,Ji Li,Zhihao Ouyang,Yage He,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WAVE-Go图像目标导航框架，将世界-动作预测与可中断命令执行解耦，实现自适应可中断执行，在导航成功率、碰撞率与重规划开销间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型能预测导航动作后果，但预测的动作序列在执行中可能失效，尤其轮腿机器人在遇到动态障碍或切换运动模式时。需要在保证性能的同时降低规划开销。

Method: WAVE-Go将世界-动作预测与可中断命令执行分离：执行器自适应选择动作前缀，并在新观测使执行失效时取消待执行命令；采用条件风险建模，在估计的累计失败预算下选择前缀；姿态与运动模式切换需通过间隙、稳定性和任务证据检查。

Result: 同分布成功率74.1%，动态分布外成功率63.3%，分别超出最强基线4.7和7.7个百分点；碰撞率从4.4降至2.9次/100米。相比可中断固定四条命令执行，成功率提升4.0个百分点，重规划频率降低51.2%，碰撞率降低6.5%。消融显示运行时中断可提升成功率、碰撞率和反应延迟，代价是额外重规划。

Conclusion: 自适应可中断执行能在导航性能与规划开销之间实现平衡，验证了该执行范式的有效性。

Abstract: World models can anticipate the consequences of navigation actions, but predicted action sequences may become invalid during execution, especially when wheel-legged robots encounter dynamic obstacles or change locomotion modes. We propose WAVE-Go, an image-goal navigation framework that separates world-action prediction from interruptible command execution. Its executor adaptively selects an action prefix and cancels pending commands when updated observations invalidate execution. A conditional-risk formulation specifies prefix selection under an estimated cumulative failure budget, while posture and locomotion-mode transitions require clearance, stability, and task-evidence checks. In the reported navigation evaluation, WAVE-Go achieves 74.1% in-distribution success and 63.3% dynamic out-of-distribution success, exceeding the strongest baseline by 4.7 and 7.7 percentage points, respectively, while reducing collisions from 4.4 to 2.9 per 100 m. Compared with interruptible fixed four-command execution, WAVE-Go raises success by 4.0 percentage points while reducing replanning frequency by 51.2% and collision rate by 6.5%. Execution ablations also show that runtime interruption improves success, collision rate, and reaction latency at the cost of additional replanning. These results support adaptive, interruptible execution as a means of balancing navigation performance and planning overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/vigorlee/wave-go.

</details>


### [87] [WholeBodyWAM: Learning Whole-Body World Action Models with Scalable Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18197)
*Bowei Zhang,Qiyao Zhang,Shuanghao Bai,Xinhua Wang,Meng Li,Yilei Wang,Leiwang Zhang,Jian Tang,Lu Zhou,Lei Sun,Zhengping Che*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WholeBodyWAM世界-动作模型，利用大规模异构人体/人形运动数据预训练运动先验，再通过非对称MoT注意力与视觉、动作专家融合，实现人形机器人全身操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形全身操作需要协调的全身动力学，但目标机器人上的大规模轨迹采集成本高、难以规模化；而人类视频、原生3D运动数据及异构人形平台的全身运动资源丰富，却因具身差异无法直接作为目标机器人的动作使用。核心问题是：这些可扩展的运动资源能否作为可迁移的预测先验，服务于人形世界-动作建模。

Method: 1) 构建UniMotion-4K语料，覆盖人类视频、原生3D运动数据集与异构人形平台，时长超4K小时，并归一化到统一运动空间；2) 预训练语言条件Motion Expert，在无目标机器人动作监督下预测未来全身运动；3) 机器人后训练阶段，通过非对称Mixture-of-Transformers(MoT)注意力将预训练Motion Expert与Video、Action Expert集成，使预测性场景动力学与全身运动共同指导具身特定动作生成。

Result: 实验表明：WholeBodyWAM随运动预训练规模增大而持续受益；未来运动预测与下游任务性能均提升；可有效迁移至真实世界人形操作；预训练运动先验在目标机器人示范数据有限时显著提升数据效率。

Conclusion: 异构人体/人形运动数据可作为可迁移的预测先验，为人形世界-动作模型提供有效的预训练信号，缓解目标机器人数据稀缺问题，并支撑真实场景下的全身操作。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body manipulation requires coordinated whole-body dynamics, yet large-scale trajectories from a target robot are expensive to collect and difficult to scale. In contrast, whole-body motion from human and humanoid sources is abundantly available, although such data cannot be directly used as embodiment-specific robot actions. This work asks whether these scalable motion resources can instead provide a transferable predictive prior for humanoid world-action modeling. We introduce WholeBodyWAM, a humanoid world-action model that learns whole-body dynamics from large-scale heterogeneous motion before target-robot training. We curate UniMotion-4K, a motion corpus spanning more than 4K hours from human videos, native 3D motion datasets, and heterogeneous humanoid platforms, and canonicalize these diverse sources into a unified motion space. A language-conditioned Motion Expert is then pretrained to predict future whole-body motion without target-robot action supervision. During robot post-training, the pretrained Motion Expert is integrated with Video and Action Experts through asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention, enabling predictive scene dynamics and whole-body motion to jointly inform embodiment-specific action generation. Experiments show that WholeBodyWAM consistently benefits from increased motion-pretraining scale, improves future-motion prediction and downstream task performance, and transfers effectively to real-world humanoid manipulation. Moreover, the pretrained motion prior substantially improves data efficiency under limited target-robot demonstrations.

</details>


### [88] [Reinforcement Learning for Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18207)
*Perry Dong,Kuo-Han Hung,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Real-Time EXPO-FT：将慢速 VLA 动作块生成与轻量级快速动作编辑解耦，实现满足实时控制要求的强化学习微调，显著缓解推理延迟导致的状态陈旧问题，在仿真与真实动态任务上均取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练 VLA 模型推理延迟高，动作选择所用的观测在执行时已陈旧，造成分布偏移，严重降低可靠性与性能。现有异步策略执行方法多基于模仿学习，缺乏超越训练分布、提升可靠性的机制。

Method: 基于 EXPO-FT 框架，将慢速、高表达力的动作生成与快速、反应式的动作编辑解耦：大型预训练 VLA 利用行为先验提出动作块，轻量级编辑策略以最新观测为条件，对动作进行快速反应式修改。该实例化称为 Real-Time EXPO-FT，用于微调实时 VLA 策略的强化学习框架。

Result: 在 Kinetix 基准上，Real-Time EXPO-FT 使延迟策略在 10/10 环境中优于延迟与非延迟方法。在四项真实动态任务（机器人传物、平衡球、桌上足球踢球、动态抓取）中，在线机器人数据限制为 10 分钟，平均策略性能从 42% 提升至 97%，且无需人工干预。

Conclusion: Real-Time EXPO-FT 可在实时控制约束下进行样本高效的强化学习微调，快速适应具有挑战性的真实世界动态，表明解耦慢速生成与快速编辑是缓解 VLA 延迟问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning fine-tuning on top of large, pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offers promise for highly reliable robot deployment. However, because of their scale, modern VLA models suffer from high inference latency, so the observation used to select an action is often stale by execution time, creating a distribution shift that can substantially degrade reliability and performance. Prior work has explored asynchronous policy execution to reduce the effect of latency, but these methods are mostly built on imitation learning and offer no mechanism for moving beyond the training distribution toward higher reliability. We close this gap by enabling RL fine-tuning that meets the real-time control requirements of dynamic real-world manipulation. Our approach builds on EXPO-FT, a framework for sample-efficient, reliable VLA fine-tuning with reinforcement learning, and decouples slow, expressive action generation from fast, reactive action edits: a large pretrained VLA proposes action chunks using its strong behavior prior, while a lightweight edit policy performs fast, reactive decision-making by editing actions in response to changes in state, conditioned on the latest observation. We instantiate this as Real-Time EXPO-FT, an RL framework for finetuning real-time VLA policies. On the Kinetix benchmark, Real-Time EXPO-FT enables a delayed policy to achieve the best performance among delayed and non-delayed methods in 10 out of 10 environments. On four dynamic real-world tasks, robot object passing, ball balancing, table soccer kicking, and dynamic object picking, with online robot data capped at 10 minutes, Real-Time EXPO-FT improves average policy performance from 42% to 97%, all without human intervention, demonstrating rapid, sample-efficient adaptation to challenging real-world dynamics. Website: https://pd-perry.github.io/real-time-expo-ft

</details>


### [89] [CANTABILE: Learning Expressive Dynamics for Robotic Piano Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18213)
*Woosik Kim,Wonhyeok Choi,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CANTABILE 动力学感知框架，通过在策略中引入力度目标与速度保真奖励，将机器人钢琴演奏的评价从单纯的音准扩展到力度动态，显著提升表现力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人钢琴演奏基准几乎仅以音高（按键）与起始时刻（onset）衡量进度，忽略了表现力所需的力度动态，导致动态表现既无奖励也无评估。

Method: CANTABILE 包含三点：1）闭合乐谱到触键的闭环，以未来力度目标为条件，并将按键在 onset 时的角速度映射回 MIDI 力度；2）耦合力度保真奖励与 onset 覆盖奖励，防止通过省略难音符来提升力度；3）以 alpha 缩放、仅手指的残差微调冻结的力度感知基础策略，将击键强度适配与常规音符执行解耦。在 RoboPianist 的 51 首动态丰富子集 EXPRESSIVE-51 上评估。

Result: Velocity F1（在 ±8 MIDI 力度容差内联合衡量音高、onset 与强度）从基线的 0.06 提升至 0.34；51 首歌曲全部改善；匹配音符力度误差减少一半以上；与参考音频的 log-mel 距离降低 8%。强度随机化训练还支持运行时控制演奏强度而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: CANTABILE 证明在机器人钢琴演奏中显式建模与奖励力度动态可大幅提升表现力，并可实现灵活的运行时可调性。

Abstract: Robotic piano playing has emerged as a standard benchmark for dexterous bimanual manipulation, yet progress on it has been measured almost entirely by note accuracy -- which keys are pressed (pitch) and when (onset) -- leaving the musical dynamics essential for expressive performance neither rewarded nor evaluated. We propose CANTABILE, a dynamics-aware framework for robotic piano performance that (i) closes the score-to-contact loop by conditioning the policy on upcoming velocity goals and mapping each key's angular velocity at onset back to MIDI velocity, (ii) couples a velocity-fidelity reward with an onset-coverage reward, so that dynamics cannot be improved by omitting difficult notes, and (iii) refines a frozen dynamics-aware base policy with an alpha-scaled, finger-only residual that localizes strike-intensity adaptation away from nominal note execution. On EXPRESSIVE-51, a dynamics-rich 51-song subset of RoboPianist, CANTABILE raises Velocity F1 -- jointly measuring pitch, onset, and intensity within a +/-8 MIDI-velocity tolerance -- from 0.06 to 0.34 over the RoboPianist baseline, improves all 51 songs, more than halves matched-note velocity error, and reduces log-mel distance to reference audio by 8%. Intensity-randomized training further enables runtime control of performance intensity without retraining.

</details>


### [90] [UMI-Bridge: Action-Anchored Latent Alignment across Human and Robot Manipulation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18232)
*Haiyi Liu,Jingming Ma,Ke Rui,Yuteng Wei,Yuan Ma,Yushen Zuo,Honglong Tian,Haoran Jia,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Shiyi Chen,Haiyan Mao,Jiaqi Zhang,Chun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMI-Bridge通过UMI作为中间域，以动作等价而非像素相似对齐表示，实现数据高效的机器人学习与UMI到机器人的任务迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人演示数据有限，促使研究者使用无机器人采集的人类操作数据（如第一人称视频与手持UMI演示）。但视角、具身形态与动作监督的差异，使跨数据源按操作动作而非视觉外观对齐表示变得困难。

Method: 以UMI作为中间域按动作等价对齐表示。UMI动作监督将潜在表示锚定到末端执行器运动与夹爪行为；同步的头-腕观测与配对的ego-UMI片段支持跨视角和跨域对齐。先在没有机器人演示的人类操作数据上训练双视角潜在动作模型（LAM），再冻结其腕部教师与动力学模型，用于正则化在UMI与机器人数据上的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）后训练。共享腕部接口使该训练期监督跨两个域生效，同时保留策略的标准推理架构。

Result: 在三个真实机器人任务上，UMI-Bridge平均成功率达91.7%，而使用匹配UMI与机器人数据的朴素协同训练为73.3%。在两个数据效率任务上，仅用25%机器人演示加UMI数据即超过全量数据的仅机器人基线。在两个额外任务上，仅从UMI演示（无任务特定机器人演示）学习，分别达到85%与90%成功率。

Conclusion: 结果支持动作锚定的潜在对齐用于数据高效机器人学习，以及UMI到机器人的任务迁移。

Abstract: Real-robot demonstrations are limited, motivating the use of human manipulation data collected without robots, including egocentric videos and handheld Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) demonstrations. However, differences in viewpoint, embodiment, and available action supervision make it difficult to align representations across these sources according to manipulation motion rather than visual appearance. We introduce UMI-Bridge, which uses UMI as an intermediate domain to align representations according to action equivalence rather than pixel similarity. UMI action supervision anchors the latent representation to end-effector motion and gripper behavior, while synchronized head-wrist observations and paired ego-UMI clips support alignment across views and domains. We train a dual-view latent action model (LAM) on human manipulation data without robot demonstrations, then freeze its wrist teacher and dynamics model to regularize vision-language-action (VLA) post-training on UMI and robot data. The shared wrist interface enables this training-time supervision across both domains while preserving the policy's standard inference architecture. Across three real-robot tasks, UMI-Bridge achieves 91.7% mean success versus 73.3% for Naive Co-training with matched UMI and robot data. On two data-efficiency tasks, it surpasses a full-data Robot-only baseline using 25% of the robot demonstrations together with UMI data. It also achieves 85% and 90% success on two additional tasks learned from UMI demonstrations without task-specific robot demonstrations. These results support action-anchored latent alignment for data-efficient robot learning and UMI-to-robot task transfer.

</details>


### [91] [ForceDelta-VLA: Distilling Force-Conditioned ActionCorrections for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18242)
*Ju Dong,Yu Fu,Jian Chen,Yimeng Liu,Haocheng Zhao,Lei Zhang,Kaixin Bai,Liding Zhang,Diwen Zheng,Alois Christian Knoll,Angela P. Schoellig,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ForceDelta-VLA 修正蒸馏框架，将力感知 VLA 策略的动作预测分解为可复用的参考动作与力/延迟修正，用轻量策略在执行中依力历史调整，无需重新生成完整动作块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力感知 VLA 策略在多接触操作中有效，但通常把任务级运动与依赖接触的调整耦合在单次动作预测中；而示范数据缺乏显式标签，无法将预测分解为可复用的参考动作与修正量。

Method: 以冻结教师的力条件模式与力无关模式成对预测，构造显式的力修正目标；另设延迟修正目标以补偿参考动作失配及参考状态变化。训练采用异步调度回放，并在执行时利用缓存的任务上下文。所得轻量策略基于近期力历史与机器人状态调整参考动作，可在两次参考动作更新之间响应接触变化，而不重新生成完整动作块。

Result: 在九个单臂与双臂接触密集型任务上，平均成功率 82.2%，对比 ForceVLA 基线 54.4%；直接执行 Stage-1 Temporal Teacher 为 70.6%。相较 ForceVLA，完整系统在两个平台上将成功试验的平均峰值接触力降低约 26%。

Conclusion: 通过显式力修正蒸馏与延迟修正目标，可把接触依赖调整从动作块生成中解耦，以轻量修正策略在保持参考动作复用的同时显著提升接触密集型任务成功率并降低接触力。

Abstract: Force-aware Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies improve contact-rich manipulation, but typically combine task-level motion and contact-dependent adjustment in a single action prediction. Demonstrations provide no explicit labels for decomposing that prediction into a reusable reference action and a correction. We present ForceDelta-VLA, a correction-distillation framework that constructs an explicit force-correction target using paired predictions from a frozen teacher's force-conditioned and learned force-agnostic modes. A separate delay-correction target accounts for reference-action mismatch and the change in reference state. Training uses asynchronous schedule replay with the cached task context available during execution. The resulting lightweight policy adjusts the reference actions using recent force history and robot state, responding to contact changes between reference-action updates without regenerating complete action chunks. Across nine single-arm and bimanual contact-rich tasks, ForceDelta-VLA achieves an 82.2% mean success rate, compared with 54.4% for the original ForceVLA baseline. Direct execution of our Stage-1 Temporal Teacher achieves 70.6%. Relative to ForceVLA, the complete system reduces mean peak contact force over successful trials by approximately 26% on both platforms.

</details>


### [92] [Acting in Meters: Learning Metric Interactions for Precise Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18243)
*Lijie Wang,Zheng Lu,Yiming Wang,Heyang Yu,Kenghou Hoi,Bowen Hu,Di Cui,Tianyu Xin,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Xingjie Fan,Yizhao Xu,Ziliang Wang,Fei Gao,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种度量交互框架，在共享度量尺度下建模物体级与场景级交互，通过两阶段自适应提升VLA与WAM基线在仿真与真实任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision-Language-Action模型与世界-动作模型虽推进了语言条件下的机器人操作，但动作、物体与场景几何之间的度量关系往往被隐式处理。人类操作则结合了对任务相关物体的语义理解与指导手部相对物体及其环境运动的空间反馈。

Method: 框架在物理笛卡尔空间中以共享度量尺度建模交互。物体级：Interaction-Centric Tokens（ICTs）显式表示末端执行器相对被操作物体的位姿轨迹，并与动作联合去噪，提供有物理基础的交互监督。场景级：Metric Action Interaction Field（MAIF）利用动作与ICT查询对度量场景点云特征进行注意力计算，学习几何条件化的动作修正。整体通过两阶段自适应集成到现有基线中。

Result: 以少量额外参数与训练步数改进多种VLA与WAM基线。在LIBERO与RoboTwin 2.0上平均成功率分别提升0.80与3.59个百分点；真实世界任务提升6.80个百分点，其分布外变体提升7.45个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式建模物体级与场景级的度量交互，可作为轻量模块有效增强现有VLA/WAM模型的物理交互能力与泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models and World-Action Models have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation, yet often leave metric relations among actions, objects, and scene geometry implicit. Human manipulation combines semantic understanding of task-relevant objects with spatial feedback that guides hand motion relative to objects and their surroundings. Inspired by this, we introduce a metric interaction framework that models object-level and scene-level interactions in physical Cartesian space at a shared metric scale. At the object level, Interaction-Centric Tokens (ICTs) explicitly represent end-effector pose trajectories relative to manipulated objects and are jointly denoised with actions, providing physically grounded interaction supervision. At the scene level, the Metric Action Interaction Field (MAIF) uses action and ICT queries to attend to metric scene point-cloud features and learns geometry-conditioned action corrections. Through two-stage adaptation, our framework improves diverse VLA and WAM baselines with a small number of additional parameters and training steps. Experiments demonstrate average success-rate gains of 0.80 and 3.59 percentage points on LIBERO and RoboTwin~2.0, respectively, alongside gains of 6.80 percentage points on real-world tasks and 7.45 percentage points on their out-of-distribution variants.

</details>


### [93] [A3P5 NEMESIS Integrated Rover Design for Environmental Reconnaissance and Robotic Sampling with Reproducible Mobility Analysis and an External Data Machine Learning Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18245)
*Shafi Bin Sultan,Sabik Bin Sultan,Safwan Sadad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出A3P5 NEMESIS四轮漫游车平台的设计研究，包含照片约束几何重建、子系统架构与可复现分析评估框架，并明确区分原型证据与拟议功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将远程巡检、环境观测与轻量操作集成于单一可服务平台上，并建立一个可追溯的工程设计研究与评估框架，明确原型集成现场性能尚待验证。

Method: 探索性检索10个查询共5,000条书目记录，去重得4,897条DOI记录与1,212条元数据候选，据此形成设计依据；进行照片约束几何重建以恢复构型（碳纹外壳、独立转向轮组、折叠机械臂、倾斜相机桅杆、侧向采样设备）；建立静力学模型计算扭矩与前倾边界；开展公开数据标定基准（7,344条合格小时观测、8个传感器/环境预测变量、按时间划分训练/验证/测试集，验证集选择岭回归）。

Result: 按24 kg场景在20度坡道等载荷下预测每轮变速箱输出扭矩3.28 N·m；2 kg前向载荷使几何前倾界限由38.1度降至32.7度（设计筛查值，非实测极限）。标定基准中岭回归留出集CO均方根误差0.502 mg/m³，95%日块自助法区间0.435–0.569 mg/m³（属外部传感器阵列结果，不能证明NEMESIS精度）。

Conclusion: 研究识别了优先测量项、拟议控制接口与任务特定验证需求；贡献在于面向原型机的可追溯工程设计研究与评估框架，其集成现场性能仍待确立。

Abstract: A3P5 NEMESIS is a four-wheel rover intended to combine remote inspection, environmental observation and lightweight manipulation within one serviceable platform. This study develops a photo-constrained geometric reconstruction, a subsystem architecture and a reproducible analytical assessment while distinguishing physical prototype evidence from proposed functions. An exploratory search retrieved 5,000 bibliographic records across ten queries, yielding 4,897 distinct DOI records and 1,212 metadata candidates; selected primary studies and technical documents informed the design. The reconstructed configuration retains the carbon-pattern enclosure, independently steered wheel assemblies, folded manipulator, inclined camera mast and side sampling equipment. A declared 24 kg scenario predicts 3.28 newton-metres of gearbox-output torque per wheel on a 20-degree grade under equal load sharing; a separate static model shows how a 2 kg forward payload reduces the geometric front-tipping bound from 38.1 degrees to 32.7 degrees. These are design screens, not measured operating limits. A public-data calibration benchmark uses 7,344 eligible hourly observations, eight sensor/environmental predictors and chronological training, validation and test partitions. Validation-selected ridge regression achieves a held-out CO root-mean-square error of 0.502 milligrams per cubic metre, with a 95% daily-block bootstrap interval of 0.435-0.569 milligrams per cubic metre. This result concerns an external sensor array and cannot establish NEMESIS accuracy. The combined analysis identifies priority measurements, proposed control interfaces and mission-specific validation requirements. The contribution is a traceable engineering design study and evaluation framework for a prototype whose integrated field performance remains to be established.

</details>


### [94] [${M}^2$Tok: Multi-head Multi-codebook Discrete Action Tokenization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18259)
*Chunpu Xu,Zhixuan Liang,Yuhao Zhang,Chi-Min Chan,Jessie Wang,Yang Xiao,Mengkang Hu,Xiaokang Yang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出M²Tok——一种多头多码本的动作分词器，通过特征分解与独立码本量化降低重构误差，从而提升VLA模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型已扩展至多模态信号处理，但原始动作信号是连续的，需要有效分词将高维输入映射为紧凑离散token。现有离散动作分词器存在高重构损失问题，无法保留精确控制所需的细粒度动态，"离散化瓶颈"显著限制了VLA模型的性能上限。

Method: M²Tok采用两项关键结构创新：(1) 将隐动作特征分解为多个头，使模型隐式地将特定头与不同动作维度对齐；(2) 为每个头分配独立码本进行量化。利用多码本的组合特性显著扩展分词器的表示能力。

Result: 在RoboTwin、Simpler-Env及3个零样本真实世界任务上评估M²Tok-based VLA，实验结果表明该方法不仅实现更优的重构保真度，还显著提升了VLA模型的成功率。消融实验进一步验证了多头和多码本机制的有效性。

Conclusion: M²Tok通过多头多码本设计有效缓解离散化瓶颈，在降低重构损失的同时提升下游VLA策略性能，为连续动作信号的离散化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements have successfully adapted autoregressive language models to process multimodal signals, such as images and actions. Since raw action signals are continuous, effective tokenization is essential to map high-dimensional inputs into compact discrete tokens for autoregressive processing. However, existing discrete action tokenizers often suffer from high reconstruction loss, failing to preserve the fine-grained dynamics required for precise control. This ``discretization bottleneck'' significantly limits the performance ceiling of downstream Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. To address this, we propose $\mathcal{M}^2$Tok, a Multi-head Multi-codebook Action Tokenizer designed to minimize reconstruction error and enhance policy performance. Our approach introduces two key structural innovations: (1) we decompose the latent action features into multiple heads, enabling the model to implicitly align specific heads with distinct action dimensions; (2) we assign independent codebooks to each head for quantization. By leveraging the combinatorial nature of multiple codebooks, we significantly expand the representational expressivity of the tokenizer, leading to substantially lower reconstruction loss compared to previous methods. We evaluate the $\mathcal{M}^2$Tok-based VLA on the RoboTwin, Simpler-Env, and 3 zero-shot real-world tasks. Experimental results demonstrate our method not only achieves superior reconstruction fidelity but also significantly boosts the success rate of VLA models. Comprehensive ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of the multi-head and multi-codebook mechanisms. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/cpaaax/M2Tok}{https://github.com/cpaaax/M2Tok}.

</details>


### [95] [Function-Preserving Data Generation for Zero-Shot Real-to-Sim-to-Real Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18293)
*Tianyi Xiang,Xupeng Xie,Jiahang Cao,Andrew F. Luo,Haoang Li,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种保功能的 Real-to-Sim-to-Real 框架，通过约束引导的网格变形生成几何多样且物理有效的合成演示，实现接触丰富任务的零样本策略部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据生成可在不采集大规模真实数据的前提下扩展机器人学习，但为接触丰富任务生成既几何多样又物理有效的数据仍具挑战。成功往往依赖精确的几何接口，而标准形状增强方法会扭曲任务关键接口，导致配合失配或相互穿透等无效接触关系，使下游交互不可行。

Method: 提出保功能的 Real-to-Sim-to-Real 框架，无需遥操作源轨迹即可从重建资产生成合成演示。该方法通过约束引导的网格变形增强任务相关物体几何，并配合任务位姿与碰撞代理的物理一致迁移；在仿真推演中进一步施加视觉域随机化。

Result: 在真实世界与仿真环境的广泛实验表明，该方法在接触丰富及长时程任务中对未见物体几何与多样视觉条件具备稳健泛化能力，并支持零样本策略部署，无需真实世界微调。

Conclusion: 该工作为通过形状变形实现接触丰富任务的可扩展机器人学习提供了一条切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Robotic data generation is a promising paradigm for scaling robot learning without collecting large-scale real-world data. However, generating geometrically diverse yet physically valid data for contact-rich tasks remains challenging, especially when success depends on precise geometric interfaces. Standard shape augmentation methods often distort task-critical interfaces, resulting in invalid contact relationships, e.g., fit mismatches or interpenetration, rendering downstream interactions infeasible. To address these limitations, we propose a function-preserving Real-to-Sim-to-Real framework that generates synthetic demonstrations from reconstructed assets without teleoperated source trajectories. Our method augments task-relevant object geometries through constraint-guided mesh deformation, together with physically consistent transfer of task poses and collision proxies. Visual domain randomization is further applied during simulation rollouts, enabling robust zero-shot policy deployment without real-world fine-tuning. Extensive experiments in both real-world and simulation settings demonstrate that our method enables robust generalization across unseen object geometries and diverse visual conditions in contact-rich and long-horizon tasks. Our method provides a practical path toward scalable robot learning for contact-rich tasks via shape deformation.

</details>


### [96] [RAFAIL: Relationship-Aware Failure Detection for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18324)
*Loris Schneider,Edgar Welte,Rania Rayyes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAFAIL是一个无需失败样本的机器人操作执行失败检测框架，通过对任务相关实体关系进行OOD检测实现失效识别，在三项真实任务上取得73.4%的平衡准确率，优于OOD与不确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作的可靠执行需要在线失败检测。现有方法存在两类局限：视觉语言模型（VLM）虽能语义判断任务结果，但引入运行时计算开销；而常规OOD检测器易对无害的场景变化产生响应，而非真正的与失败相关的偏差。

Method: RAFAIL将失败检测聚焦于任务相关实体之间的关系异常（如夹爪与物体、物体与其目标位置）。离线阶段，用VLM对成功示范标注任务进度与关系重要性，据此学习基于点云的关系表征，且不依赖策略内部特征。运行时用关系特定的OOD检测器评估这些表征，同时在不进行VLM推理的情况下预测关系重要性与任务进度。

Result: 在三个真实世界机器人操作任务上，RAFAIL达到73.4%的平衡准确率，优于所评估的最强OOD基线与不确定性基线。

Conclusion: 通过将OOD检测限定在任务相关关系上，RAFAIL在无需任何失败数据、且不在运行时调用VLM的条件下，实现了对任务无关场景变化更鲁棒、且更高效的执行失败检测。

Abstract: Detecting failures during execution is essential for reliable robotic manipulation. Vision-language models (VLMs) can assess task outcomes semantically but add runtime computation, whereas out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors may respond to harmless scene variations rather than failure-relevant deviations. We introduce RAFAIL, a framework for detecting execution failures during robotic manipulation. RAFAIL identifies failures by detecting anomalies in task-relevant relationships between entities, such as a gripper and an object or an object and its target. By focusing OOD detection on relevant parts of the observation, RAFAIL reduces sensitivity to task-irrelevant scene variation. Offline, a VLM annotates successful demonstrations with task progress and relationship importance, which are used to learn point-cloud-based relationship representations without relying on policy-internal features. At runtime, relationship-specific OOD detectors evaluate these representations while relationship importance and task progress are predicted without VLM inference. RAFAIL requires no failure data and achieves 73.4% balanced accuracy across three real-world robotic manipulation tasks, outperforming the strongest evaluated OOD- and uncertainty-based baselines.

</details>


### [97] [UAVs Meet Embodied Intelligence: Bridging Human Intents and Flying Dynamics Via Harnessing Physical-Digital AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18326)
*Yonglin Tian,Weiyi Wang,Houhua Lu,Xinyi Li,Yihao Wu,Jingyang Chen,Jianli Sun,Chengxiang Li,Yinuo Chen,Fei Lin,Tengchao Zhang,Jing Yang,Deyi Ji,Jian Di,Naiqi Wu,Yisheng Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述将无人机自主性从任务专用感知控制向具身智能范式转变，提出"5+5"框架统一刻画UAV EI与EI UAV，系统梳理关键进展并指出核心挑战与系统级发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机将具身智能延伸至连续三维空间，感知、推理、物理具身与动作通过飞行和环境交互紧密耦合。基础模型、世界模型与AI智能体的进展正推动无人机自主性从任务专用感知控制，转向能理解人类意图、理解开放环境、推理物理后果并在具身与飞行动力学约束下组织复杂行为的系统，因此需要统一视角来刻画这一新兴范式。

Method: 提出"5+5"框架：以五个能力维度描述UAV EI，以五个架构层次刻画EI UAV，涵盖物理具身、通用认知、具身技能、外部交互与系统整合。基于该框架，系统综述具身形态、具身感知、世界模型、具身规划、视觉语言导航、具身操作与具身协作等方面的最新进展。

Result: 给出了该领域的统一图景与体系化综述，明确了各研究方向的最新进展及其在框架中的定位。

Conclusion: 识别出长时程自主、预测性物理推理、测试时技能获取与自主能力演化等关键挑战；主张通过物理—数字AI智能体的持续耦合（数字智能与物理感知、动力学、动作及反馈持续连接），提供通往自适应、持续演化无人机自主性的系统级路径。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) extend embodied intelligence into continuous three-dimensional space, where perception, reasoning, physical embodiment, and action are tightly coupled through flight and environmental interaction. Recent advances in foundation models, world models, and AI agents are shifting UAV autonomy from task-specific perception and control toward systems that can interpret human intent, understand open environments, reason about physical consequences, and organize complex behaviors under embodiment and flight-dynamic constraints. We characterize this emerging paradigm as UAV embodied intelligence (UAV EI) and distinguish it from its system realization, the embodied-intelligent UAV (EI UAV). To provide a unified view of the field, we introduce a 5+5 framework that describes UAV EI through five capability dimensions and EI UAVs through five architectural layers spanning physical embodiment, general cognition, embodied skills, external interaction, and system harnessing. Based on this framework, we systematically review recent progress in embodied morphology, embodied perception, world models, embodied planning, vision-language navigation, embodied manipulation, and embodied collaboration. We further identify long-horizon autonomy, predictive physical reasoning, test-time skill acquisition, and autonomous capability evolution as key challenges toward more general aerial embodied intelligence. Finally, we argue that harnessing physical-digital AI agents, through persistent coupling of digital intelligence with physical sensing, dynamics, action, and feedback, provides a system-level pathway toward adaptive and continuously evolving UAV autonomy. Project resources are available at our project website and GitHub repository.

</details>


### [98] [GraphPoint: Semantic Entity Graphs and Point Trajectories for Compositional Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18358)
*Kang Luo,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文针对机器人操作策略泛化能力差的问题，提出CoMani评测基准与GraphPoint方法，从子任务内与跨子任务两个层面研究组合式复用，实验验证了指令相关泛化的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略常难以泛化到演示之外，即使新指令涉及熟悉的对象和行为。当训练中语言与场景强相关时，策略易学到固定的视觉-动作映射，而非响应所要求的行为，因此需要研究指令相关的泛化能力。

Method: 提出CoMani基准，通过受控划分在子任务内（组合熟悉的实体、动作类型与动作修饰符）和跨子任务（在未见长时程任务中复用已学子任务）两个层面评测组合式复用，并使用匹配初始场景与单语义因子受控变化来抑制视觉捷径。进一步提出GraphPoint，将语义实体图与几何控制相连，预测未来夹爪点轨迹并借助机器人几何转换为动作；框架按语义角色组织夹爪与物体，将交互以动作类型和修饰符为条件，并利用预测进度在执行中引导切换。

Result: 在CoMani上的实验与消融验证了所提方法在两个层面的指令相关泛化上均有效。

Conclusion: 组合式复用与语义-几何结合的建模能够提升策略对语言指令的依赖与泛化能力，代码将在GraphPoint发布。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies often struggle to generalize beyond their demonstrations, even when new instructions involve familiar objects and behaviors. When language and scenes are strongly correlated during training, a policy can learn a fixed visual-action mapping rather than respond to the requested behavior. We investigate compositional reuse at two levels: within a subtask, combining familiar entities, action types, and action modifiers; and across subtasks, reusing learned subtasks in unseen long-horizon tasks. We introduce CoMani, a benchmark with controlled splits for evaluating both capabilities. Matched initial scenes and controlled changes to a single semantic factor encourage reliance on language rather than visual shortcuts. We further propose GraphPoint, which connects semantic entity graphs to geometric control by predicting future gripper point trajectories and converting them into actions using robot geometry. The framework organizes the gripper and objects by semantic roles and conditions their interactions on action types and modifiers, while predicted progress guides transitions during execution. Experiments and ablations on CoMani validate the effectiveness of our method for instruction-dependent generalization at both levels. Code will be released at GraphPoint.

</details>


### [99] [RecMorph: Topology-Guided Spatial Recurrence for Generalized Morphology Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18359)
*Quanrui Rao,Yong Liu,Xueming Xiao,Yingbo Luo,Kun Wu,Zhenyu Xu,Meibao Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RecMorph：一种拓扑引导的空间循环架构，通过深度优先遍历将运动学树转为形态序列，共享双向转换实现跨肢体通信与表征变换，在UNIMAL任务和四足机器人迁移中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用形态控制需要单一策略在具有不同物理角色的肢体间转换信息、协调全身运动，并在体型增长时保持高效。现有通信机制仅能部分满足这些需求。

Method: RecMorph采用拓扑引导的空间循环架构，利用循环序列计算同时完成跨肢体通信与表征变换。通过深度优先遍历将运动学树转换为形态派生的序列，沿该序列由共享的双向转换在动作解码前逐步变换肢体信息。借助残差保留、RMS归一化和输入依赖的通道调制稳定这种重复的空间变换，在固定模型宽度和深度下实现线性token复杂度。

Result: 在五个UNIMAL任务上，RecMorph在所评估的通用形态控制器中取得最强平均最终训练性能，并在FT上获得最高的实测推理吞吐量，同时泛化到未见变化及多达30个肢体的身体。迁移到四平台四足设置后，RecMorph在标称与高摩擦条件下取得最佳宏平均性能，相比专家MLP将标称速度RMSE降低43.5%，单一共享策略完成40次物理Go1/Go2试验无跌倒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，拓扑引导的循环变换为通用形态控制提供了一种有效且高效的通信机制，从程序化身体迁移到物理机器人平台后仍然有效。代码与实验资源已公开。

Abstract: Generalized morphology control requires a single policy to transform information across limbs with different physical roles, coordinate whole-body motion, and remain efficient as body size grows. Existing communication mechanisms address these requirements only partially. We introduce RecMorph, a topology-guided spatial recurrent architecture that uses recurrent sequence computation to jointly perform cross-limb communication and representation transformation.
