A depth-first traversal converts the kinematic tree into a morphology-derived sequence, along which shared bidirectional transitions progressively transform limb information before action decoding. Residual preservation, RMS normalization, and input-dependent channel modulation stabilize this repeated spatial transformation, yielding linear token complexity at fixed model width and depth. Across five UNIMAL tasks, RecMorph achieves the strongest mean final training performance among the evaluated generalized morphology controllers and the highest measured inference throughput on FT, while generalizing to unseen variations and bodies with up to 30 limbs. We further migrate representative generalized controllers from UNIMAL benchmarks to a four-platform quadruped setting. RecMorph achieves the best macro-averaged performance under nominal and high friction, reduces nominal velocity RMSE by 43.5% relative to specialist MLPs, and one shared policy completes 40 physical Go1/Go2 trials without falls. These results show that topology-guided recurrent transformation provides an effective and efficient communication mechanism for Generalized Morphology Control and remains effective when transferred from procedural bodies to physical robot platforms. Code and experimental resources are publicly available at https://github.com/quanruirao/RecMorph.

</details>


### [100] [Decoupling Vision, Language, and Action for Efficient Multi-Task Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18374)
*Xiatao Sun,Chen Liang,Ziyao Zeng,Qian Wang,Haoyang Zhang,Yue Sun,Qiucheng Li,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文质疑VLA模型在每个控制步都调用数十亿参数VLM主干是否必要，通过控制变量实验解耦视觉编码器、语言编码器与动作头，提出DEM模型，在保持与SOTA VLA相当成功率的同时，实现8–17倍推理频率与6–15倍更低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将动作模块挂接在数十亿参数的VLM上，每个控制步都需承担该主干的推理开销。对于低层操作策略，这一成本可能并不必要：VLM主要负责提供视觉与语言嵌入，而当前独立的视觉编码器和仅编码器语言模型在视觉嵌入与语言理解基准上已可比肩甚至超越大型VLM。作者希望以受控实验检验这一开销是否值得。

Method: 在固定演示数据、训练预算、任务与评测平台的前提下，对解耦式策略的视觉编码器、语言编码器和动作头分别进行变量替换，并与七种VLA基线对比。最终形成的DEM（Decoupled Embodiment Model）将微调后的DINOv3编码器、冻结的NeoBERT编码器与MeanFlow动作头结合，MeanFlow头在单次前向传播中生成整段动作块。

Result: 在18个仿真操作任务（含留出语言改写与随机化场景）以及3个真实机器人任务上，DEM在作者评测协议下的观测成功率与基于VLM主干的SOTA策略相当，同时推理频率为其8至17倍，单次推理能耗低6至15倍。

Conclusion: 在该任务范围内，现代解耦组件（独立视觉编码器+仅编码器语言模型+高效动作头）能提供更优的成功率—延迟—能耗权衡，说明低层操作策略并不必然需要重型VLM主干。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models attach an action module to a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with billions of parameters and pay for that backbone at every control step. For a low-level manipulation policy, this cost may be unnecessary: the VLM supplies vision and language embeddings, and recent standalone vision encoders and encoder-only language models now match or exceed large VLMs on visual embedding and language understanding benchmarks. We study this question with a controlled experiment. Holding the demonstrations, the training budget, the tasks, and the measurement platform fixed, we vary the vision encoder, the language encoder, and the action head of a decoupled policy and compare against seven VLA baselines. The study yields the Decoupled Embodiment Model (DEM), which pairs a fine-tuned DINOv3 encoder and a frozen NeoBERT encoder with a MeanFlow head that generates each action chunk in a single forward pass. On 18 simulated manipulation tasks with held-out language paraphrases and randomized scenes, and on three real-robot tasks, DEM achieves observed success comparable to state-of-the-art VLM-backbone policies under our evaluation protocol, while running at eight to seventeen times their inference frequency and drawing six to fifteen times less energy per inference. Within this task scope, modern decoupled components offer a better success--latency--energy trade-off.

</details>


### [101] [DistAL: Distance-based Advantage Learning for VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18392)
*Reece O'Mahoney,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DistAL（Distance-based Advantage Learning），用嵌入空间距离作为奖励信号来训练价值函数，从而得到信息量更丰富的价值函数和优势条件策略，提升 VLA 机器人在仿真基准与真实双臂灵巧操作任务上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 的优势条件方法（advantage conditioning）仅使用简单的成功/失败二值奖励，信息量低，导致价值函数只能区分任务进度差异，无法区分同等进度但质量不同的状态，限制了策略的迭代改进能力。作者受 OOD 检测方法探索的启发，希望引入更具信息量的奖励。

Method: 将嵌入空间中的距离作为奖励（embedding space distance as reward），替代原有二值成功/失败奖励，以此训练价值函数，再用该价值函数训练优势条件策略，形成 DistAL 方法。

Result: 在多个仿真基准以及真实硬件上的双臂灵巧操作任务中进行了验证，结果显示 DistAL 能产生信息量更丰富的价值函数，并带来更高的下游任务成功率。

Conclusion: 以嵌入空间距离替代稀疏二值奖励是一种有效的奖励设计，可提升优势条件 VLA 的价值函数质量与实际操作成功率，并在仿真与真实机器人任务上均得到验证。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have trans- formed the field of robotic manipulation in recent years by combining the semantic understanding of LLMs with the precise control of flow-matching policies. Advantage conditioning is a recent technique that iteratively improves VLAs by training a value function on deployment data and using this to train an advantage-conditioned policy. Previous works have only applied simple, low-information success/failure rewards, which leave the value function unable to distinguish states of differing quality beyond how far along the task they appear. Motivated by an exploration of out-of-distribution (OOD) detection methods, we introduce Distance-based Advantage Learning (DistAL), which, by using an embedding space distance as a reward, produces a more informative value function and subsequently a higher downstream task success rate. We validate our method on a series of simulation benchmarks and dexterous bi-manual manipulation tasks on real hardware.

</details>


### [102] [DetAug: Obstacle-Blind Trajectory Augmentation for Zero-shot Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18395)
*Reece O'Mahoney,Moritz Zoellner,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DetAug 通过在自由空间数据集的无接触（transit）阶段施加障碍物盲增强，并将增强参数作为显式条件标签记录；推理时采样标签并选择碰撞代价最低的轨迹。在 SafeLIBERO 上无碰撞成功率比次优方法高 20pp 以上，标签空间选择比同策略引导高 26pp，真机任务上也匹配或超过障碍条件化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略依赖大规模遥操作数据集训练，但这类数据多为自由空间轨迹，难以迁移到含障碍物的测试环境。现有两类方法各有缺陷：数据集增强需事先获知障碍物几何信息；推理时引导已有 checkpoint 虽无需障碍物几何，但灵活性受限。作者希望结合二者优势而规避其缺点。

Method: DetAug 对自由空间数据集的无接触（transit）阶段施加障碍物盲（obstacle-blind）增强，保持物体交互阶段不变，并将增强参数记录为显式条件标签。推理时采样一批标签，执行碰撞代价最低的轨迹。

Result: 在 SafeLIBERO 基准上，DetAug 的无碰撞成功率比次优方法高出 20pp 以上；在标签空间上做选择比同一策略的引导方法高 26pp。在真实硬件上，推理时引导方法在需要大幅绕行的任务上失效，而 DetAug 在训练中从未见过障碍物的情况下仍匹配或超过障碍物条件化基线。

Conclusion: DetAug 将训练时增强与推理时选择统一起来，无需预先获取障碍物几何信息，即可在含障碍物环境中实现高无碰撞成功率，并在仿真与真机上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Policies for robotic manipulation are produced by training on large teleoperated datasets. These datasets typically consist of free-space trajectories, making them difficult to transfer to test-time environments with obstacles. Previous methods for closing this gap have largely fallen into two groups. Dataset augmentation addresses it at training time but needs obstacle geometry in advance, whereas steering an existing checkpoint at inference time avoids that requirement but is limited in flexibility. Our method draws from both areas without inheriting either drawback. DetAug applies an obstacle-blind augmentation scheme to the transit phases of a free-space dataset, leaving object interactions untouched, and records the augmentation parameters as an explicit conditioning label. At inference it samples a batch of labels and executes the trajectory with the lowest collision cost. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark DetAug achieves a collision-free success rate more than 20pp above the next best method, and selecting over the label space outperforms guidance on the same policy by 26pp. On real hardware, inference-time steering methods collapse on tasks requiring large detours, while DetAug matches or exceeds an obstacle-conditioned baseline without ever seeing obstacles in training.

</details>


### [103] [Hardware-Free Robotics Laboratories in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18434)
*Santiago Berrezueta-Guzman,Habiba-Loai Khalil,Andrei Koshelev,Vanesa Metaj,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MR-Robotics LAB 混合现实平台，将 MATLAB 生成的机器人轨迹按真实尺度重放于学习者物理环境，实现无硬件条件下的机器人可视化教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人教学依赖屏幕仿真，仅在抽象坐标系中显示运动，缺乏真实尺度与学习者自身空间的对应；而实体硬件受成本、安全与排期限制难以普及。

Method: 基于浏览器的服务校验 MATLAB 工作区文件（.mat），归一化单位并发布带版本号的 JSON 轨迹；Meta Quest 3 上的 Unity 应用在位置控制下复现关节配置，按声明帧率在具备碰撞检测与末端执行器抓取的物理场景中重放。

Result: 工程专业学生的单组形成性评估显示：搭建投入低（M = 4.67/5），多视角观察对工作空间理解的支持度较高（M = 4.56），83% 参与者愿意在机器人学导论课程中使用该平台。

Conclusion: 贡献为可复用的“仿真到混合现实”轨迹通路及面向工程教育的无硬件机器人可视化设计指南；因测评仅记录主观感知、无对照与学习效果测量，故不主张相对桌面仿真的比较优势。

Abstract: Teaching robotics relies on screen-based simulation, showing robot motion in an abstract coordinate frame rather than at real scale in the learner's own space, while access to physical hardware is limited by cost, safety, and scheduling constraints. We present MR-Robotics LAB, a mixed-reality (MR) platform that replays MATLAB-generated robot trajectories at real scale within the learner's physical environment. A browser-based service validates a MATLAB workspace file (.mat), normalizes units, and publishes a versioned JSON trajectory; a Unity application on a Meta Quest 3 then reproduces the authored joint configurations under position control and replays them at the declared frame rate within a physics-enabled scene that supports collision detection and end-effector grasping. A formative single-group evaluation with engineering students found that participants reported low setup effort (M = 4.67 on a 5-point scale) and perceived support for workspace understanding from multi-viewpoint inspection (M = 4.56), and 83% of participants affirmed their willingness to use the platform in an introductory robotics course. The evaluation instrument records only perceived outcomes, without counterbalancing or a learning measure, so no comparative advantage over desktop simulation is claimed. The contribution is a reusable simulation-to-MR trajectory pathway and design guidance for hardware-free robot visualization in engineering education.

</details>


### [104] [VLM-MPPI: Grounding Natural Language in Behaviorally Diverse Trajectories for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18451)
*Hanbing Zhang,Fangguo Zhao,Zerui Li,Xin Guan,Peng Cheng,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层无人机导航框架：用六个行为条件化MPPI并行生成多样候选轨迹，投影到FPV图像后由VLM根据自然语言选择，实现语义与飞行动作的对接，仿真与实机均达100%任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内杂乱环境中，自然语言意图与无人机底层可飞行动作之间存在语义鸿沟；单一或纯随机的轨迹采样难以产生语义上有意义且行为多样的候选，同时VLM推理延迟给实时闭环控制带来挑战。

Method: 构建分层导航框架：并行运行六个行为条件化的MPPI规划器，通过设计模式专属引导代价与采样偏置，诱导收敛到不同行为均值的轨迹模式；将3D候选轨迹投影到机载第一人称RGB图像上，把语言落地转化为视觉动作选择问题；由预训练VLM结合叠加的FPV图像与自然语言提示异步选择候选索引，MPPI以20Hz重规划，PID控制器跟踪所选轨迹。在NVIDIA Isaac Sim与配备LiDAR和RGB的四旋翼实机上实现完整流程。

Result: 仿真与实机飞行实验显示：行为多样性具有语义意义，在VLM延迟下仍保持稳健的语言对齐，各模式飞行安全且可重复，所评估场景中任务成功率达100%。

Conclusion: 将行为条件化MPPI的多样性轨迹生成与VLM的视觉语言选择相结合，可有效弥合语言意图与可飞行行为之间的差距，异步架构缓解了VLM延迟问题，验证了该方法在室内导航中的可行性与可靠性。

Abstract: We present a hierarchical UAV navigation framework that aligns natural-language intent with dynamically feasible flight behaviors in cluttered indoor environments. To bridge the gap between abstract semantics and low-level control, we employ a parallelized ensemble of six behavior-conditioned Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) planners. Crucially, by designing mode-specific guiding costs and sampling biases, we induce distinct trajectory modes that converge to unique behavioral means, yielding a compact set of intentionally diverse candidates rather than mere stochastic variations. We project these 3D candidates onto the onboard first-person-view RGB stream, turning language grounding into a visual action selection problem. A pretrained vision--language model (VLM) asynchronously selects the candidate index given the overlaid FPV image and a natural-language prompt, while MPPI replans at 20Hz and a PID-based low-level controller tracks the selected trajectory. We implement the full pipeline in NVIDIA Isaac Sim and on a real-world quadrotor platform equipped with LiDAR and RGB sensing. Experiments in both simulation and real-world flights show semantically meaningful behavior diversity, robust language alignment despite VLM latency, and safe, repeatable flight across all modes, achieving 100% task success in our evaluated scenarios.

</details>


### [105] [ForwardDLO: Model-Based Bimanual Shape Matching of Unconstrained Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18455)
*Tim Missal,Berk Guler,Lucas Domingues,Simon Manschitz,Jan Peters,Paula Dornhofer Paro Costa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ForwardDLO 循环潜 dynamics 模型，用于非固定双手机器人操控绳缆等可变形线性物体的形状控制，以低成本批量预测各段位移，实现高吞吐规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳缆、线缆等可变形线性物体的形状控制是机器人操控难题。在物体未固定于支撑面、双臂可沿其长度任意抓取的通用设定下，抓取点、方向和幅度的联合动作空间组合爆炸，而动力学模型的单次预测开销限制了规划器可搜索的动作数量。

Method: 提出 ForwardDLO：一种面向非固定双臂场景的循环潜动力学模型，在每一步依据观测到的绳状态预测各段位移。模型不含显式的段间操作，因而候选动作的批量评估成本低廉。在真实绳运动上做开环预测；并在固定时间预算内比较候选动作评分数量、真实形状匹配和模拟布线任务（30Hz 控制率）。

Result: 精度与更昂贵基线相当；真实绳运动开环预测误差在所评估学习模型中最低，比最强基线低 13%。固定时间预算内评估的候选动作比同精度模型多 8–22 倍，真实形状匹配相当。模拟布线任务中 30Hz 下任务成功率达 98%，基线在其自身预算下至多 30%。发布模型、代码及含 242 万模拟与 14107 条真实绳过渡的数据集。

Conclusion: ForwardDLO 通过高效批量候选动作评估，将预测吞吐转化为真实与模拟任务中的显著性能优势，为可变形线性物体的形状控制提供了有效途径。数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Ropes, cables, and other deformable linear objects appear in tasks from untangling to cable routing and suturing, yet controlling their shape remains a challenge in robot manipulation. We study model-based shape control in a general setting: the object lies unfixated on a support surface and two arms may grasp and move it anywhere along its length. Because each arm chooses a grasp point, direction, and magnitude, the joint action space is combinatorially large, and the dynamics model's per-prediction cost bounds how much of it a planner can search. We present ForwardDLO, a recurrent latent dynamics model for this unfixated bimanual setting that predicts per-segment displacements grounded in the observed rope state at every step. Our model reaches accuracy comparable to more expensive baselines while containing no explicit segment-to-segment operations, which makes batched evaluation of candidate actions cheap. On open-loop prediction of real rope motion it reaches the lowest error of the learned models we evaluate, 13% below the strongest baseline. Within a fixed time budget it scores 8 to 22 times more candidate actions than models of comparable accuracy while matching them in real-world shape matching; and on a simulated routing task at a 30Hz control rate, this throughput converts into 98% task success versus at most 30% for the baselines at their own budgets. We release the model, code, and a dataset of 2.42 million simulated and 14,107 real rope transitions at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ForwardDLO/

</details>


### [106] [Real-Time Bounded Catenary Solver for UAV Tether Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18482)
*Max Beffert,Andreas Zell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将悬链线边值问题化简为单变量超越方程，推导出闭式区间并证明单调性、凸性及根的唯一性以保证收敛，结合两段式初值与定制化 rtsafe 求解器，实现近恒定且带最坏情况保证的求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系留多旋翼无人机在真实非稳态条件下运行时，缆绳气动阻力对无人机受力的影响不可忽略，而在线应用对最大求解时间有硬性上限。此前基于通用求根器的准解析悬链线模型虽平均求解时间为 0.51 ms，但缺乏最坏情况保证与收敛性证明。

Method: 1) 将悬链线边值问题重新表述为关于单个良态未知量的超越方程；2) 推导闭式括号区间，并证明根的存在性、唯一性、单调性与凸性，从而保证求解器收敛；3) 提出两段式初始猜测以逼近真根；4) 基于混合求根法 rtsafe（牛顿-拉夫逊结合二分回退，具有有界迭代次数），实现利用问题结构省略冗余检查的专用变体。

Result: 两段式初值将真根逼近至 3.4% 以内，较教科书式初值把平均迭代次数减少 68.0% 至 2.36；专用 rtsafe 变体带来最高 1.3 倍加速；完整缆绳模型平均求解时间 6.9 us、最坏 7.7 us，较之前方法的优化重实现加速 40 倍，相对偏差 8.7e-9；在无人机飞控的 ArduPilot 上以 Lua 实现，平均求解时间 0.74 ms，处于调度预算内。

Conclusion: 该重新表述未改动底层物理模型，因此前人工作的实验验证可原样沿用。所提求解器兼具近恒定求解时间与最坏情况保证，并适用于嵌入式资源受限平台。

Abstract: For non-stationary tethered multirotor UAVs in real-world conditions, simulating the forces imposed on the drone by the aerodynamic drag of the tether becomes crucial, with online use cases placing a hard bound on the maximum solve time. In previous work, a quasi-analytical catenary tether model reached a mean solve time of 0.51 ms using a general-purpose root finder, but without any worst-case guarantees or proven convergence. In this work, we reformulate the inner solver by reducing the catenary boundary-value problem to a single transcendental equation in one well-conditioned unknown. We derive a closed-form bracket and prove monotonicity and convexity as well as existence and uniqueness of the root, which together guarantee convergence of the solver. We further propose a two-regime initial guess which approximates the true root within 3.4% and reduces the mean iteration count by 68.0% to 2.36 compared to the textbook initialization. Building on the hybrid root-finding method rtsafe (Newton-Raphson with bisection fallback giving bounded iteration counts), we implement a specialized variant that exploits the problem structure to omit unnecessary checks while retaining correctness, which gives up to 1.3 times speedup. With the proposed solver the full tether model achieves a nearly constant solve time of 6.9 us on average and 7.7 us at worst, a 40 times speedup over an optimized re-implementation of the previous method, while agreeing with it to a relative deviation of 8.7e-9. Because the reformulation leaves the underlying physical model untouched, the experimental validation of the previous work carries over unchanged. We further demonstrate its suitability for embedded, resource-constrained platforms with a Lua implementation running directly in ArduPilot on a drone's flight controller, where it stays well inside the scheduling budget with a mean solve time of 0.74 ms.

</details>


### [107] [ActionPiece: Rethinking Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18487)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Zhaolong Shen,Xiaopeng Lin,Yichao Du,Zhirui Zhang,Laurence T. Yang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出物理秩一致性（PRC）指标与ActionPiece动作分词器，通过表征学习与量化的联合监督保留物理动作关系，在多个基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分词器在自回归VLA模型中至关重要，决定策略训练目标与可执行命令。其保真度通常仅用逐点重建指标（如MSE）评估，但小的独立误差无法刻画跨演示中动作调整是否被忠实保留。压缩后相似动作可能仍聚集于代表性运动，而不同情境所需的调整会被削弱、扭曲甚至反转，因此需要新的关系保真度度量。

Method: 引入物理秩一致性（PRC），衡量分词在重建后对局部物理距离排序的保留程度，对解码动作进行评估以实现跨词汇表和不同解码器架构的可比性。提出ActionPiece，通过表征学习与量化的联合监督保留物理动作关系：物理秩保留监督编码器与量化特征距离中的远近排序，量化正则化将相同排序应用于码字分配分布。两者与重建目标共同作用，生成离散动作token，供标准自回归策略学习并通过冻结解码器执行。

Result: 在相同Qwen3-VL-4B策略训练设置下，ActionPiece在LIBERO上达94.8%，在未见过的LIBERO-Plus上达68.8%，在SimplerEnv上达71.9%，在VLA-Arena L0-L2上达51.5%。组件消融显示两个目标共同提升PRC与策略成功率。

Conclusion: PRC以关系保真度补充逐点精度评估，ActionPiece验证了物理关系监督对动作分词的价值，能够提升策略成功率并保持跨词汇表与解码器架构的可比性。

Abstract: Action tokenizers play a central role in autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) models, determining both the targets for policy training and the executable commands recovered from predicted tokens. Their fidelity is commonly evaluated using pointwise reconstruction metrics such as mean squared error (MSE), yet small individual errors do not fully characterize how faithfully action adjustments across demonstrations are preserved. After compression, similar actions may still cluster around a representative motion, while the adjustments needed for different contexts are diminished, distorted, or even reversed. We introduce physical rank consistency (PRC) to measure how well tokenization preserves local physical distance rankings after reconstruction. Evaluating decoded actions provides a common reference across token vocabularies and decoder architectures, complementing pointwise accuracy with a measure of relational fidelity. We further present ActionPiece, which preserves physical action relationships through joint supervision of representation learning and quantization. Physical rank preservation supervises near-far ordering in encoder and quantized feature distances, while quantization regularization applies the same ordering to codeword assignment distributions. Both objectives augment reconstruction, producing discrete action tokens for standard autoregressive policy learning and execution through a frozen decoder. Under the same Qwen3-VL-4B policy training setup, ActionPiece achieves 94.8% on LIBERO and 68.8% on unseen LIBERO-Plus, with additional evaluations reaching 71.9% on SimplerEnv and 51.5% across VLA-Arena L0-L2. Component ablations show that the two objectives jointly improve PRC and policy success, demonstrating the value of physical relationship supervision for action tokenization.

</details>


### [108] [TAO-Force: Unifying Force-Aware Perception and Fast-Slow Control for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18497)
*Bohan Gan,Xuanzhang Wen,Yongsheng Zhao,Baoping Cheng,Wenhe Jia,Ye Wang,Gongxin Yao,Han Gao,Jingyao Tang,Lei Zhao,Ji Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TAO-Force，一种力条件化的VLA框架，将力感知策略学习与接触调节执行相结合，用于接触丰富操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型以视觉为中心感知且采用位置控制执行，难以应对接触丰富任务：视觉观测难以提供接触发生与交互强度的证据，位置控制也无法顺应快速变化的接触动力学。

Method: 针对感知，提出力条件特征线性调制（F-FiLM），将编码后的力反馈注入冻结预训练视觉-语言主干表征，保留其语义先验；针对控制，采用接触门控快慢架构，慢速位置控制分支在非接触阶段跟踪标称轨迹，快速导纳控制分支在接触阶段调节物理交互。

Result: 在力感知任务上的详细分析以及四个真实世界接触丰富操作任务上的评估，验证了TAO-Force的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过弥合感知与控制两方面的差距，力条件化VLA可提升接触丰富操作任务的表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong performance across diverse robotic manipulation tasks, yet their predominantly vision-centric perception and position-controlled execution remain insufficient for contact-rich manipulation. Visual observations alone often provide limited evidence of contact onset and interaction magnitude, while position-control policies cannot respond compliantly to rapidly changing contact dynamics. To bridge both the perception and control gaps, we propose TAO-Force, a force-conditioned VLA framework that combines force-aware policy learning with contact-regulated execution. For force-aware perception, TAO-Force introduces Force-conditioned Feature-wise Linear Modulation (F-FiLM) to inject encoded force feedback into the representations of a frozen pretrained visual-language backbone while preserving its semantic priors. For responsive control, it employs a contact-gated fast-slow architecture, with a slow position-control branch tracking nominal trajectories during non-contact phases and a fast admittance-control branch regulating physical interaction during contact phases. Detailed analyses on a force-perception task and real-world evaluations across four contact-rich manipulation tasks validate the effectiveness and robustness of TAO-Force.

</details>


### [109] [InterMASH: A Unified Geometric Representation for Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18504)
*Xuanze Yang,Yumeng Liu,Haiyang Xin,Changhao Li,Haowei Shen,Kai Xu,Ligang Liu,Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出InterMASH统一几何表征，用球面固定锚点+低阶球谐编码手部/物体几何与接触，作为跨具身交互的显式token序列，并基于条件Diffusion Transformer联合生成手部几何与接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抓取合成需生成稳定且物理合理的手-物交互，但人手与机械手在手部形态和表面建模上存在差异，缺乏统一表征；现有接触图或稠密隐式描述子表征不完整，或计算昂贵且冗余。

Method: 提出InterMASH统一几何表征：以球面固定锚点建立跨具身对应关系；在每个锚点用低阶球谐紧凑编码局部手部几何、物体几何与接触，构成显式、可解释的token序列；在此基础上引入条件Diffusion Transformer，直接在InterMASH表征空间中运行，联合生成手部几何与接触。

Result: 在大型ShadowHand基准上，关键物理可行性指标达到与SOTA相当的性能；支持多手联合训练；用人类抓取数据跨具身微调可提升机器人抓取成功率与多样性。

Conclusion: InterMASH提供了跨人手与机械手的统一、显式且可解释的交互表征，并配合条件扩散Transformer实现几何与接触的联合生成，验证了跨具身迁移的有效性。

Abstract: Grasp synthesis aims to generate stable and physically plausible hand--object interactions, and has become a fundamental problem in both human hand modeling and robotic manipulation. However, a unified representation across human and robotic hands is still lacking, mainly due to differences in hand morphology and surface modeling. Prior methods typically rely on either contact maps or dense implicit descriptors to represent interaction, but these representations are often incomplete or computationally expensive and redundant. We propose InterMASH, a unified geometric representation that establishes cross-embodiment correspondence using sphere-fixed anchors. At each anchor, low-degree spherical harmonics compactly encode local hand geometry, object geometry, and contact, forming an explicit and interpretable token sequence. Building on this natively tokenized structure, we introduce a conditional Diffusion Transformer that operates directly in the proposed InterMASH representation space and jointly generates hand geometry and contact, improving consistency and physical plausibility. Our method achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods on key physical feasibility metrics in a large-scale ShadowHand benchmark, supports joint training across multiple hands, and shows that cross-embodiment fine-tuning with human grasp data can improve robotic grasp success and diversity. Project page is available at https://inter-mash.github.io/.

</details>


### [110] [ActiveScale: Scaling Active Perception for Robots across Model, Data, and Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18514)
*Shuai Zhou,Kaisheng Pang,Wenxuan Song,Wenjie Zhang,Xinhu Zheng,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ActiveScale 框架，通过模型、数据与硬件的协同设计提升 VLA 模型的主动感知能力，在主动感知任务上取得更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定视角会遮挡或遗漏任务相关信息，主动感知对机器人操作至关重要；但让 VLA 模型在变化的视角间推理并主动获取有信息量的观测仍具挑战。

Method: 1) 模型：在 VLA 中引入历史视频观测与显式相机位姿监督，通过逐帧位姿 token 和轻量预测头跨视角关联观测；2) 数据：利用 1000 小时第一人称与机器人数据，设计可扩展的人类-机器人中训练配方，使模型适配时序输入与位姿监督；3) 硬件：提出 AMP 主动感知移动操作平台，支持单操作员遥操作，可规模化采集协同视角变化与操作的示范数据。

Result: 实验显示主动感知任务成功率提升；消融研究验证了相机位姿感知建模与第一人称中训练的贡献。

Conclusion: 上述组件共同为机器人操作中主动感知的研究与开发提供了集成化基础。

Abstract: Active perception is essential for robotic manipulation when fixed viewpoints leave task-relevant information occluded or unobserved. However, enabling vision-language-action (VLA) models to reason across changing viewpoints and actively acquire informative observations remains challenging. We present ActiveScale, a framework that advances active perception through coordinated model, data, and hardware designs. Our model augments a VLA with historical video observations and explicit camera-pose supervision, using per-frame pose tokens and a lightweight prediction head to associate observations across viewpoints and support a coherent understanding of the scene. To learn from the camera motion naturally present in human activity, we introduce a scalable human--robot mid-training recipe using 1000 hours of egocentric and robotic data, adapting the model to temporal inputs and pose supervision. We further introduce Active-perception Mobile-manipulation Platform (AMP), a robotic platform that supports active perception and mobile manipulation through single-operator teleoperation, enabling scalable collection of demonstrations that coordinate viewpoint changes and manipulation. Experiments demonstrate improved success rates on active-perception tasks, while ablation studies validate the contributions of camera-pose-aware modeling and egocentric mid-training. Together, these components provide an integrated foundation for studying and developing active perception in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [111] [DynoFluxBench: Benchmarking Kinodynamic Space-Time Planners in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18549)
*Franz Queißner,Andreas Orthey,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DynoFluxBench基准框架，用于在已知动态环境中比较无界到达时间下的运动动力学规划器；并开发ST-Db-RRT、ST-GBRRT与KIST三种新规划器，建立强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人若离开结构化、静态环境，必须在移动障碍物间生成既满足运动动力学约束又安全的运动。然而目前缺乏同时兼顾运动动力学可行性与动态障碍规避的专用基准框架，导致此类规划器难以公平比较与评估。为弥补这一空白，作者提出DynoFluxBench。

Method: 构建DynoFluxBench框架，支持在已知动态环境、无界到达时间条件下比较运动动力学规划器。为展示其效用并建立基线，提出三种融合运动动力学与时空方法的规划器：ST-Db-RRT以随机选取的不连续度有界（discontinuity-bounded）运动基元、结合轨迹优化进行扩展；KIST与ST-GBRRT则在运动动力学可行树上采用不同启发式引导。论文还从理论上分析了新规划器在动态环境中的概率完备性。

Result: 在DynoFluxBench上的评估表明：ST-Db-RRT求取首个解的速度最高快32倍；在轨迹优化易失效的场景中，KIST与ST-GBRRT仍具价值。

Conclusion: DynoFluxBench填补了动态环境下运动动力学规划器缺乏统一基准的空白，并提供了三种覆盖不同搜索范式的新规划器作为强基线。ST-Db-RRT在求解速度上优势显著，而KIST与ST-GBRRT为轨迹优化脆弱的情形提供互补方案。视频与进一步分析见 https://dynofluxbench.github.io/dynofluxbench/。

Abstract: Robots that leave structured, static environments must plan motions that are kinodynamically feasible and safe among moving obstacles. However, there are no dedicated benchmark frameworks that combine both aspects. To overcome this, we present DynoFluxBench, a framework to compare kinodynamic planners in known, dynamic environments with unbounded arrival time. To demonstrate its utility and establish strong baselines, we develop three dedicated planners, named ST-Db-RRT, ST-GBRRT, and KIST, that fuse kinodynamic and space-time methods, covering different kinodynamic search paradigms: ST-Db-RRT expands with randomly selected discontinuity-bounded motion primitives using trajectory optimization, whereas KIST and ST-GBRRT maintain a kinodynamically feasible tree with different heuristic guidance. We analyze the probabilistic completeness guarantees of those new planners in dynamic environments. Finally, we evaluate ST-Db-RRT, ST-GBRRT, and KIST using DynoFluxBench, showing that ST-Db-RRT reaches a first solution up to 32 times faster, while KIST and ST-GBRRT remain valuable where trajectory optimization is fragile. Videos and further analysis can be found at https://dynofluxbench.github.io/dynofluxbench/.

</details>


### [112] [GroundingVLN: Reasoning and Acting with Grounding for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18581)
*Kailing Li,Yu Han,Tianwen Qian,Yuqian Fu,Jingyu Gong,Jiangming Shi,Xiaoling Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GroundingVLN，以视觉定位作为推理与动作的共享接口，弥合 VLN 中语义推理与空间执行的间隙，并实现 SOTA 性能与高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航（VLN）智能体难以将语义推理与空间执行联系起来：中间推理未显式锚定于视觉证据，高层决策缺乏精确的空间目标来引导低层运动。认知科学表明人类导航通过将认知锚定于关键地标、并朝空间目标引导移动来分层地弥合这两个层级，受此启发提出本方法。

Method: 提出 GroundingVLN，将视觉定位用作推理与动作之间的共享接口：推理阶段在结构化推理过程中把任务相关视觉证据锚定到精确图像位置（reasoning with grounding）；行动阶段预测与进度对齐的像素级目标，由几何规划器转换为原子动作（acting through grounding）。同时构建 GroundingCOTVLN-188K（时间对齐的带定位推理轨迹数据集），并提出 Grounded and Execution-Aware Reinforcement Learning（GEAR），将带定位推理与空间决策同下游执行对齐。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 上达到 69.9% SR，在 RxR-CE 上达到 75.1% SR，取得 SOTA 性能；样本效率高，仅使用最强基线约 0.9% 的训练数据。泛化能力强：仅在 R2R 上训练时，在 RxR-CE 上仍达 59.9% SR，较最强基线提升 20.1%。

Conclusion: 以视觉定位作为推理与动作的统一接口，可有效弥合 VLN 中语义推理与空间执行的鸿沟，在性能、样本效率与跨数据集泛化上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Although vision-language models (VLMs) possess strong visual understanding and reasoning capabilities, existing vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents struggle to connect semantic reasoning with spatial execution. Two coupled gaps remain in this connection, as intermediate reasoning is not explicitly anchored to visual evidence and high-level decisions lack precise spatial goals to guide low-level motion. Cognitive science suggests that human navigation bridges these levels hierarchically by anchoring cognition to relevant landmarks and guiding locomotion toward spatial goals. Motivated by this principle, we propose GroundingVLN, which uses visual grounding as a shared interface between reasoning and action. GroundingVLN first reasons with grounding by anchoring task-relevant visual evidence to precise image locations throughout structured reasoning. It then acts through grounding by predicting a progress-aligned pixel goal that a geometric planner translates into primitive actions. To learn these capabilities, we construct GroundingCOTVLN-188K, a dataset of temporally aligned grounded reasoning traces, and introduce Grounded and Execution-Aware Reinforcement Learning (GEAR), which aligns grounded reasoning and spatial decisions with downstream execution. Experiments demonstrate that GroundingVLN achieves state-of-the-art performance (69.9% SR on R2R-CE and 75.1% SR on RxR-CE) with high sample efficiency, using just 0.9% as much training data as the strongest baseline. It also generalizes strongly across datasets, attaining 59.9% SR on RxR-CE when trained solely on R2R, a gain of 20.1% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [113] [DeformSmith: Physics Harness-Guided Hierarchical Generation of Deformable Assets for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18620)
*Can Li,Jie Gu,Zishun Deng,Jingmin Chen,Lei Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeformSmith：通过分层智能体构建与物理闭环交互反馈，从文本/单图生成可用于机器人操作的可变形资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为机器人操作创建可变形资产需同时指定几何、外观与物理属性。文本与图像对变形及接触响应提供的证据有限，而响应特性直接决定资产是否适合交互；自动生成因此需解决耦合的物理约束，并以交互证据指导构建与精炼。

Method: 提出 DeformSmith 框架：采用分层智能体式（hierarchical agentic）构建流程，配合共享的物理基座（physics-grounded harness），逐步构建、测试并精炼几何、物理模型、材料行为与机器人交互，直至资产可用于仿真与操作；通过操作反馈和可回放的交互数据闭合生成回路。

Result: 在视觉质量与物理合理性上优于 PhysGen3D、PhysGM、PhysX-Omni 等最先进基线，并支持为可变形物体的机器人操作合成数据。

Conclusion: 交互闭环（操作反馈+可回放交互数据）是提升生成可变形资产物理可信度与可操作性的关键，DeformSmith 为交互式可变形资产生成与机器人操作数据合成提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Creating deformable assets for robot manipulation requires jointly specifying their geometry, appearance, and physical properties. This is especially challenging for deformable objects, since text and images provide limited evidence about how they deform and respond to contact, yet these responses directly affect their suitability for interaction. Automated generation therefore needs to resolve coupled physical requirements and use interaction evidence to guide construction and refinement. We present DeformSmith, a framework that enables automated generation of interactive, physically credible deformable assets from text or a single image. Through hierarchical agentic construction and a shared physics-grounded harness, it progressively builds, tests, and refines geometry, physical models, material behavior, and robot interaction until the resulting asset is ready for simulation and manipulation. Robot interaction closes the generation loop through manipulation feedback and replayable interaction data. Results show that DeformSmith generates assets with better visual quality and physical plausibility than state-of-the-art baselines, including PhysGen3D, PhysGM, and PhysX-Omni, while supporting the synthesis of data for robotic manipulation of deformable objects. Project page: https://can-lee.github.io/deformsmith-web/

</details>


### [114] [Benchmarking Visual-Inertial Odometry in Subterranean Environments Under Sensor Degradation, Miscalibration, and Dynamic Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18628)
*Yueying Zhu,Xiang Li,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Xuehe Wang,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向地下环境的VIO失效导向压力测试基准，基于CERBERUS数据集，在九种扰动下系统评估四类VIO系统，并引入覆盖率与失效阈值揭示鲁棒性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIO是GPS拒止地下环境自主运行的核心能力，但传感器漂移、标定误差与动态遮挡会显著降低其可靠性；现有评估多聚焦标称条件精度，难以揭示实际部署中的失效时机与原因。

Method: 基于CERBERUS数据集构建失效导向压力测试基准，覆盖滤波、优化、学习三类范式的四种代表性VIO系统，设置九种实际扰动（IMU偏置与噪声变化、相机内参和外参漂移、动态场景遮挡）；除轨迹误差外，采用覆盖率与失效阈值分析鲁棒性极限。

Result: 揭示了仅凭标称性能无法体现的崩溃行为，并发现不同VIO范式具有差异化脆弱模式：部分方法对惯性退化更敏感，另一些受几何误标定或动态干扰影响更大。

Conclusion: 该研究为地下场景中VIO选型、标定优先级与可靠运行提供面向部署的指导，并将发布完整基准脚本与评估流程以支持可复现评估与后续扩展。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) is a core capability for autonomous operation in GPS-denied subterranean environments, yet its reliability can degrade sharply under sensor drift, calibration errors, and dynamic occlusion. Existing evaluations mainly emphasize nominal-condition accuracy, offering limited insight into when practical deployment failures occur. In this work, we present a failure-centric stress-test benchmark for VIO in underground environments using the CERBERUS dataset. We systematically evaluate four representative VIO systems spanning filtering-, optimization-, and learning-based paradigms under nine practical perturbation settings, including IMU bias and noise variation, camera intrinsic and extrinsic drift, and dynamic scene occlusion. Beyond conventional trajectory error, we analyze robustness limits through coverage ratio and failure thresholds, revealing breakdown behaviors that are not captured by nominal-condition performance alone. Our study shows distinct vulnerability patterns across VIO paradigms: some methods are more sensitive to inertial degradation, while others are more affected by geometric miscalibration or dynamic interference. These results provide deployment-oriented guidance for VIO selection, calibration prioritization, and reliable operation in challenging underground scenarios. To support reproducible evaluation and future extensions, we will release the full benchmark scripts and evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [115] [From Gameplay to Policy: Towards Scalable Robot Data Collection via Gamified Robot-Free Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18650)
*Zheng Li,Liang Zhu,Junzhe Wang,Huayuan Chen,Ziyun Liu,Jiahang Cao,Xinyu Sheng,Pei Qu,Yufei Jia,Ximeng Zhang,Jiarui Xie,Zizhao Yuan,Haoang Li,Yi Cai,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Project Kitchen——基于VR的体感游戏化数据采集平台，以及Game2Policy——从游戏轨迹中提取具身无关可供性线索并迁移到真实机器人的方法，在少样本设定下显著提升策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作策略需要大规模多样化交互数据，但真实世界示教采集成本高、难以规模化。现有数据采集方法或依赖特定机器人硬件（限制众包与迁移性），或存在标注不完整、行为多样性不足的问题。受游戏能长期维持人类参与度的启发，作者探索将数据采集转化为游戏化体验，并把人类操作经验迁移到真实机器人。

Method: 提出Project Kitchen：基于VR的游戏化第一人称视角数据采集平台，可激发多样化、目标导向的操作行为，且不依赖特定机器人本体与硬件，便于大规模部署。为弥合"游戏到现实"的差距，进一步提出Game2Policy：从游戏轨迹中提取具身无关的可供性线索（接触点、子目标状态），在游戏数据上预训练可供性模型，再用少量真实机器人示教与下游策略联合微调。

Result: 在少样本设定下，Game2Policy在仿真中平均成功率提升10.0个百分点，在真实机器人上提升18.3个百分点。用户研究与定量分析表明，Project Kitchen能促进多样化操作行为并提供富有吸引力的数据采集体验。

Conclusion: 结果证明了游戏化虚拟环境作为可扩展操作知识来源的潜力。平台与代码将在论文接收后开源。

Abstract: Learning generalizable robot manipulation policies requires large-scale and diverse interaction data, yet collecting real-world demonstrations remains costly and difficult to scale. Existing approaches to data collection are either dependent on specific robot hardware that limits crowdsourcing and transferability, or suffer from incomplete annotation and limited behavioral diversity. Inspired by how games sustain long-term human engagement, we explore an alternative paradigm that turns data collection into an engaging gameplay experience and transfers the resulting human manipulation experience to real robots. We present Project Kitchen, a VR-based gamified egocentric data collection platform that elicits diverse, goal-directed manipulation while remaining independent of specific robot embodiments and hardware, making it applicable to broader and potentially large-scale deployment. To bridge the game-to-real gap, we further introduce Game2Policy, which extracts embodiment-invariant affordance cues, including contact points and sub-goal states, from gameplay trajectories. An affordance model is pre-trained on game-collected data and then jointly fine-tuned with downstream policies using only a handful of real-robot demonstrations. Experiments show that Game2Policy improves average success rates by 10.0 points in simulation and 18.3 points on real robots in the few-shot setting. User studies and quantitative analyses further show that Project Kitchen promotes diverse manipulation behaviors and provides an engaging data collection experience. These results demonstrate the potential of gamified virtual environments as a scalable source of manipulation knowledge. The platform and code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [116] [FIERCE: From Generalist Robot Policies to Fast Specialists via Progress-Failure Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18651)
*Runjia Tan,Yuang Tu,Yujie Yan,Lan Yu,Xuesong Tian,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIERCE 是一个以通用策略为初始化的强化学习框架，核心是统一、任务自适应的"进展-失败"评估器；仅需目标任务的有限真实交互即可将通用策略精炼为紧凑专家策略，部署时只保留该专家策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略虽可作为有效初始化，但将其精炼为紧凑专用策略时，受限于有限的物理交互，需要高信息量的学习反馈（如进展信号与失败风险），而人工标注密集奖励或依赖专用仿真器代价高昂。

Method: 提出 FIERCE：构建统一的"进展-失败"评估器，在"观测-语言"表示上共享一个观测进展头与一个动作条件潜变量预测器，后者用过去与当前预测驱动因果序列头估计任务失败。评估器由进展标签与偏好标签、同步指令与观测、以及终止结果联合监督训练，并借助目标任务 rollout 进行适应与校准。训练中冻结评估器快照，提供进展塑形奖励与失败风险惩罚，并叠加独立验证的终止奖励；评估器与策略交替更新。精炼过程不需要持续查询通用策略动作，也不需要目标任务专用仿真器或人工密集奖励标注。

Result: 在仿真环境与两个接触密集的真实任务上开展评估，分别从反馈质量、策略学习效率与部署成本三个维度进行对比分析。

Conclusion: FIERCE 能够在不依赖通用策略持续查询、专用仿真器和人工密集奖励的前提下，由通用策略有效精炼出紧凑专用策略，并在真实接触密集任务上实现高效学习与低部署成本；代码、模型权重与数据恢复工具已开源。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies offer useful initialization, but refining compact specialists through limited physical interaction requires informative learning feedback. We present FIERCE, a generalist-initialized reinforcement learning framework centered on a unified, task-adaptive progress-failure evaluator. Its architecture shares an observation-language representation between an observed-progress head and an action-conditioned latent predictor whose past and current predictions feed a causal sequence head for task-failure estimation. Joint supervision from progress and preference labels, synchronized commands and observations, and terminal outcomes trains the evaluator; target-task rollouts support adaptation and calibration. Fixed evaluator snapshots provide progress shaping and failure-risk penalties alongside independently verified terminal rewards, while evaluator and policy updates alternate as new experience is collected. Refinement requires neither continued generalist action queries nor a dedicated target-task simulator or manually annotated dense rewards. Only the compact specialist is retained at deployment. The evaluation separates feedback quality, policy-learning efficiency, and deployment cost across simulation and two contact-rich real tasks. Code, model weights, and data-restoration tools are released at https://github.com/ar-mine/FIERCE.

</details>


### [117] [VLA-ULAP: Interleaving Cloud VLA Calls with Ultra-Lightweight Local Action Prediction at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18663)
*Deyu Cao,Ryuji Oi,Kosuke Matsushima,Yuxuan Pan,Ziheng Wang,Daichi Fujiki,Atsutake Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 VLA-ULAP，将远程 VLA 调用与超轻量本地动作预测器（ULAP）交错执行，在减少 VLA 调用与能耗的同时基本保持成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿参数级 VLA 策略机载功耗高，而远程推理的通信延迟又影响实时响应，因此需要在保证任务成功率的前提下降低计算与能耗开销。

Method: ULAP 仅约 7.4M 参数（含冻结视觉编码器），融合当前视图、本体感受与已执行动作历史，一次前向预测动作块；独立训练，不需要 VLA 隐状态、在线验证或服务器往返。系统以交错方式在远程 VLA 调用之间运行 ULAP。

Result: 在 Jetson Orin Nano 上，ULAP 单次推理 19.9 ms、0.183 J；GR00T 在 RTX A6000 上为 284.3 ms、50.55 J。三组仿真基策略/基准中，选定工作点减少 48.8--76.7% 的 VLA 调用，保留 95.0--97.5% 基线成功率。对比 VLA-JEPA 上的加速方案，在相当成功率下较 ACT 节省约 49.2% 推理时间与 51.0% GPU 能耗，较 SP-VLA 节省 77.1% 时间与 79.9% 能耗。SO-101 实物实验在已见与未见摆放上保留 95.2--100% 成功率，推理时间减少约 47.9--58.0%，设备能耗降低 52.1--62.5%。在延迟感知的 LIBERO-Safety 仿真中，VLA-ULAP 在两个任务上比 π0.5 高 11.0 和 15.5 个百分点，同时 VLA 调用约减半。

Conclusion: VLA-ULAP 通过轻量本地预测器交错远程 VLA 调用，实现了低能耗、低延迟且高成功率的 VLA 策略执行，并改善动态任务表现。

Abstract: Billion-parameter vision--language--action (VLA) policies demand substantial onboard power, while communication delays in remote inference hinder timely responses. We propose VLA-ULAP, which interleaves remote VLA calls with an Ultra-Lightweight Local Action Predictor (ULAP). With approximately 7.4M parameters including the frozen vision encoder, ULAP combines current views, proprioception, and executed action history to predict chunks in one pass. Trained independently, it requires no VLA hidden states, online verification, or server round trips. On Jetson Orin Nano, ULAP takes 19.9 ms and 0.183 J per inference, compared with 284.3 ms and 50.55 J for GR00T on RTX A6000. Across three simulated base-policy/benchmark pairs, selected operating points remove 48.8--76.7\% of VLA calls while retaining 95.0--97.5\% of the baseline success rate. Against local VLA-acceleration alternatives on VLA-JEPA, ULAP uses an estimated 49.2\% less inference time and 51.0\% less GPU energy per successful episode than ACT at comparable success rates, and 77.1\% less time and 79.9\% less energy than SP-VLA at equal success rates. Physical SO-101 experiments retain 95.2--100\% of the baseline success rate across seen and held-out placements while reducing inference time by an estimated 47.9--58.0\% and inference-device energy by 52.1--62.5\%, based on successful-episode call counts and measured device costs. Faster responses also improve dynamic-task success rates: in latency-aware LIBERO-Safety simulation, VLA-ULAP exceeds $π_{0.5}$ by 11.0 and 15.5 percentage points on two tasks while approximately halving VLA calls.

</details>


### [118] [M$^3$P-R1: Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Model Guided Multi-Modal Motion Planning via MIP Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18669)
*Xingpeng Sun,Zherong Pan,Kai Cheng,Xindi Tang,Syed Talha Bukhari,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 M³P-R1，用强化学习微调 LLM，将多模态运动规划任务分解为 MIP 变量、约束与目标，并生成可执行的 Python 代码交由求解器求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态运动规划（M³P）需联合推理连续运动与离散模式切换，纯离散或纯连续规划器均无法处理；MIP 虽有原则性框架，但针对非凸机器人任务构建可解且基于离散化的公式通常依赖人工、领域特定，难以高效自动化。

Method: 提出 M³P-R1：用强化学习微调 LLM，将 M³P 任务分解为 MIP 变量、约束和目标；模型不直接输出答案（易幻觉），而是生成使用 MIP 优化库与约束接口的可执行 Python 代码；以求解器结果为结果驱动的奖励进行训练，学习组合模态级离散化原语并合成跨模态耦合约束。

Result: 模型能生成可执行 MIP 程序，经求解器后端执行，获得稳健且可验证的解；能够组合模态级离散化原语并合成跨模态耦合约束，处理复杂 M³P 任务。

Conclusion: 通过 RL 微调 LLM 自动构建 MIP 公式并生成可执行代码，M³P-R1 为多模态运动规划提供了可验证、求解器支持的自动化求解路径，缓解了人工构建非凸 MIP 公式的瓶颈。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Motion Planning (M$^3$P) requires joint reasoning over continuous motions and discrete mode transitions, making it difficult to solve efficiently. For instance, a bipedal robot may walk to a target location and then use its arms to grasp an object. This scenario captures both mode transitions and continuous dynamics, yielding feasible paths that neither purely discrete nor continuous planners can handle. While Mixed-Integer Programming (MIP) offers a principled framework, constructing tractable formulations for non-convex problems is typically manual and domain-specific, especially in the approximate, discretization-based MIP regime needed for non-convex robotic tasks. We propose M$^3$P-R1, a reinforcement learning method that fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to decompose M$^3$P tasks into MIP variables, constraints, and objectives. Instead of directly outputting answers, which are often prone to hallucination, the model generates executable Python code using MIP optimization libraries and constraint interfaces. This enables solver-backed execution for robust and verifiable solutions. Trained with an outcome-driven reward against the solver, M$^3$P-R1 learns to compose modality-level discretization primitives and synthesize cross-modal coupling constraints, producing executable MIP programs for complex M$^3$P tasks.

</details>


### [119] [WeaveRL: Weaving Reconstruction into Scene-Aware Fabrics for Perceptive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18685)
*Remo Steiner,Vikram Ramasamy,David Tingdahl,Sam Mady,Karl Van Wyk,Nathan Ratliff,David Recasens Lafuente,Soha Pouya,Tuur Stuyck,Alex Millane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GPU加速方法，在数千并行仿真实例的主动rollout中将场景重建为surfel集合，使策略基于传感器几何而非手工几何，在碰撞密集操作任务上优于基线并保持sim-to-real，对未见障碍更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL可让机器人习得复杂技能，但几何复杂操作的策略生成仍困难。基于避碰控制器（如geometric fabrics）学习是有前景的方向，但现有方法依赖静态、手工指定的场景表示，无法将主动、在线3D感知融入大规模并行RL训练。

Method: 提出GPU加速的场景重建方法，在数千个并行仿真实例的主动rollout过程中，将场景重建为surfel（面元）集合，使策略可直接作用于传感器-derived的几何，而非手工指定的几何表示，并与collision-avoidant控制器（geometric fabrics）结合用于并行RL训练。

Result: 在一组碰撞密集的操作任务上，surfel fabrics使策略能够处理几何复杂场景，而基于图元的基线方法失败，同时保持sim-to-real迁移能力。场景感知fabric训练的策略对测试时引入的新几何更具鲁棒性：在未见障碍下的无碰撞任务完成率从35%提升至61%。

Conclusion: 该方法证明将主动在线3D感知以surfel形式集成进大规模并行RL训练是可行且有效的，提升了复杂几何操作的策略学习与泛化能力。作者开源了重建系统、训练代码与测试数据集，以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning allows robots to acquire complex skills, but producing policies for geometrically complex manipulation remains difficult. A promising approach is to learn on top of collision-avoidant controllers, such as geometric fabrics. However, these approaches have relied on static, hand-specified representations of the scene. Integrating active, online 3D perception into massively parallel RL training has so far been inaccessible. We introduce a GPU-accelerated method that reconstructs the scene as a collection of surfels across thousands of parallel simulation instances during active rollouts. This lets policies operate over sensor-derived, rather than hand-specified, geometry. On a suite of collision-dense manipulation tasks, our surfel fabrics enable policies to tackle geometrically complex scenes where primitive-based baselines fail, while maintaining sim-to-real transfer. Furthermore, policies learned with a scene-aware fabric are more robust to the introduction of novel geometry at test time, improving collision-free task completion under unseen obstacles from 35% to 61%. We release our reconstruction system, training code and test dataset to spur research in this direction.

</details>


### [120] [Toward 3D Printable Non-Planar Electroadhesive Structures for Active Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18700)
*Mostafa A. Atalla,Carol R. Derla,Pep Canyelles Pericàs,Gijs Krijnen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索用多材料3D打印制造带打印电极图案的非平面结构，以实现电粘附。打印了平面电粘附垫作为基准，以及圆柱形非平面电粘附垫作为演示，导电叉指电极与结构一体打印。力测量表明两种几何形状均呈现电压调控调制：3 kV下平面垫约0.11–0.13 N，5 N预载下圆柱垫约0.16 N。这向具有内置电控粘附与摩擦的打印件迈出了一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电粘附垫多为平面结构，而许多实际应用需要非平面表面；若能将电极图案直接集成到打印件中，就可实现内置的可电控粘附与摩擦，从而突破传统平面电粘附垫的限制。

Method: 采用多材料3D打印，将导电叉指电极作为结构的一部分直接打印，制备平面电粘附垫（基准）与圆柱形非平面电粘附垫（演示）；随后通过法向力测量评估两种几何结构在不同电压与预载下的电粘附性能。

Result: 法向力测量显示两种几何形状均具有电压可控调制。在3 kV电压下，平面垫产生约0.11–0.13 N的法向力；在5 N预载下，圆柱形垫达到约0.16 N。

Conclusion: 多材料3D打印可用于制造带内置电极图案的非平面电粘附结构，并在圆柱形几何中实现有效的电控粘附，表明该路径有望拓展至传统平面电粘附垫之外的应用。

Abstract: This paper investigates multi-material 3D printing as a method to fabricate non-planar structures with 3D-printed electrode patterns for electroadhesion. We printed flat electroadhesion pads as a planar benchmark and cylindrical pads as a non-planar demonstration, with conductive interdigitated electrodes 3D printed as part of the structure. Normal-force measurements showed voltage-controlled modulation in both geometries. At 3 kV, the flat pads generated approximately 0.11-0.13 N, while the cylindrical pads reached approximately 0.16 N at 5 N preload. These results demonstrate a step toward printed parts with built-in, electrically controlled adhesion and friction, beyond conventional planar electroadhesion pads.

</details>


### [121] [Calibrated Probabilistic Obstruction Reasoning with Vision-Language Models for Grasping in Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18718)
*Thanh-Tuan Tran,Ngoc-Chien Chu,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Nak Young Chong,Nguyen-Viet Ha,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CPOR-Grasp，一种校准概率式遮挡推理框架，通过标定并融合VLM、深度与amodal掩码线索，构建有效遮挡图分布并边缘化，实现可认证的抓取/移除/推迟决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱场景中抓取目标需决策：直接抓取、移除阻碍物或推迟。现有方法通常固定单一遮挡图或移除策略，忽视不同场景解释间的不确定性；依赖未校准的VLM预测，且成对遮挡关系可能联合不一致；近似方法无法保证被丢弃假设对最终决策的影响。

Method: CPOR-Grasp：1) 校准并融合VLM、深度、amodal掩码线索估计遮挡概率；2) 诱导有效遮挡图的分布；3) 对这些图边缘化，计算目标可达或指定阻碍物应被移除的似然；4) 仅保留最高概率图以保证可处理性，并推导被丢弃概率质量的总变差上界，从而支持认证决策、自适应停止与有原则的推迟。

Result: 在合成与真实UNOBench场景上优于SOTA基线。Gemini Robotics骨干上校准误差从0.1416降至0.0185；图截断在99.74%决策上与精确推理一致，且用图数量少56倍。真实实验中平均成功率达77.8%，超过SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 将成对证据不确定性传播到动作决策，并以总变差界保证截断近似的可靠性，使遮挡推理兼具精度、效率与可认证性，在仿真与真实抓取任务上均取得领先表现。

Abstract: Retrieving a target from clutter requires deciding whether to grasp the target, remove a blocker, or defer. Existing methods typically commit to a single obstruction graph or removal strategy, ignoring uncertainty across alternative scene interpretations. They also rely on miscalibrated vision-language model (VLM) predictions and can produce pairwise obstruction relations that are jointly inconsistent. Moreover, current approximations provide no guarantees about the impact of discarded hypotheses on the final decision. We propose CPOR-Grasp, a calibrated probabilistic obstruction-reasoning framework that propagates uncertainty from pairwise evidence to action decisions. CPOR-Grasp calibrates and fuses VLM, depth, and amodal-mask cues to estimate obstruction probabilities, induces a distribution over valid obstruction graphs, and marginalizes over these graphs to compute the likelihood that the target is accessible or that a given blocker should be removed. To make inference tractable, it retains only the highest-probability graphs and derives a total-variation bound on the discarded probability mass, enabling certified decisions, adaptive stopping, and principled deferral. On synthetic and real UNOBench scenes, CPOR-Grasp outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Calibration error decreases from 0.1416 to 0.0185 on the Gemini Robotics backbone, while graph truncation matches exact inference on 99.74\% of decisions using 56 times fewer graphs. In real-world experiments, CPOR-Grasp achieves a 77.8\% average success rate, surpassing SOTA baselines.

</details>


### [122] [PASSAGE: Scaling Scene-Aligned Motion Learning for Perceptive Humanoid Traversal in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18732)
*Yuxuan Ma,Zicheng Zeng,Chunlin Peng,Zhoujian Li,Zetong Zhao,Zhikai Zhang,Yunrui Lian,Han Xue,Sikai Liang,Weiyi Zhu,Mulin Chen,Chenghuai Lin,Jiayu Zeng,Yanwei An,Songan Zhang,Jiayuan Gu,Jilong Wang,Jingbo Wang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PASSAGE 是一个感知条件化的规划器-跟踪器框架，使类人机器人无需特定障碍策略或技能标注即可在杂乱场景中自主选择并组合越障行为，并在仿真与实物上验证了跨未见几何的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人虽具备跨、挤、蹲等越障动作能力，但从机载感知中学习选择与协调这些行为仍很困难。现有方法多依赖任务特定的强化学习目标或人工整理的动作库，导致行为覆盖范围扩大时成本高昂。

Method: 采用 VR 与惯性动捕采集 100 小时、覆盖 1500 个杂乱场景的场景对齐人体运动数据；条件流匹配规划器依据运动历史、局部目标点与机器人中心多层高程图生成短时域参考轨迹；感知式全身跟踪器以 50 Hz 结合几何反馈执行；实时分块（chunking）提升块间一致性；在冻结跟踪器下对规划器做 RL 后训练以改善闭环性能。

Result: 仿真中通过组件消融量化各阶段贡献；三个独立训练种子下，数据从 6 小时增至 100 小时使留出场景的平均无接触成功率由 48.1% 提升至 68.9%，加入经验证场景增强的最终模型达 70.3%；全机载系统在 Jetson AGX Orin 上集成第一视角 3D 激光雷达感知、在线占据建图、6.25 Hz 规划与 50 Hz 控制，在 50 个未见真实布局中无需预建地图或离机计算即可完成穿越。

Conclusion: 单一规划器-跟踪器组合即可在未见几何上选择并组合越障行为，无需技能标注或障碍专用策略；数据规模与场景增强显著提升泛化性，且系统可完全机载实时运行。

Abstract: Humanoid robots can step over, squeeze past, and duck under obstacles, but learning to select and coordinate these behaviors from onboard perception remains challenging. Many existing approaches rely on task-specific reinforcement-learning objectives or curated motion libraries, making broad behavioral coverage costly. We present PASSAGE, a perception-conditioned planner--tracker framework for humanoid traversal. Using virtual reality and inertial motion capture, we collect 100 h of scene-aligned human motion across 1,500 cluttered scenes. A conditional flow-matching planner generates short-horizon references from motion history, a local destination, and a robot-centric multi-layer elevation map, while a perceptive whole-body tracker executes them at 50 Hz with geometric feedback. Real-time chunking promotes inter-chunk consistency, and planner-side RL post-training under the frozen tracker further improves closed-loop performance. Without skill annotations or obstacle-specific policies, one planner--tracker pair selects and composes traversal behaviors across unseen geometries. In simulation, component ablations quantify the contribution of each stage. Across three independent training seeds, scaling captured data from 6 to 100 h increases mean contact-free success from 48.1% to 68.9% on held-out scenes, while the final model with validated scene augmentation reaches 70.3%. The fully onboard system integrates egocentric 3D LiDAR perception, online occupancy mapping, 6.25 Hz planning, and 50 Hz control on a Jetson AGX Orin; tests across 50 unseen physical layouts demonstrate traversal without prebuilt maps or offboard computation.

</details>


### [123] [Active perception for robotic harvesting: 3D reconstruction and localisation of tomatoes hidden within clusters in a Mediterranean greenhouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18738)
*Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,Rowan Border,José C. Moreno,José L. Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向地中海温室成簇番茄的3D重建与果实精确定位流水线，通过NBV主动规划、SOR滤波、区域生长分割、DBSCAN遮挡果实恢复与3D位姿估计，实现对严重遮挡果实的识别与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地中海温室密集农业的机器人采摘面临植株几何结构复杂与果实遮挡严重的问题。已有研究多针对苹果、甜椒、桃等孤立生长的果实，而固定传感器安装方式难以探测成簇蔬菜中被遮挡在可见表面之后的果实，这构成本研究的核心挑战。

Method: 提出五阶段顺序流水线：i）利用AgriSEE Next Best View（NBV）主动规划器进行点云采集；ii）采用Statistical Outlier Removal（SOR）进行随机噪声滤波；iii）使用Region Growing（RG）进行曲面分类与分割；iv）通过DBSCAN对遮挡果实进行分离与恢复；v）完成3D位姿（位置与朝向）估计。该方法可提取完整簇几何结构。

Result: 在多种不同遮挡程度的模拟场景中评估，仿真框架经真实条件严格验证，系统取得超过90%的精确率、平均82.8%的召回率与80.7%的平均IoU（mIoU）；质心估计重复性高，RMSE仅为4.2 mm。

Conclusion: 该方法能可靠识别部分被遮挡的番茄，验证了其在自主采摘作业中的技术可行性与高精度，为成簇蔬菜的机器人采摘提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Automating robotic harvesting in intensive agriculture within Mediterranean greenhouses requires overcoming significant challenges related to the geometric complexity of plants and occluded fruits. Although existing literature offers solutions targeting crops that grow in isolation (e.g., apples, sweet peppers, or peaches), the fundamental challenge lies in cluster-growing vegetables, where fixed sensors mounted on robotic systems fail to detect fruits hidden behind the visible surface. To address this limitation, this study presents a comprehensive pipeline for the 3D reconstruction and precise localization of each fruit within a cluster, including heavily occluded instances. The proposed methodology is structured into five sequential stages: i) point cloud acquisition using the AgriSEE Next Best View (NBV) active planner; ii) stochastic noise filtering via Statistical Outlier Removal (SOR); iii) surface classification and segmentation using Region Growing (RG); iv) isolation and recovery of occluded fruits through Density-Based Spatial Clustering of Applications with Noise (DBSCAN); and v) 3D pose estimation (position and orientation). This approach extracts the complete cluster geometry, ensuring the reliable identification of partially hidden tomatoes. Evaluated across multiple scenarios with varying occlusion levels within a simulation framework rigorously validated against real-world conditions, the system achieves a precision exceeding 90\%, an average recall of 82.8\%, and a mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 80.7\%. Furthermore, it demonstrates high repeatability in centroid estimation with a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of merely 4.2~mm, verifying its technical feasibility and high accuracy for autonomous harvesting operations.

</details>


### [124] [QMSR: Query-Conditioned Mask-wise Expert Routing for Robust Open-Vocabulary Underwater Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18752)
*Fuming Zhang,Dongyue Huang,Junjie Wen,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出QMSR——查询条件化的掩码级专家路由框架，为每个查询-候选对自适应选择UIE专家并预测Raw-Expert融合强度，在水下开放词汇检索中NDCG@10较共享路由器提升17.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下开放词汇目标检索在复杂环境中仍具挑战性。固定式水下图像增强（UIE）策略作为统一预处理步骤，其检索效果甚至可能不如原始（Raw）表示，说明增强不应笼统地应用于所有样本，需要一种随查询与候选自适应调整的增强方式。

Method: QMSR框架：对每个查询-候选对选择一个预训练UIE专家，并预测连续的Raw-Expert融合强度，从而在保留有用Raw语义的同时实现自适应增强。训练阶段采用特权排序oracle提供专家选择与融合强度监督，并通过退火软路由松弛来优化硬Top-1路由策略。

Result: 相比图像-查询共享路由器，QMSR在NDCG@10上提升17.6%；持续优于固定UIE策略，并在留出的查询类别上依然有效。

Conclusion: 查询条件化、候选特定的增强路由对水下开放词汇检索有效，验证了自适应增强路由优于固定增强策略的结论。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object retrieval remains challenging in complex underwater environments. Although underwater image enhancement (UIE) can improve visual quality, fixed UIE strategies may even underperform the Raw representation in retrieval, indicating that enhancement should not be applied as a uniform preprocessing step. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{QMSR}, a query-conditioned mask-wise expert routing framework for underwater open-vocabulary retrieval. Specifically, QMSR selects one pretrained UIE expert for each query--candidate pair and predicts a continuous Raw--Expert fusion strength, enabling adaptive enhancement while preserving useful Raw semantics. During training, a privileged ranking oracle provides expert-selection and fusion-strength supervision, while an annealed soft-routing relaxation facilitates optimization of the hard Top-1 routing policy. Experiments show that QMSR improves NDCG@10 by 17.6\% over an image--query shared router, while consistently outperforming fixed UIE strategies and remaining effective on held-out query categories. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of query-conditioned and candidate-specific enhancement routing for underwater open-vocabulary retrieval.

</details>


### [125] [Gated Residual Body-Hand Coordination for Whole-Body Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18763)
*Ruiming Wu,Shuang Li,Liding Zhang,Alois Knoll,Zhaopeng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出门控残差协调框架，冻结运动跟踪与手部重定向模块并对输出施加有界修正，在保持全身跟踪的同时显著降低手腕与指尖几何误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身人形机器人遥操作通常由运动跟踪策略与独立的手部重定向器组合而成，但两者独立生成的指令无法显式保持身体—手部几何关系，导致双臂交互中相对手腕姿态与指尖位置出现不匹配。

Method: 提出门控残差协调框架：保持两个模块冻结，仅对其输出施加有界修正。由运动条件动作门在关节组间分配修正权限，由依赖参考几何的奖励门在训练中强调相关交互目标。为在 Agile One 上建立标称身体控制器，引入多姿态形态标定，联合估计三轴尺度与末端执行器局部偏移，并进行分阶段运动数据整理以训练基于 SONIC 的跟踪器。残差策略仅使用人体运动参考、初始指令与机器人本体感知，不使用显式物体或接触观测。

Result: 在仿真中，于留出的 GRAB 运动上，相比直接组合，手腕与指尖几何误差降低 39.2%–56.3%；在 AMASS 上保持全身跟踪，成功率由无残差协调时的 89.03% 变为 89.29%（含残差协调）。消融实验刻画了奖励门、自适应修正权限以及身体与手部分离修正头的贡献。

Conclusion: 门控残差协调可在不牺牲全身跟踪性能的前提下有效改善身体—手部几何一致性，各协调组件均具必要性。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid teleoperation commonly combines a motion-tracking policy with a separate dexterous-hand retargeter. However, independently generated commands do not explicitly preserve body-hand geometric relations, leading to mismatches in relative wrist poses and fingertip positions during bimanual interaction. We present a gated residual coordination framework that keeps both modules frozen and applies bounded corrections to their outputs. A motion-conditioned action gate allocates correction authority across joint groups, while reference-geometry-dependent reward gates emphasize relevant interaction objectives during training. To establish the nominal body controller on Agile One, we introduce multi-pose morphology calibration that jointly estimates triaxial scales and effector-local offsets, together with staged motion dataset curation for training a SONIC-based tracker. The residual policy uses human motion references, initial commands, and robot proprioception without explicit object or contact observations. In simulation, it reduces wrist and fingertip geometry errors by 39.2-56.3% over direct composition on held-out GRAB motions, while preserving whole-body tracking on AMASS, with success rates of 89.03% without residual coordination and 89.29% with it. Ablations characterize the contributions of reward gating, adaptive correction authority, and separate body and hand correction heads.

</details>


### [126] [TRACER: Adaptive Multi-Robot Social Navigation via Joint Human-Response Prediction and Interaction-Aware Replanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18776)
*Lan Hu,Minghui Liwang,Wenbo Zhu,Xinlei Yi,Wei Gong,Yiguang Hong,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACER是一个双向滚动时域框架，通过分离单体效应与成对交互的响应模型，并将执行后的观测用于更新身份绑定的响应模式信念，从而闭环预测与自适应，提升多机器人社交导航表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人社交导航方法通常将动作条件预测、多机器人规划与在线自适应分开处理，缺乏统一机制来建模机器人-实体联合交互，也难以从已执行交互结果中更新后续决策，因此需要一个新的闭环框架。

Method: 提出TRACER双向滚动时域框架：用逐实体概率响应模型评估候选轨迹，该模型将个体机器人效应与非可加成对交互分离；执行所选轨迹前缀后，利用同步观测到的响应更新持久且身份绑定的潜在响应模式信念；更新后的信念再在概率安全与响应感知代价准则下指导后续候选评估。

Result: 实验表明：(i) 相比容量匹配的可加预测器，TRACER更准确捕捉非可加多机器人交互效应；(ii) 持久且身份一致的证据改善响应预测与下游重规划；(iii) 在SocialGym2多机器人社交导航基准上，完整TRACER框架相较独立机器人基线提升无碰撞完成率。

Conclusion: TRACER通过将交互建模、预测与在线自适应统一为闭环滚动时域过程，验证了身份绑定持久信念与非可加交互建模对多机器人社交导航的有效性，并在无碰撞完成指标上优于独立基线。

Abstract: Multi-robot navigation in human-shared spaces is inherently interactive: coordinated robot motions influence how nearby entities respond, while those responses provide valuable information for subsequent robot decisions. However, existing methods typically address action-conditioned prediction, multi-robot planning, or online adaptation separately, and therefore lack a unified mechanism for modeling joint robot-entity interactions and adapting future decisions from executed interaction outcomes. To address this gap, we propose TRACER, a bi-directional receding-horizon framework that closes the loop between prediction and adaptation. TRACER evaluates candidate (i.e., alternative feasible future motion plans for the robot team) trajectories using a per-entity probabilistic response model that separates individual-robot effects from non-additive pairwise interactions; after executing the selected trajectory prefix, it updates persistent identity-bound beliefs over latent response modes using the synchronized observed responses. These updated beliefs then guide subsequent candidate evaluation under probabilistic safety and response-aware cost criteria. Experiments show that (i) TRACER more accurately captures non-additive multi-robot interaction effects than a capacity-matched additive predictor, (ii) persistent identity-consistent evidence improves response prediction and downstream replanning, and (iii) the complete TRACER framework improves collision-free completion over an independent-robot baseline on the SocialGym2 multi-robot social-navigation benchmark.

</details>


### [127] [AdaGeoVLN: Selective Geometry Across Representation Depth and Navigation Time for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18789)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,Danh Vinh Le,Nguyen Viet Tri Pham,The-Anh Nguyen,Zhirui Dai,Yiyu Chen,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出流式VLN框架，在表示深度与导航时间两个维度解决GFM特征利用与历史几何证据保留问题，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上以单一RGB流取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航需在对齐语言与视觉观测的同时保持时序空间理解。几何基础模型(GFM)暴露了层级中的中间表示，但导航策略应如何使用这些特征、以及如何保留历史几何证据，尚属未解问题。

Method: 提出流式VLN框架，含两个核心机制：(1) 分层GFM-VLM融合，将早、中、后期GFM表示分别耦合到策略的连续阶段，而非重复注入终端特征；(2) 导航感知GFM记忆，按指令相关性、几何置信度和转场新颖度，在逐层有界预算下保留历史VGGT全局注意力KV状态，在融合前为后续观测提供几何上下文。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上，仅用单一RGB流、无需额外导航专用外部数据即取得强性能。受控消融显示：在相同融合位置，多深度耦合显著优于重复的终端特征注入；有界导航感知保留在维持导航性能的同时，相对更大内存的时序保留大幅降低GFM-KV内存。

Conclusion: 应联合考察暴露给策略的几何表示与为未来推理保留的历史证据。代码将在接收后于 https://humanoid-research.github.io/adageovln/ 发布。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation requires aligning language with visual observations while maintaining spatial understanding over time. Geometry foundation models (GFMs) expose intermediate representations throughout their hierarchy, but how navigation policies should use these features and retain historical geometric evidence remains unresolved. We introduce \method{}, a streaming VLN framework that addresses these questions across \textbf{representation depth} and \textbf{navigation time}. Hierarchical GFM--VLM fusion couples earlier, intermediate, and later GFM representations to successive policy stages instead of repeatedly injecting a terminal feature. Navigation-aware GFM memory retains historical VGGT global-attention KV states according to instruction relevance, geometric confidence, and transition novelty under a bounded per-layer budget. Retained states provide geometric context for subsequent observations before fusion with the policy. Across R2R-CE and RxR-CE, \method{} achieves strong performance using a single RGB stream without additional navigation-specific external data. Controlled ablations show that multi-depth coupling substantially outperforms repeated terminal-feature injection at matched fusion locations. Bounded navigation-aware retention preserves navigation performance while considerably reducing GFM-KV memory relative to larger-memory temporal retention. These findings support jointly examining the geometric representations exposed to the policy and the historical evidence retained for future inference. Code will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-research.github.io/adageovln/.

</details>


### [128] [Asymptotically Optimal Multi-Robot Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18813)
*Thi Thuy Ngan Duong,Cheuk Tung Shadow Yiu,Rahul Shome,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究多机器人任务与运动规划（MR-TAMP），刻画了不同任务转换涉及不同机器人子集所导致的"转换结构"，给出全局渐近最优性的充分条件，并提出结合增量个体路标图与隐式张量积搜索的高效渐近最优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有TAMP渐近最优算法难以直接扩展到多机器人场景：不同任务转换涉及不同机器人子集，对复合构型空间施加不同维度的约束，因此规划器不仅要在每个任务模式内优化运动，还必须充分探索连接各模式的各类转换。作者希望刻画该转换结构并建立MR-TAMP全局渐近最优性的充分条件。

Method: 首先刻画MR-TAMP的转换结构，给出全局渐近最优的充分条件：需对相关转换保持持续覆盖（persistent coverage），并在各连通可行域内渐近改进运动规划。基于这些条件，提出高效渐近最优MR-TAMP算法：融合演化的个体机器人路标图与隐式张量积搜索，避免显式构建复合路标图；并采用条件转换采样、惰性碰撞检测以及模式级与解级引导以提升有限时间规划效率，同时保持持续探索。

Result: 所提出框架在保留个体机器人运动规划结构的同时，为多机器人操作提供渐近最优性保证；通过条件转换采样、惰性碰撞检测和模式/解级引导，在保持持续探索的前提下提升了有限时间规划效率。

Conclusion: 该工作将TAMP的渐近最优性保证扩展到多机器人场景，明确了转换覆盖的要求，并给出兼具渐近最优性与实用效率的MR-TAMP算法框架。

Abstract: Multi-robot task and motion planning (MR-TAMP) requires jointly reasoning about discrete task decisions and continuous collision-free motions of multiple interacting robots. Although asymptotically optimal algorithms have been developed for task and motion planning, extending these guarantees to the multi-robot setting introduces an important challenge: different task transitions may involve different subsets of robots and therefore impose constraints of different dimensions on the composite configuration space. Consequently, an asymptotically optimal planner must not only optimize motion within each task mode, but also ensure sufficient exploration of the different types of transitions connecting them. We characterize this transition structure and establish sufficient conditions for global asymptotic optimality in MR-TAMP, requiring persistent coverage of relevant transitions and asymptotically improving motion planning within connected feasible regions. Based on these conditions, we develop an efficient asymptotically optimal MR-TAMP algorithm that combines evolving individual-robot roadmaps with implicit tensor-product search, avoiding explicit construction of the composite roadmap. The planner further employs conditional transition sampling, lazy collision checking, and mode- and solution-level guidance to improve finite-time planning efficiency while retaining persistent exploration. The resulting framework provides asymptotic optimality guarantees for multi-robot manipulation while efficiently exploiting the structure of individual-robot motion planning.

</details>


### [129] [SEAM: Submap-Anchored Evidence for Lifelong LiDAR Mapping under Trajectory Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18819)
*Kyuwon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAM——一种基于LiDAR的终身建图框架，通过子地图级锚点优化、动态物体移除与变化检测实现高效高精度建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统终身建图方法依赖单一锚点跨越整个会话，当轨迹被新会话修改时需从头重算，且会话间回环边几何可靠性不足易导致对齐错误，同时难以精确区分动态物体与环境变化。

Method: SEAM采用子地图级锚点优化轨迹以生成证据，通过子地图级重投影使证据在轨迹被新会话修改后仍可用，避免全局重算；使用基于DOP的置信度度量抑制几何不可靠的会话间回环边；并引入方向性体素级证据模型，考虑随射线方向变化的占据模式以实现方向感知的证据分离。

Result: 在真实建筑工地数据集和长期多会话数据集上的实验表明，SEAM相比现有方法实现了更高的精度和更快的处理速度。

Conclusion: 子地图级锚点与重投影机制、DOP置信度回环抑制以及方向感知体素证据模型共同提升了终身建图的精度与效率，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: We propose SEAM, a LiDAR-based lifelong mapping framework. Instead of relying on a single anchor spanning the entire session, SEAM generates evidence based on a trajectory optimized with submap-level anchors, and performs dynamic object removal and change detection. Through submap-level reprojection, the generated evidence remains usable even if the trajectory is subsequently modified by a new session, eliminating the need to recompute the entire process from scratch. SEAM suppresses geometrically unreliable inter-session loop edges using a DOP-based confidence measure. Suppressing unreliable loop edges prevents alignment errors. SEAM also uses a directional voxel-wise evidence model. The model accounts for occupancy patterns that vary with ray direction. Direction-aware evidence separates dynamic objects from environmental changes more precisely. Experiments on a real construction-site dataset and a long-term multi-session dataset show that SEAM achieves higher accuracy and faster processing than existing methods.

</details>


### [130] [KINO: A Keyframe Interface for VLM Planning and Whole-Body Control in Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18869)
*Sitong Chen,Fatemeh Zargarbashi,Jin Cheng,Tianxu An,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层框架，以运动关键帧作为VLM规划与RL控制之间的中间表示，实现人形机器人全身移动操作，仿真与Unitree G1实机验证成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形移动操作要求机器人既能理解任务指令与场景语义，又要执行协调的全身运动。直接由VLM高层规划驱动底层RL控制存在表示鸿沟，难以兼顾语义理解与精细全身动作生成。

Method: 采用分层框架，以关键帧作为VLM规划与RL控制的中间表示：每个关键帧指定目标全身机器人位姿，必要时指定物体位姿。VLM根据语言指令、场景观测与执行反馈，从预定义关键帧库中选择任务相关关键帧；关键帧被重定向到当前场景以适配物体位姿与尺寸；再由关键帧条件的全身策略输出关节级动作以达成目标。此外提出基于显著性的关键帧采样策略用于底层策略训练。

Result: 基于显著性的关键帧采样策略在使用稀疏VLM关键帧时，将端到端任务成功率从44%提升至92%。在仿真及Unitree G1人形机器人上完成物体拾取、搬运与放置任务，系统可执行单手与双手操作，并能泛化到训练参考数据之外的放置位置。

Conclusion: 以关键帧为中间表示的分层框架有效衔接VLM语义规划与RL全身控制，配合显著性采样显著提升稀疏关键帧条件下的任务成功率，并在真实人形平台上验证了泛化能力。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires robots to interpret task instructions and scene semantics while executing coordinated whole-body motions. We propose a hierarchical framework that uses motion keyframes as an intermediate representation between Vision-Language Model (VLM) planning and Reinforcement Learning (RL) control. Each keyframe specifies a target whole-body robot pose and, when applicable, an object pose. Given a language instruction, scene observations, and execution feedback, the VLM selects successive task-relevant keyframes from a predefined library. The selected keyframes are retargeted to the current scene to account for object poses and dimensions. A keyframe-conditioned whole-body policy then generates joint-level actions to reach these goals. We introduce a saliency-based keyframe sampling strategy for low-level policy training that improves end-to-end task success rate from 44% to 92% when using sparse VLM keyframes. We evaluate our framework on object pickup, transport, and placement tasks in simulation and on a Unitree G1 humanoid. The system successfully performs both one- and two-handed manipulation and generalises to placement locations beyond the training reference data.

</details>


### [131] [Body-Motion Control of a Simulated Aerial Swarm from a First-Person View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18881)
*Yang Chen,Darius Giannoli,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种上身姿态映射的FPV集群遥操作界面，将躯干倾角、手部位置、头部旋转映射为五个连续指令维度，并在14人被试内实验中与常规遥控器对比；结果显示身体动作控制使完成时间缩短19.4%、质心路径长度减少7.0%、路径更直接、指令波动降低88.8%，但体力需求更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视角（FPV）下的空中集群遥操作要求操作者同时协调集群的整体平移、观察方向和编队间距。常规遥控器在同时、连续地控制这些多维指令时存在操作负担与效率问题，因此需要一种更直观、能与人类自然身体运动耦合的交互界面。

Method: 设计一种上身交互界面，将躯干倾角、手部位置和头部旋转映射为五个连续指令维度，并为每位被试校准中立姿态与运动范围。采用被试内实验设计，14名被试分别使用该身体动作界面和常规遥控器，在三维障碍赛道中操控15个智能体的仿真集群。除任务绩效外，还测量了交付指令的变化程度与并发指令变化频率；作者指出这些指令指标刻画的是包含校准与滤波差异在内的完整界面。

Result: 相比常规遥控器，身体动作控制使任务完成时间减少19.4%，集群质心路径长度减少7.0%，路径直接性提高；交付指令变化降低88.8%，并发指令变化更频繁。在门中心对齐误差、集群收拢收益、碰撞或断连次数、整体工作负荷与可用性方面未检测到差异。所有被试均报告身体动作控制的体力需求更高。

Conclusion: 所实现的界面以更高的体力需求为代价，提升了FPV集群导航效率；其在导航效率与指令稳定性上的优势未伴随任务安全性与可用性的下降。

Abstract: First-person-view (FPV) teleoperation of aerial swarms requires an operator to coordinate collective translation, viewing direction, and formation spacing. We present an upper-body interface that maps torso inclination, hand position, and head rotation to five continuous command dimensions. Neutral postures and motion ranges are calibrated for each participant. In a within-subject study, 14 participants navigated a simulated 15-agent swarm through three-dimensional obstacle courses using this interface and a conventional transmitter. Body-motion control reduced completion time by 19.4% and centroid path length by 7.0%, and increased path directness. Delivered-command variation was 88.8% lower, and concurrent command changes were more frequent. These command measures characterize the complete interfaces, which differed in calibration and filtering. No differences were detected in gate-centering error, collection yield, crash or disconnection counts, overall workload, or usability. All participants reported higher physical demand with body-motion control. The implemented interface therefore improved FPV navigation efficiency at the cost of greater physical demand.

</details>


### [132] [SOL-SLAM: Inverse Compositional Gauss-Newton Direct Registration for Fast Sonar-Only Local SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18893)
*Kalvik Jakkala,Jason O'Kane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖前视声呐（FLS）的稠密直接配准局部SLAM方法，通过逆合成高斯-牛顿优化实现实时运行，在平移误差与里程计精度上优于稀疏特征基线，并接近多传感器融合方案，且在资源受限嵌入式平台上完成AUV实地验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下导航通常依赖复杂昂贵的多模态传感器套件以追求全局SLAM精度，但粗导航、避障等局部反应行为仅需局部一致性，理论上仅用前视声呐即可实现。然而该方向研究不足，且现有声学SLAM框架多依赖稀疏特征提取，丢弃了大量本就稀疏的声学回波信息。

Method: 提出稠密直接配准方法，将完整的声学强度扫描与递归更新的局部地图对齐；采用逆合成高斯-牛顿优化策略降低计算开销，实现实时执行。

Result: 相比稀疏关键点基线，该方法在平移误差上显著改善，在较大位移间隔下保持稳定的亚米级跟踪精度；其里程计性能可与FLS、DVL和IMU多传感器融合管线相当，在特征丰富环境中摆脱了对昂贵载荷的依赖。

Conclusion: 通过在AUV实地试验中于嵌入式资源受限计算机上运行完整局部SLAM流程，验证了该方法的实际适用性。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater navigation typically relies on complex and expensive multi-modal sensor suites designed to prioritize global Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) accuracy. However, local reactive behaviors such as coarse navigation and obstacle avoidance require only local consistency---a capability that should be feasible using only a Forward-Looking Sonar (FLS), yet remains largely unaddressed, leaving a critical gap in FLS-only local SLAM.
  Moreover, existing acoustic SLAM frameworks predominantly rely on sparse feature extraction methods that discard substantial portions of the already information-sparse acoustic returns. To overcome these limitations, this work introduces a dense direct registration approach that aligns full acoustic intensity scans to a recursively updated local map. Real-time execution is achieved via an Inverse Compositional Gauss-Newton optimization strategy that minimizes computational overhead.
  Experimental evaluations show that this dense method yields significant improvements on translation error compared to sparse keypoint baselines, maintaining stable sub-meter tracking precision over wide displacement gaps. Moreover, this approach delivers odometry performance comparable to multi-sensor fusion pipelines (FLS, DVL, and IMU), bypassing expensive payload dependencies in feature-rich environments. We validate real-world applicability through AUV field trials, running the full local SLAM approach onboard an embedded, resource-constrained computer.

</details>


### [133] [Examining the Difference in Human Behavior Between Virtual and Real-World Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18900)
*Sean Dallas,Absalat Getachew,Motaz AbuHijleh,Andrea Macklem-Zabel,Douglas Zytko,Mark Brudnak,Wing-Yue Geoffrey Louie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究比较人机团队（HRT）场景在虚拟与真实环境中的表现差异。混合方法结果显示：两种模态下工作量存在显著差异，且在策略、机器人心理模型与信任类型上也有不同。虚拟实验用于研究人机交互时，其效度受人类行为一致性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界中制作原型并评估人机团队（HRT）场景成本高昂，因此虚拟仿真常被用作替代方案，以研究人机交互中的用户感知、行为与绩效。然而，虚拟实验的效度取决于人在真实与虚拟世界之间行为的一致性。本文的动机正是考察这种跨模态的人类行为差异。

Method: 开展了一项用户研究，将同一HRT场景分别置于虚拟环境与真实环境中执行，比较人类行为差异。采用混合方法，同时考察团队绩效与人因指标，包括定量测量与定性分析。

Result: 定量结果显示两种模态之间在工作量（workload）上存在显著差异。定性分析进一步拓展了定量结果，发现参与者在策略、对机器人的心理模型、以及对机器人的信任类型上，均因模态不同而存在差异。

Conclusion: 真实与虚拟环境下的人类行为并不完全一致：除工作量差异外，策略、心理模型与信任类型也受模态影响。因此，将虚拟仿真作为HRT研究的替代方案时需谨慎，其效度受跨模态行为一致性限制。

Abstract: Prototyping and evaluating human-robot teaming (HRT) scenarios in the real-world is costly. Virtual simulation of HRT scenarios has been adopted as an alternative to conducting user studies in the real-world to investigate user perceptions, behaviors, and performance during human-robot interactions. The consistency of human behavior between the real and virtual-worlds is integral to the validity of utilizing such virtual experimentation. This paper presents a user study examining the difference in human behavior between an HRT scenario conducted in a virtual vs real environment. We employed mixed-methods to examine team performance and human factors during the HRT. Our quantitative results showed a significant difference in workload between the two modalities. Our qualitative analysis expanded on the quantitative results and found differences in participants' strategy, their mental model of robots, and the type of trust they had for robots between modalities.

</details>


### [134] [CaSCo: Cascade-Aware Soft-Collision Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18910)
*Shivaram Kumar,Gaoyuan Liu,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO
