  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN训练本质上是一类非凸、非光滑的多重复合优化问题，现有方法多仅能保证收敛到一阶稳定点，难以刻画二阶最优性，且每次迭代计算代价较高。因此需要建立一阶与二阶d-稳定点之间的联系，并设计兼顾收敛保证与计算效率的算法。

Method: 首先建立易于验证的条件，保证问题的近似一阶d-稳定点即为近似二阶d-稳定点；据此提出序贯正则化分段仿射算法SRPA，每次迭代最小化一个正则化分段仿射函数，并引入主动集策略降低单次迭代计算开销。

Result: 在给定二阶d-稳定条件的基础上，证明了SRPA的全局收敛性，即收敛到二阶d-稳定点，并获得达到ε-近似二阶d-稳定点的复杂度上界O(ε^-2)。在合成数据集与真实数据集上的RNN训练实验表明，SRPA相较当前先进算法具有更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: SRPA为RNN训练中的非凸非光滑多重复合优化提供了具有二阶稳定性和复杂度保证的求解框架，并通过实验验证了其有效性与竞争力。

Abstract: This paper focuses on a class of nonconvex nonsmooth multicomposite optimization problems for training RNNs (Recurrent Neural Networks). We first establish easily verifiable conditions under which an approximate first-order d-stationary point of the problem is guaranteed to be an approximate second-order d-stationary point. Subsequently, we propose a sequential regularized piecewise affine algorithm, SRPA, that minimizes a regularized piecewise affine function at each iteration, where the idea of active-set strategy is incorporated to reduce the computational cost per iteration. Leveraging the aforementioned conditions for second-order d-stationarity, we establish the global convergence of SRPA to second-order d-stationary points and the complexity bound of $ \mathcal{O}(ε^{-2}) $ for obtaining an $ ε$-approximate second-order d-stationary point. Finally, numerical experiments for training RNNs on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the promising performance of SRPA compared with state-of-the-art algorithms.

</details>


### [176] [A Robust Perceptron Cycling Theorem and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18342)
*Tobias Harks*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文证明了经典感知机循环定理的稳健变体，给出加性轨迹的有界性判据，并将其应用于仿射强单调变分不等式的Frank-Wolfe算法，得到 O(k^{-1}) 的最后迭代范数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Block–Levin 感知机循环定理仅适用于更新与当前状态内积非正的有限集合修正序列。作者希望放宽条件（允许 B-近似最小化、非对称正定算子），并推广 Bach 等人关于二次 Frank–Wolfe/herding 的保证到非对称仿射算子情形。

Method: 考虑加性轨迹 z_{k+1}=z_k+u_k，增量取自有限集合 U⊂E，0∈conv U；设 A:E→E 具有正定对称部分（不要求对称），B≥0。假设每个 u_k 是 u↦⟨Az_k,u⟩ 在 U 上的 B-近似最小化点，证明 sup_k ‖z_k‖ ≤ C(1+‖z_0‖+B)，其中 C 仅依赖 E,U,A。随后将该界应用于多面体上仿射强单调变分不等式的调和顶点回归 Frank–Wolfe。

Result: 证明了轨迹范数的统一有界性，得到调和顶点回归 Frank–Wolfe 在相对内点解情形下的 O(k^{-1}) 最后迭代范数界，并将 Bach 等（2012）的二次 Frank–Wolfe/herding 保证推广至非对称仿射算子。

Conclusion: 该稳健循环定理为近似更新下轨迹有界性提供了统一工具，其算法推论扩展了既有收敛保证的适用范围（非对称算子），并可应用于强稳定线性种群博弈的经验最优响应、二次正则双线性鞍点问题以及具强制仿射非对称成本映射的交通分配。

Abstract: The classical perceptron cycling theorem of Block and Levin \cite{BlockLevin1970} bounds correction sequences whose selected updates come from a finite set and have nonpositive inner product with the current state. We prove a robust variant for additive trajectories $z_{k+1}=z_k+u_k$, for all integers $k\geq0$, with increments in a finite set $U\subset E$, where $E$ is a finite-dimensional real inner-product space and $0\in\conv U$. Let $A\colon E\to E$ have positive-definite symmetric part, without requiring symmetry, and let $B\geq0$. If each $u_k$ is a $B$-approximate minimizer of $u\mapsto\ip{Az_k}{u}$ over $U$, then $\sup_{k\geq0}\norm{z_k}\leq C(1+\norm{z_0}+B)$, with $C=C(E,U,A)$ independent of the initial state, $B$, and all admissible update choices. Our central algorithmic consequence is an $O(k^{-1})$ last-iterate norm bound for harmonic vertex-returning Frank--Wolfe for affine strongly monotone variational inequalities on polytopes with relatively interior solutions. It extends the quadratic Frank--Wolfe/herding guarantee of Bach, Lacoste-Julien, and Obozinski \cite[Section~4.2]{BachEtAl2012} to nonsymmetric affine operators. We apply this bound to empirical best responses in strongly stable linear population games, quadratically regularized bilinear saddle problems, and traffic assignment with coercive affine asymmetric cost maps.

</details>


### [177] [A Parameter-Free Adaptive Reflected Gradient Method for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18355)
*Yekini Shehu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究单调Lipschitz变分不等式VI(C,B)的单次评估迭代法，建立弱收敛性、自适应步长规则及R-线性收敛率，并给出锐利步长上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对单调Lipschitz算子的变分不等式，现有方法常需已知Lipschitz常数或额外评估，且收敛性与步长范围的理论刻画不够精确。作者希望构造无需L、无需额外评估的自适应步长规则，并给出精确的耗散预算与锐利步长上界。

Method: 采用每次迭代一次评估的格式 x_{k+1}=P_C(x_k-λB(u_k))，其中 u_k=x_k+θ_k(x_k-x_{k-1})+β_k(x_k-u_{k-1})，θ_k+β_k=1。对常数步长与可和滤波(∑β_k<∞)利用Lyapunov函数证明弱收敛，包含Malitsky反射梯度法；提出数据驱动的自适应步长规则，用数据驱动Lyapunov权重将L从耗散预算中消去；对仿射B及多面体C分析距离衰减与R-线性率，并用旋转下界证明步长锐利性。

Result: 常数步长下弱收敛且对可和算子误差鲁棒；C有界时λ<(√2-1)/L，无界时退化为先验有界性，经耗散交易认证至λL=0.387。仿射B与多面体C上dist(x_n,S)→0且∑dist^2<∞，有界C强收敛，面识别后R-线性。自适应步长规则无条件弱收敛。仿射B、C=H时λL=1/√3锐利，无约束非线性B同样锐利；低于1/(√3 L)的无条件收敛归结为边缘极点绝对稳定性。强单调下R-线性收敛并有显式压缩因子，认证相对常数步长有Ω(L)加速。数值实验验证增益。

Conclusion: 该单次评估迭代法在无需Lipschitz常数、无需额外评估的情况下实现无条件弱收敛，并给出锐利步长界限与强单调下的R-线性加速；投影情形在1/(√3 L)以下的无条件收敛仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We analyze a one-evaluation-per-iteration method for $\mathrm{VI}(C,B)$ with monotone $L$-Lipschitz $B$: $x_{k+1}=P_C(x_k-λB(u_k))$, $u_k=x_k+θ_k(x_k-x_{k-1})+β_k(x_k-u_{k-1})$, $θ_k+β_k=1$. For constant step and summable filter ($\sum_kβ_k<\infty$) we prove weak convergence via a Lyapunov function with exact rational dissipation budgets, including Malitsky's reflected gradient method. It is robust to summable operator errors; if $C$ is bounded, the constant-step range reaches $λ<(\sqrt2-1)/L$. For unbounded $C$ this reduces to an a priori boundedness statement, certified to $λL=0.387$ by dissipation trading; for affine $B$ on polyhedral $C$, $\dist(x_n,S)\to0$ with $\sum_n\dist^2(x_n,S)<\infty$, strong convergence for bounded $C$, and $R$-linear rates after face identification. The main result is a safeguarded adaptive step-size rule needing no $L$ and no extra evaluations, proved weakly convergent unconditionally: a data-driven Lyapunov weight removes $L$ from the dissipation budgets. For affine $B$ with $C=\mathcal H$, sharpness of $λL=1/\sqrt3$ via a rotation lower bound; the same constant is sharp for unconstrained nonlinear $B$, with convergence for square-summable operator values. Unconditional convergence below $1/(\sqrt3\,L)$ reduces to a marginal-pole absolute-stability statement; the projected case remains open. Under strong monotonicity we prove $R$-linear convergence with explicit contraction, and certify $Ω(L)$ speedups over constant steps. Numerics confirm the gains.

</details>


### [178] [The sharp step-size constant for one-call reflection splittings on monotone inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18373)
*Yekini Shehu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明Malitsky–Tam前向-反射-后向分裂的步长上界1/(2L)是紧的，并给出斜旋转族的精确稳定性阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Malitsky–Tam前向-反射-后向分裂在步长λ∈(0,1/(2L))（B单调且L-Lipschitz）下弱收敛，但该界是否紧一直是公开问题。

Method: 构造匹配斜实例A=LJ、B=LJ（J为R^2中逆时针π/2旋转），并推广到斜旋转族A=γJ、B=J，分析其迭代稳定性阈值。

Result: 对匹配斜实例，λ≥1/(2L)时迭代不收敛，λ>1/(2L)时发散；斜旋转族的精确稳定性阈值为λ*(γ)=1/√((1+γ)(3−γ))，在γ=1处取最小值1/2，在γ=0处恢复反射梯度常数1/√3。

Conclusion: 该实例同时是Cevher–Vũ反射-前向-后向方法的反例；在线性算子情形下该方法与前向-反射-后向一致。

Abstract: The forward-reflected-backward splitting of Malitsky and Tam converges weakly for step sizes $λ\in(0,\tfrac{1}{2L})$, where $B$ is monotone and $L$-Lipschitz, and the question whether this bound is tight has been recorded as open \cite{GS}. We show that it is: for the matched-skew instance $A=LJ$, $B=LJ$ ($J$ the counterclockwise rotation by $π/2$ in $\R^2$), the iterates fail to converge for every $λ\ge\tfrac{1}{2L}$ and diverge for $λ>\tfrac{1}{2L}$. More generally, for the skew--rotation family $A=γJ$, $B=J$, the exact stability threshold is $λ^\star(γ)=1/\sqrt{(1+γ)(3-γ)}$, which attains its minimum $1/2$ at the matched skew $γ=1$ and recovers the reflected gradient constant $1/\sqrt3$ at $γ=0$. The same instance is a counterexample for the reflected--forward--backward method of Cevher and Vũ, which coincides with forward-reflected-backward on linear operators.

</details>


### [179] [Gradient Descent with Stochastic Subspaces via Persistence of Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18416)
*Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Clement Z. Q. Ng,Pierre-Louis Poirion,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出"记忆持久性"技术来扩展随机子空间方法：利用一个与梯度弱相关的引导向量来指导随机子空间的生成，该向量可在大量迭代中保持不变并按间隔刷新，从而提升大规模优化问题的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机子空间方法（SSD）作为大规模优化（尤其是分布式场景）的梯度下降类技术已广为流行，但存在改进空间。作者希望大幅扩展并改进这类方法，通过在随机子空间的生成过程中引入稳定的引导结构，减少重复计算、提升收敛效率。

Method: 引入"记忆持久性"技术：使用一个与梯度仅弱相关的向量作为引导向量，为下降方向所依赖的随机子空间生成过程提供引导结构。该引导向量可在大量迭代中固定不变，仅以较大间隔刷新，且刷新间隔可依据问题参数给出保证。在稀疏或小批量结构等机器学习问题中，利用问题的结构特性以高效且计算低廉的方式获得引导向量。同时首次对稀疏函数的经典SSD方法进行理论分析。

Result: 在最优点的局部邻域内，证明了梯度估计与Hessian的低阶特征向量之间存在对齐现象，使得引导向量可一次性计算；即便在非结构化目标场景下，该方法也在计算上具有优势。

Conclusion: 通过"记忆持久性"引导向量机制，该方法显著扩展并改进了随机子空间方法，在稀疏、小批量等结构化问题及非结构化目标中均表现出计算优势，并首次给出了稀疏函数SSD方法的理论分析及引导向量与Hessian低阶特征向量的对齐现象。

Abstract: Stochastic subspace methods have gained popularity as gradient descent based techniques for large scale optimisation problems, especially in distributed settings. In this paper, we introduce the technique of "persistence of memory" to greatly extend and improve the random subspace methods. To this end, we leverage a vector that is only weakly correlated with the gradient in order to provide a guiding structure to the generative process of the random subspace along which the descent is going to take place. This guidance vector may be fixed for a large number of iterations, only to be refreshed at wide intervals (on whose size we can provide guarantees in terms of problem parameters). In important machine learning settings, such as optimisation problems embodying sparsity or a minibatch structure, we show that the guidance vector can be obtained in an effective and computationally inexpensive manner by leveraging the structured properties of the problem. En route, we establish to our knowledge the first theoretical analysis of classical SSD methods for sparse functions. In a local neighbourhood of the optimum, we demonstrate an alignment phenomenon of our gradient estimates with a low-lying eigenvector of the Hessian, allowing a once-for-all computation of the guidance vector which renders the method computationally favourable even in scenarios with unstructured objectives.

</details>


### [180] [The Projected Hessian Quantification Theorem: Exact Duality For Constrained Eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18538)
*Meiling Wang,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发展约束对称特征值问题的定量罚理论，给出约化Hessian极值特征值的精确刻画、罚路径的有限恢复与渐近误差展开，并提出无矩阵Penalty-Split-Merge算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Projected Hessian Lemma（源于Finsler定理）只能通过二次罚判定约束零空间上的定性，无法量化受约束特征值及对应特征值的罚路径，缺少定量理论。

Method: 通过正交分解、Schur补分析、半定规划对偶给出三种证明；刻画极值特征值罚路径的有限精确恢复，在无有限恢复时建立带显式首项系数的一阶误差渐近展开；利用Hellmann-Feynman灵敏度关系预测罚参数；据此提出无矩阵Penalty-Split-Merge方法，结合罚延拓、Split-Merge迭代、deflation与投影认证求解连续约束极值特征对。

Result: 建立约化Hessian极值特征值经全空间罚特征值问题的精确刻画；获得罚路径的有限精确恢复条件与渐近一阶误差展开及显式首项系数；数值实验验证了预测的罚参数区间，评估了投影认证，并在中等规模基准与大规模无矩阵测试实例上考察了计算性能。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典判定性结果推广为定量罚理论，为约束特征值问题提供可预测的罚参数选择与高效无矩阵求解框架。

Abstract: The classical Projected Hessian Lemma, originating from Finsler's theorem, characterizes definiteness over a constraint null space through quadratic penalties. However, it does not quantify the corresponding constrained eigenvalues or the associated eigenvalue penalty path. This work develops a quantitative penalty theory for constrained symmetric eigenvalue problems and establishes exact characterizations of the extremal eigenvalues of the reduced Hessian via full-space penalized eigenvalue problems. Three proofs are provided based on orthogonal decomposition, Schur complement analysis, and semidefinite programming duality. We characterize finite exact recovery along the extremal-eigenvalue penalty paths and, in the absence of finite recovery, establish asymptotic convergence with a first-order error expansion and an explicit leading coefficient. The associated Hellmann--Feynman sensitivity relation leads to a strategy for predicting penalty parameters. Based on these results, we develop a matrix-free Penalty--Split--Merge method for successive constrained extremal eigenpairs using penalty continuation, Split--Merge iterations, deflation, and projected certification. Numerical experiments illustrate the predicted penalty regimes, evaluate projected certification, and assess computational performance on moderate-scale benchmarks and large-scale matrix-free test instances.

</details>


### [181] [Rapid drone-based wildfire detection at a fraction of current prevention spending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18556)
*Romain Puech,Danique de Moor,Ana Trišović,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在现实运行约束下联合优化监测基础设施布局与自主无人机路径的定量框架，用于评估大规模野火快速检测所需投资。基于加州2021-2024年3,693起起火事件的样本外评估表明，1亿美元五年预算的优化无人机网络可检测97.3%的火灾，其中74%在首小时内发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期野火检测对防止小火情演变为大规模灾害至关重要，但现有监测系统缺乏在大规模尺度上配置检测基础设施的定量框架，难以为投资决策提供依据。

Method: 在现实运行约束下，联合优化监测基础设施的选址与自主无人机的路径规划，构建可量化快速、大规模野火检测所需投资的优化模型，并在加州2021-2024年3,693起起火事件上进行样本外评估。

Result: 在1亿美元五年预算下，优化后的无人机网络检测率达97.3%，其中74%的火灾在首小时内被检测到；五年摊销约合每年2,000万美元，约为加州年度野火防控支出的5%。在当前技术成本下，无人机监测的性价比显著优于静态地面传感器。检测能力主要由空间覆盖度决定，而路径策略主要决定检测速度。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了量化野火检测基础设施投资的框架，证明无人机网络在大规模快速检测中兼具高检测率与成本效益，且覆盖度与路径策略分别主导检测率与检测速度，可为监测系统的规模化部署提供决策支持。

Abstract: Early wildfire detection is critical to prevent small ignitions from escalating into large-scale disasters, yet current monitoring systems lack a quantitative framework for allocating detection infrastructure at scale. We jointly optimize the placement of monitoring infrastructure and the routing of autonomous drones under realistic operational constraints to quantify the investment required for rapid, large-scale wildfire detection. Evaluated out-of-sample on 3,693 California ignitions from 2021-2024, an optimized drone network operating at a $100 million five-year budget detects 97.3% of fires, including 74% within the first hour. Amortized over five years, that budget is about $20 million per year, roughly 5% of California's annual wildfire-prevention expenditure. Under current technology costs, drone-based monitoring is substantially more cost-effective than static ground sensors. Detection is governed primarily by spatial coverage, while routing strategy mainly determines detection speed.

</details>


### [182] [Nonlinear parameter-varying embeddings for nonlinear state estimation with application to a two-link robot manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18603)
*Jiaxin Ji,Shivaraj Mohite,Jan Heiland*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文提出非线性参数变化（NLPV）嵌入的观测器设计方法，兼顾线性结构与非线性结构，减少调度参数数量，并以双臂机器人模型为例与EKF、MHE进行数值对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统观测器设计是系统与控制领域重要且具挑战性的任务；标准LPV嵌入虽可利用线性结构，但需引入大量调度参数，表征复杂。作者希望在保留部分非线性结构的同时受益于LPV的线性框架，从而降低调度参数数量。

Method: 将被观测系统嵌入非线性参数变化（NLPV）系统类，给出面向一般非线性系统的NLPV观测器设计流程，并提出若干改进措施；以双臂机器人模型为应用实例进行说明。

Result: 在数值研究中，将NLPV设计方案的性能与扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）、移动 horizon 估计（MHE）等已有标准非线性方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: NLPV嵌入为非线性观测器设计提供了一条兼顾线性结构利用与非线性结构保留的途径，可减少调度参数并具备与EKF、MHE等成熟方法竞争的潜力，双臂机器人案例验证了其适用性。

Abstract: Observer design for nonlinear systems is a relevant and challenging task in systems and control design. In this work, we follow the idea of embedding the system in the class of nonlinear parameter-varying systems to benefit from linear structures as in a standard LPV embedding while keeping some nonlinear structures and, thus, reducing the numbers of scheduling-parameters in the representation. We lay out the NLPV observer design procedure for general nonlinear systems, propose a number of improvements, and exemplify the application for a two-arm robot model. In a numerical study, we compare the performance of the NLPV design to established standard nonlinear approaches such as the \emph{extended Kalman filter} and the \emph{moving horizon estimation}.

</details>


### [183] [A $\mathcal{VU}$-calculus for composite functions and the $\mathcal{U}$-Hessian of partly smooth functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18683)
*Shuai Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了复合非光滑函数的有限维 \(\mathcal{VU}\)-微积分（外函数为凸），包括链式法则、可分与凸和、lsc项的严格可微扰动、模型 \(\delta_X+f_0+\theta\circ F\) 及 \(C^1\) 函数的有限最大值，并给出 \(\varepsilon\)-\(\mathcal{VU}\) 链式法则；在凸链式法则成立点集上以因子表示 \(\mathcal{V}f\)、\(\mathcal{U}f\)、正交标架与 \(\mathcal{U}\)-梯度 \(\bar g_u\)，并证明 \(C^1\)/\(C^2\)-部分光滑下 \(\bar g_u=U_f^\top\nabla_{\mathcal M}f(\bar x)\)、\(\mathcal{U}\)-Hessian/local矩阵等于Gram矩阵 \(U_f^\top\nabla^2_{\mathcal M}f(\bar x)U_f\)；进一步证明 \(H_\varepsilon\succ0\) 等价于倾斜稳定性与 \(\partial f\) 的强度量正则性，且 \(\mathrm{lip}((\partial f)^{-1})(0\mid\bar x)=\|H_\varepsilon^{-1}\|\)，最后在hinge复合与两个 \(H_U\succ0\) 初等测试上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对复合非光滑函数（外函数为凸），需要一套可计算的有限维 \(\mathcal{VU}\)-微积分及其二阶信息（Hessian）刻画。现有 \(\mathcal{VU}\) 分解缺乏对复合结构的链式法则、梯度/Hessian的显式因子表达，以及与倾斜稳定性、强度量正则性等变分性质之间等价关系的系统建立。本文旨在填补这一空白，并给出可用于验证二阶充分条件（\(H\succ0\)）的判别测试。

Method: 建立有限维 \(\mathcal{VU}\)-calculus：给出链式法则、可分和与凸和、lsc项的严格可微扰动、模型 \(\delta_X+f_0+\theta\circ F\)、\(C^1\)函数的有限最大值等运算规则，以及关于次微分真外逼近的 \(\varepsilon\)-\(\mathcal{VU}\) 链式法则。在集合 \(\mathcal R_{h,F}\) 上用因子表示 \(\mathcal Vf\)、\(\mathcal Uf\)、\(\mathcal Uf\) 的正交标架和 \(\mathcal U\)-梯度 \(\bar g_u\)。在 \(C^1\)-部分光滑与 \(C^2\)-部分光滑假设下，将 \(\bar g_u\) 和 \(\mathcal U\)-Hessian \(H_U\)（凸情形）或局部矩阵 \(H_\varepsilon\)（在 \(\bar x\) 关于0 prox-正则）表示为 \(U_f^\top\nabla_{\mathcal M}f(\bar x)\) 与 Gram 矩阵 \(U_f^\top\nabla^2_{\mathcal M}f(\bar x)U_f\)，并在连续标架下沿活跃流形讨论连续性。结合 prox-正则性与次微分连续性建立 \(H_\varepsilon\succ0\) 与倾斜稳定性、\(\partial f\) 在 \((\bar x,0)\) 强度量正则性的等价，并用 hinge 复合与两个初等测试进行说明。

Result: 在所设假设下，链式法则与 \(\varepsilon\)-\(\mathcal{VU}\) 链式法则成立；\(\mathcal Vf\)、\(\mathcal Uf\)、正交标架与 \(\bar g_u\) 可由因子显式表示。若 \(f\) 在 \(\bar x\) 处 \(C^1\)-部分光滑，则 \(\bar g_u=U_f^\top\nabla_{\mathcal M}f(\bar x)\)；若 \(f\) 为 \(C^2\)-部分光滑且 \(0\in\mathrm{ri}\,\partial f(\bar x)\)，则凸 \(\mathcal U\)-Hessian \(H_U\) 或局部矩阵 \(H_\varepsilon\) 等于Gram矩阵 \(U_f^\top\nabla^2_{\mathcal M}f(\bar x)U_f\)，且沿活跃流形在连续标架下连续依赖于基点。进一步，在 \(C^2\)-部分光滑、prox-正则与次微分连续条件下，\(H_\varepsilon\succ0\) 等价于 \(\bar x\) 的倾斜稳定性及 \(\partial f\) 在 \((\bar x,0)\) 的强度量正则性，且 \(\mathrm{lip}((\partial f)^{-1})(0\mid\bar x)=\|H_\varepsilon^{-1}\|\)。结果在 hinge 复合与两个 \(H_U\succ0\) 的初等测试上得到验证。

Conclusion: 本文给出了复合非光滑凸外函数情形的有限维 \(\mathcal{VU}\)-微积分与 \(\varepsilon\)-\(\mathcal{VU}\) 链式法则，并用因子显式刻画了 \(\mathcal Vf\)、\(\mathcal Uf\)、\(\mathcal U\)-梯度与 \(\mathcal U\)-Hessian。核心结论是 \(\mathcal U\)-Hessian/局部矩阵与 \(\nabla^2_{\mathcal M}f\) 的Gram矩阵一致，且 \(H_\varepsilon\succ0\) 同时等价于倾斜稳定性与 \(\partial f\) 的强度量正则性，正则性模恰为 \(\|H_\varepsilon^{-1}\|\)。该框架为验证二阶充分条件提供了可计算的测试，并在 hinge 复合与初等例子中得到说明。

Abstract: We assemble a finite-dimensional \(\mathcal{VU}\)-calculus for composite nonsmooth functions whose outer function is convex: the chain rule, separable and convex sums, a strictly differentiable perturbation of a lower semicontinuous (lsc) term, the model \(δ_X+f_0+θ\circ F\), and a finite maximum of \(C^1\) functions. The same algebra yields an \(\varepsilon\)-\(\mathcal{VU}\) chain rule for a proper outer approximation of the subdifferential. On the set \(\mathcal{R}_{h,F}\) of points at which the convex chain rule holds, the subspaces \(\mathcal{V}f\) and \(\mathcal{U}f\), an orthonormal frame of \(\mathcal{U}f\), and the \(\mathcal{U}\)-gradient \(\bar g_u\) are written in terms of the factors. If \(f\) is \(C^1\)-partly smooth at \(\bar{x}\), that gradient is \(\bar g_u=U_f^\top\nabla_{\mathcal{M}}f(\bar{x})\). If \(f\) is \(C^2\)-partly smooth and \(0\in\ri\partial f(\bar{x})\), the convex \(\mathcal{U}\)-Hessian \(H_U\) (when \(f\) is convex) or the local matrix \(H_\varepsilon\) (when \(f\) is prox-regular at \(\bar{x}\) for \(0\)) equals the Gram matrix \(U_f^\top\nabla^2_{\mathcal{M}}f(\bar{x})\,U_f\); the same matrix, written along the active manifold in a continuous frame, depends continuously on the base point. If in addition \(f\) is \(C^2\)-partly smooth at \(\bar{x}\), then under prox-regularity and subdifferential continuity at \(\bar{x}\) for \(0\), \(H_\varepsilon\succ 0\) is equivalent to tilt stability of \(\bar{x}\) and to strong metric regularity of \(\partial f\) at \((\bar{x},0)\), with \(\lip\bigl((\partial f)^{-1}\bigr)(0\mid\bar{x})=\|H_\varepsilon^{-1}\|\). The calculus and the test are illustrated on a hinge composite and two elementary tests of \(H_U\succ 0\).

</details>


### [184] [Near-Optimal Exact-Value Zeroth-Order Complexity for Smooth Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18728)
*Wendao Wu,Haihan Zhang,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究确定性自适应优化中β-光滑、μ-强凸函数的最小极大值复杂度，给出下界与上界，并在精度主导区间及常数相对精度处实现匹配，但一般精度依赖仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究确定性自适应优化在精确标量函数值查询下的复杂度，刻画光滑强凸函数最小极大值查询复杂度随维度、条件数与精度参数的变化。

Method: 下界采用精确屏蔽光滑链（exactly shielded smooth chain）与批量延迟旋转（batched delayed rotations）；上界结合有限差分、加速与重启技术。设定查询与输出位于 $B_2^d(R)$，最优解位于 $B_2^d(R/2)$，并引入 $\kappa=\beta/\mu$、$Q=\beta R^2/\varepsilon$、$D_d=(d/\log(ed))^{1/3}$。

Result: 对充分大的 $d$ 与 $0<\varepsilon\le c_\varepsilon\beta R^2$，最小极大值复杂度满足下界 $N_\varepsilon\ge c d\min\{\sqrt Q,\sqrt\kappa,D_d\}$，上界 $N_\varepsilon\le C d\min\{\sqrt Q,\sqrt\kappa[1+\log_+(Q/\kappa)]\}$。当 $\min\{Q,\kappa\}\le D_d^2$ 时，在精度主导区间 $Q\le\kappa$ 与常数相对精度 $\varepsilon=\Theta(\mu R^2)$ 处上下界匹配至常数因子；在 $\kappa\le D_d^2$ 的更高精度范围内，两者最多相差 $1+\log(\mu R^2/\varepsilon)$。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了确定性自适应光滑强凸优化的最小极大值复杂度结构，在多种区间实现上下界匹配，但一般情形下最优精度依赖仍未解决。

Abstract: We study deterministic adaptive optimization of globally $β$-smooth, $μ$-strongly convex functions using exact scalar function values. Queries and outputs lie in $B_2^d(R)$, and the minimizer lies in $B_2^d(R/2)$. Set $κ=β/μ$, $Q=βR^2/ε$, and $D_d=(d/\log(ed))^{1/3}$. For sufficiently large $d$ and $0<ε\le c_εβR^2$, the minimax value complexity $N_ε$ satisfies \[ \begin{aligned} N_ε&\ge c d\min\{\sqrt Q,\sqrtκ,D_d\},\\ N_ε&\le C d\min\left\{ \sqrt Q,\sqrtκ[1+\log_+(Q/κ)] \right\}, \end{aligned} \] where $c,C,c_ε>0$ are universal constants and $\log_+(t)=\max\{0,\log t\}$. The lower bound uses an exactly shielded smooth chain and batched delayed rotations; the upper bound combines finite differences, acceleration, and restart. When $\min\{Q,κ\}\le D_d^2$, these bounds match up to constants in the accuracy-dominated regime $Q\leκ$ and at constant relative accuracy $ε=Θ(μR^2)$. For arbitrarily higher accuracy in the range $κ\le D_d^2$, the bounds differ by at most $1+\log(μR^2/ε)$; the optimal accuracy dependence remains unresolved in general.

</details>


### [185] [Regularity of a Multidimensional Principal-Agent Problem with Separable Effort Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18770)
*Shuaijie Qian,Guan Qiao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究可分离非二次努力成本下多维连续时间委托-代理模型值函数的正则性，通过引入独立正则化噪声并限制努力证明值函数局部属于Sobolev空间W^{2,1}_{∞,loc}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二次成本设定下可构造经典解，但可分离非二次努力成本使HJB方程完全非线性、退化且系数可能无界，原有论证失效，故需建立新的正则性分析。

Method: 以产出与代理人续期效用为状态变量构建随机控制问题；引入独立正则化噪声并对努力加界，先证明正则化问题值函数的经典正则性与最优努力的唯一性、正性及一致紧致性，再依次去掉控制约束并令噪声消失。

Result: 正则化值函数收敛到原值函数，原值函数局部属于W^{2,1}_{∞,loc}；最优努力唯一、为正且始终位于固定紧子集中，且该结论对控制限制与正则化参数一致成立。

Conclusion: 将值函数正则性分析拓展到可分离非二次努力成本情形，克服了二次成本下经典解方法不再适用的困难。

Abstract: This paper studies the regularity of the value function arising from a multidimensional continuous-time principal-agent model with separable, nonquadratic effort costs. The associated stochastic control problem has the output and the agent's continuation utility as state variables, and its Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation is fully nonlinear and degenerate, with potentially unbounded coefficients. We address these difficulties by adding an independent regularization noise and bounding the effort. For the resulting problem, we establish classical regularity of the value function and show that the optimal effort is unique, positive and remains in a fixed compact subset, uniformly with respect to both the control restriction and the regularization parameter. These estimates allow us first to remove the control restriction and then to let the additional noise vanish. Consequently, we prove that the regularized value function converges to the original value function and conclude that the latter belongs locally to the Sobolev space $W^{2,1}_{\infty, \mathrm{loc}}$, thereby extending the regularity analysis to separable nonquadratic effort costs, for which the arguments yielding classical solutions in the quadratic-cost setting no longer apply.

</details>


### [186] [Optimizing Lyapunov Certificates via Stability-Preserving Quadratization for Polynomial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18871)
*Yubo Cai,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出最优耗散二次化（ODQ）框架，针对固定单项式提升，联合设计稳定器增益与表示规范（gauge），以最小化二次Lyapunov证书的谱范数界，并给出闭环验证的吸引域子水平集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式系统的吸引域（ROA）证书随状态维数和次数增长而代价高昂：直接SOS公式需要组合式增长的单项式基。二次化方法可在二次系统的不变流形上精确表示多项式向量场，从而用二次Lyapunov函数认证ROA。但已有工作先用可行性启发式固定增益，再优化gauge，未能联合优化，限制了证书质量。

Method: 将问题形式化为最优耗散二次化（ODQ）：固定单项式提升、参考延拓、稳定器分解与Lyapunov权重Q=I，联合设计增益与gauge，增益限定在预定紧致Hurwitz盒内。在每个增益处，用精确半定规划（SDP）在gauge上全局最小化谱范数界。引入残差感知界，考虑浮点Lyapunov残差，得到经过认证的闭Lyapunov子水平集。采用外搜索，理论上证明理想化外搜索的每个聚点均为盒-Clarke稳定点；有限运行返回最佳且独立验证的候选解，不声称增益搜索的全局最优性。

Result: 在平面五次系统上，优化增益使认证面积相比匹配的零增益gauge提升2.238倍。在16个具有稳定器自由度的异质多项式系统上，ODQ优于两个固定增益提升基线。中继基准上全部36次ODQ运行均完成；9个完全配对案例的27次重复层级比较中，ODQ在固定方向代理指标与构造时间上均优于采用固定二次Lyapunov函数的SOS基线。与直接SOS方法的更广泛比较结果仍为混合。

Conclusion: ODQ通过联合优化增益与gauge，在固定单项式提升下显著提升ROA认证质量与效率，并具备残差感知的严格认证与理论稳定性保证；但其增益搜索不保证全局最优，与直接SOS方法相比优势并不一致。

Abstract: Region-of-attraction (ROA) certificates for polynomial systems become expensive as state dimension and degree grow: direct sum-of-squares (SOS) formulations require combinatorially growing monomial bases. Quadratization represents a polynomial vector field exactly on an invariant manifold of a quadratic system, allowing a quadratic Lyapunov function to certify the ROA. For a fixed lift, stabilizer gains shape the off-manifold extension and transverse dynamics, while representation gauges change the matrix representation but not the vector field. Both affect the spectral-norm certificate, yet prior work fixes the gain by a feasibility heuristic before optimizing the gauge. We formulate optimal dissipative quadratization (ODQ), jointly designing gains and gauges for a fixed monomial lift, reference extension, stabilizer factorization, and Lyapunov weight $Q=I$. Gains lie in a prescribed compact Hurwitz box. At each gain, an exact semidefinite program globally minimizes the spectral-norm bound over the gauge. Residual-aware bounds yield a certified closed Lyapunov sublevel set, accounting for the floating-point Lyapunov residual. Under our stated assumptions, every accumulation point of the idealized outer search is box-Clarke stationary. A finite run returns the best independently verified candidate; global optimality of the gain search is not claimed. On a planar quintic, optimizing the gain increases the certified area by a factor of $2.238$ over a matched zero-gain gauge. Across 16 heterogeneous polynomial systems with stabilizer freedom, ODQ improves on both fixed-gain lifted baselines. All 36 ODQ runs on the relay benchmark complete, and all 27 repeat-level comparisons across nine fully paired cases favor ODQ over an SOS baseline with a fixed quadratic Lyapunov function in both the fixed-direction proxy and construction time. Broader comparisons with direct SOS methods remain mixed.

</details>


### [187] [From Optimal Transport to Optimal Quantization: A Variational Study of the Witsenhausen Counterexample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18992)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文用最优传输重构标量 Witsenhausen 反例，将其化为关于目标分布的变分问题 $J^*=\inf_Q\{k^2W_2^2(P,Q)+\operatorname{mmse}(Q)\}$，并给出存在性、Euler-Lagrange 条件、Fisher 信息表示、有限支撑量化版本及渐近分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Witsenhausen 反例是分散控制中的经典难题，长期缺乏能同时刻画最优控制器结构的解析框架。作者希望借助最优传输与 MMSE 的视角，为这一问题提供统一的变分表述与结构性结论。

Method: 将第一控制器吸收进传输映射，把问题改写为在目标分布 $Q$ 上最小化 $k^2W_2^2(P,Q)+\operatorname{mmse}(Q)$；最优第一控制器由把先验 $P$ 推至极小点 $Q^*$ 的单调重排恢复。建立 $Q^*$ 的存在性与绝对连续性，推导 Euler-Lagrange 条件，给出估计代价的 Fisher 信息等价表示，并构造带线性最优性显式阈值的半闭式 Gaussian 基准。进一步把 $Q$ 限制为有限支撑，得到 MMSE 正则化最优量化问题；其平稳性条件将质心与 Voronoi 判决区耦合，控制代价高时退化为经典 Lloyd-Max 条件。用关于控制惩罚 $k$ 的确定性退火同伦求解该非凸问题，并推导 $J^*$ 的小 $k$ 与大 $k$ 渐近及极限控制器。

Result: 得到 $Q^*$ 的存在性与绝对连续性、Euler-Lagrange 条件、估计代价的 Fisher 信息表示，以及 Gaussian 基准下的线性最优性阈值。有限支撑情形下，平稳性条件在控制昂贵时退化为 Lloyd-Max 条件；数值求解给出渐近结果并识别极限控制器。

Conclusion: 最优传输为 Witsenhausen 反例提供了统一的变分框架：控制昂贵时极限为线性控制器，控制便宜时为两级信令量化器，两种机制均获数值验证。

Abstract: We study the scalar Witsenhausen counterexample through optimal transport. Absorbing the first control into a transport map recasts the problem as the variational problem $J^=\inf_Q{k^2W_2^2(P,Q)+\operatorname{mmse}(Q)}$ over target laws $Q$. This formulation balances quadratic Wasserstein transport cost against minimum mean-square estimation error, with the optimal first controller recovered as the monotone rearrangement that pushes the prior $P$ to a minimizing law $Q^$. We establish existence and absolute continuity of $Q^$, derive an Euler-Lagrange condition, give an equivalent Fisher-information representation of the estimation cost, and obtain a semi-closed-form Gaussian benchmark with an explicit threshold for linear optimality. Restricting the target law to finite support yields an MMSE-regularized optimal quantization problem. Its stationarity conditions couple centroid levels with Voronoi decision cells and reduce to the classical Lloyd-Max conditions when control is expensive. We solve this nonconvex problem using a deterministic-annealing homotopy in the control penalty $k$. Finally, we derive the small- and large-$k$ asymptotics of $J^$ and identify the limiting controllers: a linear controller when control is expensive and a two-level signaling quantizer when it is cheap. Numerical results illustrate both regimes.

</details>


### [188] [Capacity-Constrained Wasserstein Barycenters: Existence, Duality, and Entropic Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19054)
*Chamila Gamage*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出容量约束的Wasserstein重心问题，证明其存在性与强对偶，并研究熵正则化版本的对偶、最优解结构及收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Wasserstein重心允许任意运输计划，缺乏对运输量的容量限制；本文引入容量度量约束每条的运输计划，以刻画更实际的受限运输场景。

Method: 在紧致域上定义容量约束Wasserstein重心；证明存在性与强对偶公式；进一步研究有界容量密度假设下的熵正则化问题，分析其唯一最优运输计划元组与唯一重心、对偶中的显式截断指数惩罚、容量裁剪Gibbs公式，以及最优值的O(ε)估计。

Result: 证明了容量约束重心的存在性与强对偶；熵正则化问题具有唯一最优运输计划元组和唯一重心；对偶含显式截断指数惩罚；对偶最大化子存在时最优密度满足容量裁剪Gibbs公式；得到最优值的O(ε)估计，且正则化计划收敛到未正则化问题的最小熵最优解。

Conclusion: 该工作将容量约束引入Wasserstein重心，建立了存在性、对偶与正则化理论，并给出正则化向最小熵解的收敛保证。

Abstract: We introduce a capacity-constrained Wasserstein barycenter problem in which each transport plan from an input measure to the barycenter is bounded by a prescribed capacity measure. On compact domains, we prove existence of capacity-constrained barycenters and establish a strong duality formula. We then study an entropy-regularized version under bounded capacity-density assumptions. The regularized problem has a unique optimal tuple of transport plans and a unique barycenter, while its dual involves an explicit capped-exponential penalty. Whenever dual maximizers exist, the optimal densities satisfy a capacity-clipped Gibbs formula. Finally, we prove an $O(ε)$ estimate for the optimal values and show that the regularized plans converge to the minimum-entropy optimal solution of the unregularized problem.

</details>


### [189] [Trajectory Manifolds for Nonlinear Data-Enabled Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19079)
*Arda Bayer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 证明终端状态增广的有限时域行为是 $C^r$ 嵌入子流形（内蕴维数 $n+Nm$），rollout 映射为全局微分同胚，给出典范精确编解码表示并导出潜在维数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为数据使能预测控制（data-enabled predictive control）中的轨迹流形表示提供确定性几何基础。现有轨迹表示多依赖可控性、稳定性或可逆性等假设；本文旨在去除这些限制，建立不依赖控制理论假设的精确几何刻画。

Method: 研究对象为离散时间系统 $x_{k+1}=f(x_k,u_k)$（状态可测、转移映射 $C^r$，$r\geq 1$）。考虑终端状态增广的有限时域行为——预测时域 $N$ 上所有容许的状态-输入轨迹集合；利用微分流形/嵌入子流形理论分析该集合的几何结构；构造函数从容许初始状态与输入坐标 $(x_0,\mathbf u)$ 到轨迹的 rollout 映射，并证明其为 $C^r$ 微分同胚。方法延伸至零阶保持采样的连续时间系统与固定步长数值转移映射。

Result: 该终端状态增广行为是环境轨迹空间的 $C^r$ 嵌入子流形，内蕴维数为 $n+Nm$（$n,m$ 为状态与输入维数）。rollout 映射 $(x_0,\mathbf u)\mapsto$ 轨迹 是从容许坐标到行为流形的 $C^r$ 微分同胚，提供显式全局光滑坐标。由此得到典范的精确编码器-解码器表示，并推出任何该行为的精确可微潜在表示其潜在维数至少为 $n+Nm$。上述几何结论不要求可控性、可稳定化或动力学可逆性。

Conclusion: 在温和正则性（$C^r$）下，有限时域行为具有精确的流形结构与全局坐标，且潜在维数下界 $n+Nm$ 为表示学习的信息-几何限制。该结果为后续数据驱动近似与预测控制发展提供了确定性几何基础。

Abstract: This note establishes a geometric foundation for trajectory-manifold representations of deterministic nonlinear systems in a behavioral setting motivated by data-enabled predictive control. For a discrete-time system $x_{k+1}=f(x_k,u_k)$ with measured state and a $C^r$ transition map, $r\geq 1$, we consider the terminal-state-augmented finite-horizon behavior consisting of all admissible state-input trajectories over a prediction horizon $N$. We prove that this behavior is a $C^r$ embedded submanifold of the ambient trajectory space with intrinsic dimension $n+Nm$, where $n$ and $m$ are the state and input dimensions. Moreover, the rollout map from the admissible initial-state and input coordinates $(x_0,\mathbf u)$ is a $C^r$ diffeomorphism onto the behavior manifold, providing explicit global smooth coordinates. This yields a canonical exact encoder--decoder representation and implies that any exact differentiable latent representation of the full behavior must have latent dimension at least $n+Nm$. The geometric result does not require controllability, stabilizability, or invertibility of the dynamics. Corresponding results are given for zero-order-hold sampled continuous-time systems and fixed-step numerical transition maps. These results provide the deterministic geometric foundation for subsequent data-driven approximation and predictive-control development.

</details>


### [190] [Level-Set Geometry and the Theoretical Performance of PDHG for Conic Linear Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19097)
*Zikai Xiong,Robert M. Freund*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析重启原对偶混合梯度法（rPDHG）在一般凸锥线性优化问题上的理论收敛性能，并揭示其收敛速率与原始-对偶（次）水平集几何性质之间的关系；LP作为特例，其性能仅由最优非最优极值点对应的水平集之直径与"锥半径"之比决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rPDHG 配合启发式增强与 GPU 实现，在大规模线性优化（LP）上已非常成功，但其在更一般的凸锥线性优化问题中的应用研究不足。作者希望弥补这一理论空白，为 rPDHG 在一般锥线性优化中的表现提供理论刻画。

Method: 对一般（凸）锥线性优化问题（LP 为其特例）进行 rPDHG 的理论性能分析，建立原始-对偶 δ-（次）水平集 W_δ 的几何性质与 rPDHG 收敛速率之间的联系，并证明一个收敛速率上界。该上界在满足如下条件时更优：(i) W_δ 在 Hausdorff 距离意义下接近最优解集；(ii) W_δ 的直径与"锥半径"之比很小。

Result: 证明了 rPDHG 的一个收敛速率上界，该界随上述几何条件改善而变优；在 LP 特例中，rPDHG 的性能仅由对应"最佳非最优极值点"的（次）水平集上的"直径/锥半径"之比所界定。该比值依问题实例不同可取值极端，从而在理论与实践中均可能导致 rPDHG 表现优异或很差。

Conclusion: rPDHG 的收敛行为可由原始-对偶水平集的几何（接近最优解集的程度及直径与锥半径之比）刻画；这一统一的几何视角既适用于一般锥线性优化，也涵盖 LP，并解释了其在不同实例上性能差异巨大的现象。

Abstract: We consider solving (convex) conic linear optimization problems, at the scale where matrix-factorization-free methods are attractive or necessary. The restarted primal-dual hybrid gradient method (rPDHG) -- with heuristic enhancements and GPU implementation -- has been very successful in solving huge-scale linear optimization problems (LPs). However, its application to more general conic convex optimization problems is not so well-studied. We analyze the theoretical performance of rPDHG for general (convex) conic linear optimization, and LP as a special case thereof. We show a relationship between the geometry of the primal-dual $δ$-(sub-)level sets ${W}_δ$ and the convergence rate of rPDHG. Specifically, we prove a bound on the convergence rate of rPDHG that improves when there is a primal-dual (sub-)level set ${W}_δ$ for which (i) ${W}_δ$ is close to the optimal solution set in Hausdorff distance, and (ii) the ratio of the diameter to the ``conic radius'' of ${W}_δ$ is small. And in the special case of LP, the performance of rPDHG is bounded only by this ratio applied to the (sub-)level set corresponding to the best non-optimal extreme point. Depending on the problem instance, this ratio can take on extreme values and can result in excellent or poor performance of rPDHG both in theory and in practice.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Distinct Consecutive Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17543)
*Przemek Chojecki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了存在密度为1的正整数集合，使得所有不同连续区块的乘积互不相同，从而回答了Erdős和Graham的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Erdős和Graham提出的关于连续区块乘积（即连续整数乘积）唯一性问题，即寻找密度为1的整数集合使得这些乘积彼此不同。

Method: 构造方法基于对相邻素数间隙内部的整体保留或拒绝，因而对取更长前缀具有稳定性；借助一致仿射曲线估计控制没有先前被拒绝间隙的见证元，并用尺度收缩森林无条件控制所有剩余见证元。

Result: 某密度为1的正整数集合中，所有不同连续区块的乘积均互不相同。

Conclusion: 通过素数间隙构造与仿射曲线估计、尺度收缩森林相结合，证明了满足乘积互异性的整数集合具有密度1，解决了Erdős–Graham的相关问题。

Abstract: We prove that there is a density-one set of positive integers for which the products of all distinct consecutive blocks are distinct, answering a question of Erdős and Graham. The construction retains or rejects entire interiors of consecutive-prime gaps and is therefore stable under passage to longer prefixes. Uniform affine-curve estimates control the witnesses with no previous rejected gap, and a scale-contracting forest controls every remaining witness unconditionally.

</details>


### [192] [Transcendence measure for the values of the logarithmic derivative of the Bessel function $J_0$ at algebraic arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17641)
*Stéphane Fischler,Tanguy Rivoal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了Bessel函数对数导数及二阶E函数对数导数的超越测度指数，将经典的$4d^2\delta$减小为显式的$\mu(d,\delta)\le 4d^2\delta-2d\delta-1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lang与Galochkin的一般性结果给出了$P(J_0'(\alpha)/J_0(\alpha))$的超越测度，其指数为$4d^2\delta+\varepsilon$。作者希望像此前改进$e^\alpha$的超越测度那样，通过优化Siegel-Shidlovskii方法中出现的行列式大小来降低这一指数。

Method: 沿用经典的Siegel-Shidlovskii方法，通过优化该方法中自然出现的行列式的大小，对超越测度的指数进行改进，并将其推广至任意二阶E函数的对数导数。

Result: 对于Bessel函数$J_0$，证明了指数$4d^2\delta$可被更小的显式量$\mu(d,\delta)\le 4d^2\delta-2d\delta-1$替代；当$f$为二阶E函数且$f$与$f'$齐次代数无关时，其对数导数的超越测度也有类似的改进。

Conclusion: 通过优化Siegel-Shidlovskii方法中的行列式规模，可有效改进Bessel函数对数导数的超越测度指数，且该改进可推广至更一般的二阶E函数情形。

Abstract: Let $P\in \mathbb Z[X]\setminus\{0\}$ be of degree $δ\ge 1$ and usual height $H\ge 1$, and let $α\in \overline{\mathbb Q}^*$ be of degree $d\ge 2$. As a consequence of general result due to Lang and Galochkin, we have the following transcendence measure: for any $\varepsilon>0$, there exists $c>0$ such that $\vert P(J_0'(α)/J_0(α))\vert>c/H^{4d^2δ+\varepsilon}$ where $J_0$ is the Bessel function. In this paper, we prove that the exponent $4d^2δ$ can be replaced by a smaller (explicit) quantity $μ(d,δ)\le 4d^2δ-2dδ-1$. A similar improvement holds more generally for the logarithmic derivative of any $E$-function $f$ of differential order 2 and such that $f$ and $f'$ are homogeneously algebraically independent. Our method rests upon the optimization of the size of a determinant that appears naturally in the classical Siegel-Shidlovskii method, following the steps of our previous improvement of the transcendence measure of the value $e^α$ for any $α\in \overline{\mathbb Q}^*$.

</details>


### [193] [Lattice points close to a smooth curve and squarefull numbers in short intervals. II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17715)
*Ognian Trifonov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 利用Browning–Heath-Brown关于三元二次型整点解数的新估计，得到了曲线附近格点计数的新估计，并由此导出平方满数在短区间分布的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线附近格点计数与平方满数在短区间中的分布是解析数论中的经典问题，已有方法在精度或适用范围上存在局限，需要借助更强的三元二次型整点估计来改进。

Method: 应用Browning和Heath-Brown关于盒内三元二次型整数解个数的最新估计，将其用于估计靠近一条曲线的格点数目，进而把该估计应用于平方满数的短区间分布问题。

Result: 得到了曲线附近格点数目的新估计，并据此获得了平方满数在短区间分布方面的新结果。

Conclusion: 三元二次型整点解数的精细估计可作为工具，改进曲线附近格点计数及平方满数短区间分布的相关结论。

Abstract: By applying recent estimates on the number of integer solutions of ternary quadratic forms in boxes due to Browning and Heath-Brown, we obtain new estimates for the number of lattice points close to a curve. This leads to a new result on the distribution of squarefull numbers in short intervals.

</details>


### [194] [Effective Hecke eigenvalue equidistribution over the Atkin--Lehner subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17806)
*Aarya J. Kumar,Sargam Mondal,Erick Ross,Hui Xue*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Atkin-Lehner子空间上Hecke特征值的有效且显式的p-adic Plancherel测度等分布，并给出五个应用，包括推广Atkin-Serre猜想、极值newform计数、模Jacobian有理点渐近、大Q-单因子下界以及对超奇异模Jacobian有限性的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对固定素数p，Atkin-Lehner子空间S_k^σ(N)和S_k^{new,σ}(N)上Hecke特征值的p-adic等分布此前缺乏有效且显式的版本。本文旨在建立显式等分布结果，以便将若干经典结论推广到Atkin-Lehner情形并获得定量估计。

Method: 利用p-adic Plancherel测度μ_p，证明第p个Hecke特征值在Atkin-Lehner子空间S_k^σ(N)⊆S_k(N)与S_k^{new,σ}(N)⊆S_k^{new}(N)上的有效显式μ_p-等分布。

Result: 得到显式等分布定理，并给出五个应用：(1) 将Kim的Atkin-Serre猜想垂直类比推广到Atkin-Lehner设定；(2) 给出p在S_k^{new,σ}(N)上为极值的newform个数f∈S_k^{new,σ}(N)的显式上界；(3) 证明模Jacobian J_0(N)及其因子J_0^{new}(N)、J_0^σ(N)、J_0^{new,σ}(N)上F_{p^r}-点数的显式渐近，并使Serre关于模曲线X_0(N)点数的渐近结果显式化；(4) 将Murty-Sinha关于J_0(N)大Q-单因子大小的下界推广到J_0^σ(N)；(5) 用于证明对任意固定素数只有有限多个模Jacobian模该素数为超奇异。

Conclusion: 该显式p-adic等分布结果统一并强化了Atkin-Lehner设定下的多个算术与几何计数问题，具有广泛的推论价值。

Abstract: For a fixed prime $p$, let $μ_p$ denote the $p$-adic Plancherel measure. Then the first main goal of this paper is to prove effective (and moreover explicit) $μ_p$-equidistribution of the $p$-th Hecke eigenvalues over the Atkin--Lehner subspaces $S_k^σ(N) \subseteq S_k(N)$ and $S_k^{\operatorname{new}, σ}(N) \subseteq S_k^{\operatorname{new}}(N)$. We then highlight five applications of this explicit equidistribution result. For the first application, we generalize Kim's vertical analog of the Atkin--Serre conjecture to the Atkin--Lehner setting. For the second application, we obtain explicit bounds on the number of newforms $f \in S_k^{\operatorname{new}, σ}(N)$ for which $p$ is extremal over $S_k^{\operatorname{new}, σ}(N)$. For the third application, we prove explicit asymptotics for the number of $\mathbb{F}_{p^r}$-points on the modular Jacobian $J_0(N),$ as well as on its factors $J_0^{\operatorname{new}}(N),$ $J_0^σ(N),$ and $J_0^{\operatorname{new}, σ}(N)$. We also make explicit an asymptotic result of Serre concerning point counts of the modular curves $X_0(N)$. For the fourth application, we generalize lower bounds due to Murty and Sinha on the sizes of large $\mathbb{Q}$-simple factors of $J_0(N)$ to analogous bounds for $J_0^σ(N)$. Finally, for the fifth application (the details of which are given in a separate paper), we use our explicit equidistribution result to prove that only finitely many modular Jacobians are supersingular modulo any fixed prime.

</details>


### [195] [Only finitely many modular Jacobians are supersingular modulo a given prime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17819)
*Aarya J. Kumar,Sargam Mondal,Erick Ross,Hui Xue*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从纵向（固定素数p、变化水平N）和横向（固定新形式f、变化素数p）两个视角研究模Jacobian J₀(N)及GL₂型阿贝尔簇A_f的超奇异性，证明纵向仅有限多个水平N使J₀(N)模p超奇异，横向对充分大的p，A_f模p超奇异当且仅当其与超奇异椭圆曲线幂同源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模Jacobian与GL₂型阿贝尔簇的超奇异性是算术几何与模形式理论中的重要问题，此前多从单一视角研究。本文希望同时从纵向（水平N变化）与横向（素数p变化）两个维度系统刻画超奇异现象，厘清其有限性与同源刻画。

Method: 1. 纵向：固定素数p，考察J₀(N)随水平N变化的模p超奇异性，运用模曲线几何与Hecke代数作用证明仅有有限多个N使其超奇异。2. 横向：固定GL₂型新形式f，考察A_f随素数p变化的模p约化，利用Tate猜想/同源判据等刻画超奇异性与超奇异椭圆曲线幂的同源关系。

Result: 纵向结果：对给定素数p，J₀(N)模p超奇异仅对有限多个水平N成立。横向结果：对充分大的素数p，A_f模p约化超奇异当且仅当A_f与某条超奇异椭圆曲线的幂同源。

Conclusion: 本文从纵向与横向双重视角完整刻画了模Jacobian J₀(N)与GL₂型阿贝尔簇A_f的超奇异性：纵向具有有限性，横向在充分大素数下超奇异性等价于与超奇异椭圆曲线幂同源，为相关算术几何问题提供了统一结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we study supersingularity of modular Jacobians $J_0(N)$ and abelian varieties $A_f$ of $\mathrm{GL}_{2}$-type from both the vertical perspective (where the prime $p$ is fixed, and the level $N$ varies) and the horizontal perspective (where the newform $f$ is fixed, and the prime $p$ varies). Vertically, we prove that $J_{0}(N)$ is supersingular modulo a given prime $p$ for only finitely many levels $N$. Horizontally, we show that for sufficiently large $p$, the reduction of $A_f$ modulo $p$ is supersingular if and only if it isogenous to a power of a supersingular elliptic curve.

</details>


### [196] [Stable Trace Formula for Newton strata of Shimura varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18665)
*Dhruva Kelkar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对阿贝尔型Shimura簇的单个Newton层，构造了Frobenius--Hecke对应在紧支$\ell$-进上同调（带$\ell$-进局部系数）上交错迹的稳定化公式，并给出判别哪些椭圆内窥群能贡献于给定Newton层的群论判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shimura簇的special fibre按$\sigma$-共轭类$b\in B(G_{\mathbb{Q}_p},\mu_h^{-1})$分层。作者希望将单个Newton层上的上同调迹表达为椭圆稳定几何分布，以便与自守谱数据比较，从而理解各层的内窥贡献结构。

Method: 修改Langlands--Kottwitz方法，对每个Newton层构造稳定化公式，将Frobenius--Hecke对应的Lefschetz数表示为$G$的内窥群上的椭圆稳定几何分布；并给出确定哪些椭圆内窥群能贡献于给定Newton层的群论判据。

Result: 得到了单个Newton层上带局部系数上同调交错迹的稳定化公式；给出了内窥群贡献的群论判据；对某些unitary Shimura簇，展示了一个无非平凡内窥贡献的中间Newton层，尽管周围Shimura簇的上同调中存在非平凡内窥贡献。

Conclusion: Newton层的内窥贡献不必然来自周围Shimura簇的整体内窥贡献；稳定化公式可用于与自守谱数据比较，并揭示了分层结构中内窥贡献的精细行为。

Abstract: Let $(G,X)$ be a Shimura datum of abelian type satisfying the hypotheses of the main theorem, and suppose that the associated Shimura varieties have hyperspecial good reduction at $p$. Their special fibres are stratified by the $σ$-conjugacy classes $b\in B(G_{\mathbb{Q}_p},μ_h^{-1})$. For an individual Newton stratum, this paper constructs a stabilized formula for the alternating traces of Frobenius--Hecke correspondences on its compactly supported $\ell$-adic cohomology, with coefficients in an $\ell$-adic local system. The formula, obtained by modifying the Langlands--Kottwitz method, expresses these Lefschetz numbers as elliptic stable geometric distributions on endoscopic groups of $G$, placing the cohomological traces in a form suitable for comparison with automorphic spectral data. We also give a group-theoretic criterion determining which elliptic endoscopic groups can contribute to a given Newton stratum. For certain unitary Shimura varieties, we exhibit an intermediate Newton stratum with no non-trivial endoscopic contribution, although there are non-trivial endoscopic contributions in the cohomology of the ambient Shimura variety.

</details>


### [197] [On two forms in many variables of different degrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17864)
*Kiseok Yeon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究多个变量下两个不同次数形式系统的整点解，在奇点轨迹维数较小的条件下，降低了阈值 $n_0(\boldsymbol{F})$，得到了与 Manin-Peyre 猜想一致的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同次数的两个形式系统在多个变量下的整点解，并期望得到与 Manin-Peyre 猜想一致的渐近公式，同时试图改进前人工作中可容许阈值 $n_0(\boldsymbol{F})$ 的结果。

Method: 发展了一种新的差分方法，结合 van der Corput 差分方法，对与多个变量中两个不同次数形式相关的指数和均值给出高效上界估计，适用于次数差足够大的情形。

Result: 在奇点轨迹维数较小的条件下，得到当 $n>n_0$ 时 $N(\boldsymbol{F};P)=c_{\boldsymbol{F}}\cdot P^{n-d_1-d_2}+O(P^{n-d_1-d_2-\delta})$。相比前人工作，在大多数情形下降低了阈值 $n_0(\boldsymbol{F})$，除 $d_2-d_1=1$ 外；对非奇异形式，当 $d_2\geq 5d_1$、$d_1\geq 2$ 时得到 $n_0(\boldsymbol{F})=3(d_2-1)2^{d_2-1}+(d_1-1)2^{d_1}$。

Conclusion: 该结果显著改进此前的界 $n_0(\boldsymbol{F})=(d_1+2)(d_2-1)2^{d_2-1}+d_12^{d_1-1}$；所提出的方法具有灵活性，可推广应用到一般不同次数的多变量形式。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate integral solutions satisfying the system of two forms in many variables of different degrees. Let $d_1$ and $d_2$ be natural numbers with $d_2>d_1\geq 2$. Let $F_{i}(\boldsymbol{x}) \ (i=1,2)$ be forms in $n$ variables of degrees $d_i\ (i=1,2)$, respectively. Define $$N(\boldsymbol{F};P):=\#\{\boldsymbol{x}\in [-P,P]^n\cap \mathbb{Z}^n:\ F_{i}(\boldsymbol{x})=0\ (i=1,2)\}.$$ When each dimension of singular loci of $F_1=0$ and $F_2=0$ is small, we obtain a number $n_0:=n_0(\boldsymbol{F})$ such that whenever $n> n_0$ one has the expected asymptotic formula \begin{equation*} N(\boldsymbol{F};P)=c_{\boldsymbol{F}}\cdot P^{n-d_1-d_2}+O(P^{n-d_1-d_2-δ}),\ \text{for some }δ>0, \end{equation*} where the constant $c_{\boldsymbol{F}}$ is the product of local densities. We note that this asymptotic formula agrees with the Manin-Peyre conjecture.
  Compared to the previous work, we lower the admissible threshold $n_0(\boldsymbol{F})$ in most cases, with the exception of case $d_2-d_1=1$. In particular, if $F_1$ and $F_2$ are non-singular forms, then we obtain $$n_0(\boldsymbol{F})=3(d_2-1)2^{d_2-1}+(d_1-1)2^{d_1},$$ provided that $d_2\geq 5d_1$ with $d_1\geq2$. This yields a substantial improvement over the previous bound $n_0(\boldsymbol{F})=(d_1+2)(d_2-1)2^{d_2-1}+d_12^{d_1-1}$.
  To achieve this, we develop a new differencing argument together with the van der Corput differencing argument, delivering an efficient upper-bound estimate for mean values of exponential sums associated with two forms in many variables of different degrees, when the difference between degrees is sufficiently large. Furthermore, the method described in this paper is flexible enough to apply to forms in many variables of differing degrees in general.

</details>


### [198] [Zero-Density Concentration for Dirichlet Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17875)
*Eric Dubon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了广泛有限 Dirichlet 级数的归一化竖直零点密度会在单一直线上确定性集中，并给出一般判别准则：归一化 Jessen 势局部一致收敛到带单一角点的分段线性凸函数，故描述零点实部分布的归一化测度弱收敛到 Dirac 质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限 Dirichlet 级数零点的竖直分布，希望刻画零点实部在竖直平均意义下是否渐近集中到一条临界线；这对 Riemann zeta 的部分和、固定 Dirichlet L-函数及 Hecke 特征形式等对象具有统一意义。

Method: 将有限 Bohr 提升与针对孤立素坐标的平移一致反集中估计相结合，建立一般判别准则：若归一化 Jessen 势局部一致收敛到仅有一个角点的分段线性凸函数，则相应归一化零点实部测度弱收敛到 Dirac 质量。

Result: 对 Riemann zeta 函数部分和、固定 Dirichlet L-函数，以及固定 level、平凡 Dirichlet 特征、无复乘的原始全纯 Hecke 特征形式均验证该准则。每个固定截断下零点可占据非平凡的实部范围，但渐近上归一化竖直密度集中到一条线；归一化情形下集中线为 Re s=1/2，而对权 k 形式的经典 Fourier 系数则为 Re s=k/2。

Conclusion: 该结果给出有限 Dirichlet 级数零点竖直密度单线集中的一般机制，并统一覆盖若干数论重要对象；零点实部的渐近分布退化为 Dirac 质量，表明归一化后竖直零点密度具有确定性集中现象。

Abstract: We prove that broad families of finite Dirichlet sums exhibit deterministic concentration of normalized vertical zero density on a single line. The main result gives a general criterion under which the normalized Jessen potentials converge locally uniformly to a piecewise-linear convex function with a single corner. The associated normalized measures describing the distribution of real parts of zeros in vertical mean density then converge weakly to a Dirac mass. Thus, for each fixed truncation, zeros may occupy a nontrivial range of real parts, while asymptotically their normalized vertical density concentrates on one line.
  The proof combines the finite Bohr lift with a translation-uniform anti-concentration estimate for isolated prime coordinates. We apply the criterion to partial sums of the Riemann zeta function, fixed Dirichlet $L$-functions, and primitive holomorphic Hecke eigenforms of fixed level, trivial Dirichlet character, and without complex multiplication. The concentration line is $\operatorname{Re} s=1/2$ in the normalized setting and becomes $\operatorname{Re} s=k/2$ for the classical Fourier coefficients of a form of weight $k$.

</details>


### [199] [The non-spherical sup-norm problem for $\mathrm{GL}(n)$ and generalized spherical functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19121)
*Valentin Blomer,Gergely Harcos,Péter Maga,Djordje Milićević*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文解决了GL(n,Z)\GL(n,R)上任意尖点表示中最小权向量的sup-norm问题，给出了关于阿基米德数据（谱参数及最小K-型维数）一致幂次节省的界，其关键工具是广义球函数的新一致衰减界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sup-norm问题旨在量化自守形式的L∞范数相对其L2范数的增益，是高维自守形式解析理论的核心问题之一。此前结果多针对特定表示或在界中依赖于非一致参数，本文希望得到对任意尖点表示一致有效的幂次节省界。

Method: 通过建立广义球函数（generalized spherical functions）的新一致衰减界，并将其应用于GL(n,Z)\GL(n,R)上尖点表示的最小权向量，推导sup-norm界。

Result: 得到任意尖点表示π的最小权向量的sup-norm界，其幂次节省对于所有π一致，并以π的阿基米德数据（谱参数、最小K-型维数）显式表出；同时给出广义球函数的一致衰减界。

Conclusion: 该工作将sup-norm问题的定量估计推广到GL(n)的任意尖点表示，且界限关于阿基米德参数一致，主要创新在于广义球函数的一致衰减工具。

Abstract: We solve the sup-norm problem for minimal weight vectors in an arbitrary cuspidal representation $π$ of $\mathrm{GL}(n,\mathbb{Z})\backslash\mathrm{GL}(n,\mathbb{R})$ with a uniform power saving bound in terms of the archimedean data of $π$, including its spectral parameters and the dimension of its minimal $K$-type. As a key ingredient, we establish new uniform decay bounds for generalized spherical functions.

</details>


### [200] [Unramified Geometric Iwasawa theory of function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17975)
*Bryden Cais,Daniel Lewis*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究正特征代数闭域上光滑射影连通曲线的非分歧 $\mathbf{Z}_p$-塔，用 Iwasawa 理论刻画其 Jacobian 的 Newton 多边形，证明第 $n$ 层"新部分"的斜率最终是 $p^{-n}$ 的有理系数仿射函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正特征 $p>0$ 代数闭域 $k$ 上曲线塔的 Jacobian 在 tower 中随层数演化的算术/几何结构（如 Newton 多边形）尚缺乏系统的 Iwasawa 理论描述，尤其"新部分"斜率的增长规律是理解该塔分歧与同源类群结构的关键。

Method: 对 $k$ 上光滑、射影、连通曲线构成的非分歧 $\mathbf{Z}_p$-塔 $\{X_n\}_{n\ge 0}$，引入 Iwasawa 理论框架，将其 Jacobian 分解为旧部分与新部分，并分析新部分在层级 $n$ 上的 Newton 多边形斜率随 $n$ 的渐近行为。

Result: 证明第 $n$ 层新部分的 Newton 多边形斜率最终（对充分大的 $n$）是 $p^{-n}$ 的仿射函数，且其系数为有理数。

Conclusion: 该结果给出 $\mathbf{Z}_p$-塔 Jacobian Newton 多边形斜率的精确渐近公式，将正特征下曲线塔的 Iwasawa 理论与 Newton 多边形理论联系起来，为后续研究塔的几何与算术不变量提供了定量工具。

Abstract: For an algebraically closed field $k$ of characteristic $p>0$, we study unramified $\mathbf{Z}_p$-towers $\{X_n\}_{n\ge 0}$ of smooth, projective and connected curves over $k$. Our main result provides an Iwasawa-theoretic description of the Newton polygons of their Jacobians, establishing that the slopes of the new part at level $n$ are eventually affine functions of $p^{-n}$ with rational coefficients.

</details>


### [201] [Surjectivity of the Enots Wolley Sequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18054)
*Nathan Myles Nichols*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明 Enots Wolley 序列包含所有具有至少两个不同素因子的正整数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证 Enots Wolley 序列的满射性质，即刻画哪些正整数必然出现在序列中。

Method: 反证法：假设某合格整数被遗漏，取其素因子集合 T。分析被 T 中至少一个素数整除的极大连续段的起点与长度结构；用素数交换构造给出相反不等式；通过加权双重计数得出固定因子的超出；再用素数递归与不相交覆盖论证排除有限的最终素覆盖。解析数论输入仅用素数定理与 Mertens 倒素数和估计。

Result: 被遗漏整数的素因子集合 T 所对应的极大连续段只能在有限多处出现，且素数递归迫使足够晚的项必被 T 中某素数整除；最终排除有限最终素覆盖，从而证明满射。

Conclusion: Enots Wolley 序列包含每个至少有两个不同素因子的正整数，证明仅依赖素数定理与 Mertens 估计。

Abstract: We prove that the Enots Wolley sequence contains every positive integer with at least two distinct prime divisors. Suppose an eligible integer is omitted, and let T be its finite set of prime divisors. There is a finite cutoff such that any maximal run of terms divisible by at least one prime of T and beginning after the cutoff starts with a term divisible by every prime of T and has length at most two. If infinitely many such runs occur, then terms divisible by some but not all primes of T can outnumber terms divisible by all of them by at most a fixed constant. A prime-exchange construction gives the opposite inequality at arbitrarily large scales: after discarding a negligible exceptional set, a weighted double count produces a fixed-factor excess of the former terms. Hence only finitely many such runs occur. Prime recurrence then forces every sufficiently late term to be divisible by some prime of T. Finally, a disjoint-cover argument rules out any finite eventual prime cover, proving surjectivity. The only analytic number-theoretic inputs are the prime number theorem and Mertens' estimate for reciprocal primes.

</details>


### [202] [Continuous approximation to the reciprocal sum of the cubes of Fibonacci numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18179)
*WonTae Hwang,Jond-Do Park,Kyunghwan Song*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出求解倒数斐波那契数立方尾项整数部分的完整统一代数方法，构造显式序列 g_n 并建立上下界，最终完全确定 s=3 时的取整函数精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倒数斐波那契数尾项整数部分的精确公式已在若干情形获得，但立方情形（s=3）因误差项高度振荡而复杂得多，难以同时构造精确的连续逼近与代数估计，故需新的统一方法。

Method: 构造保持主部 f_n^3 - f_{n-1}^3 的显式闭式序列 g_n，使其与尾项倒数之差趋于零；通过分解误差项并考察代数恒等式，建立 g_n 与尾项倒数之间的上下界估计。

Result: 证明了极限为零，并对充分大的 n 建立双边不等式 g_n < (∑_{k=n}^∞ 1/f_k^3)^{-1} < g_n + 2/f_n；据此完全确定了 s=3 时取整函数（floor）的精确值。

Conclusion: 该代数方法有效克服了立方情形误差项振荡带来的困难，为倒数斐波那契数尾项整数部分问题提供了统一、可推广的处理框架。

Abstract: Let $f_n$ be the $n$-th Fibonacci number with $f_1=f_2=1$. Recently exact formulas for the integer parts of the tails of inverse reciprocal Fibonacci numbers have been obtained in several cases. However, the cubic case ($s=3$) is much more complicated because of highly oscillating error terms. Thus it is difficult to construct a precise continuous approximation and algebraic estimates simultaneously. In this paper, we give a complete and unified algebraic method to solve this difficulty. More precisely we construct an explicit closed form of sequence $g_n$, preserving the principal part $f_n^3-f_{n-1}^3$, such that $\ds \lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\left\{ \left( \sum^\infty_{k=n}\frac{1}{f_k^3} \right)^{-1}-g_n \right\}=0. $
By decomposing the error terms and investigating the algebraic identities, we establish the lower and upper bounds $
\ds g_n<\left( \sum^\infty_{k=n}\frac{1}{f_k^3} \right)^{-1}<g_n+2/f_n $
for sufficiently large $n$. As an application of the explicit form of the sequence $g_n$ and these estimates, we completely determine the exact value of the floor function for $s=3$.

</details>


### [203] [Exact universal normalizations for the Gál--Koksma lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18198)
*Ying Wai Lee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Gál–Koksma引理在抽象连续块二阶矩假设下的普适规范化问题，给出了非降规范化有效的充要可和性判据，该判据在特定最坏情形下是锐利的，并确定了临界对数与重对数阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gál–Koksma引理是在无独立性、混合性或正交性假设下，将连续块上的二次均值估计转化为部分和的几乎处处界的标准工具。自然产生的开放问题是：仅由该假设本身，究竟哪些普适增长规范化是必然被强制的，即刻画在整个可行类上一致有效的非降规范化。

Method: 在抽象的连续块二阶矩假设下研究普适规范化问题，刻画哪些非降规范化在整个可行类上一致有效，并给出相应的充要可和性判据。

Result: 得到了必要且充分的充要可和性判据。该判据即使对具有常控制项和精确线性块方差的有界严格中心系统也是锐利的，并据此确定了临界对数与重对数阈值。

Conclusion: 普适规范化问题在抽象连续块二阶矩假设下被完全解决，充要可和性判据是锐利的，并精确刻画了临界对数与重对数阈值。

Abstract: The Gál--Koksma lemma is a standard tool for converting quadratic-mean estimates on consecutive blocks into almost-everywhere bounds for partial sums, without assumptions of independence, mixing, or orthogonality. A natural open problem is to determine exactly which universal growth normalizations are forced by this hypothesis alone. The corresponding universal normalization problem under the abstract consecutive-block second-moment hypothesis is resolved by characterizing exactly which non-decreasing normalizations are valid uniformly over the entire admissible class. The resulting necessary-and-sufficient summability criterion is sharp even for bounded exactly centred systems with constant majorants and exact linear block variance, and determines the critical logarithmic and iterated-logarithmic thresholds.

</details>


### [204] [Nonvanishing of ray class $L$-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18220)
*Thurman Ye,Xu Zhuang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究虚二次域上射线类L-函数中心值的非零问题，证明当模趋于无穷时，至少1/3比例的原始射线类特征满足L(1/2,χ)≠0，方法是推广经典mollifier方法并建立一、二阶mollified矩的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心值非零比例是解析数论中L-函数研究的核心问题之一（如非零比例、矩估计）。经典mollifier方法在有理数域或一般数域上的Dirichlet L-函数已有丰富结果，但在虚二次域上射线类L-函数的射线类特征族中尚缺乏相应的非零比例刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将经典mollifier（软化因子）方法推广到射线类（ray class）情形；针对虚二次域K上模𝔣的原始射线类特征χ，构造合适的mollifier，并计算/推导对应的第一与第二mollified矩的渐近公式。

Result: 证明了当N(𝔣)→∞时，原始射线类特征χ模𝔣中满足L(1/2,χ)≠0的比例至少为1/3−o_K(1)。同时给出了第一与第二mollified矩的渐近公式。

Conclusion: 在虚二次域射线类L-函数族中获得了正比例（≥1/3）的中心值非零结果，表明mollifier方法可有效迁移至射线类设置，为后续提高非零比例或推广到其他数域族提供了框架。

Abstract: We study the nonvanishing of central values of ray class $L$-functions over a fixed imaginary quadratic field $K$. We prove that, as $\mathrm{N}(\mathfrak f)$ tends to infinity, at least a proportion $1/3-\mathsf{o}_K(1)$ of the primitive ray class characters $χ$ modulo $\mathfrak f$ satisfy $L(1/2,χ)\neq 0$. Our proof adapts the classical mollifier method to the ray class setting and establishes asymptotic formulas for the first and second mollified moments.

</details>


### [205] [A $q$-recurrence for a finite Apéry limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18271)
*Henrik Bachmann*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Tasaka关于$Z(3)$与某个递推式两解之商相关的猜想，并借助二指标$q$-递推与有限调和$q$-级数，在单位根处得到$3Z(3)/4$与$3\zeta(3)/4$的代数极限和解析极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kaneko-Zagier猜想预测有限多重zeta值与对称多重zeta值之间存在对应关系，其中$\zeta(3)$对应由Bernoulli数定义的$Z(3)$。理解$Z(3)$的结构及其递推刻画，有助于推进该对应关系的研究，而Tasaka提出的相关猜想尚待证明。

Method: 证明Tasaka猜想，建立$Z(3)$与某递推式两解之商之间的联系；引入二指标$q$-递推，将该商与有限调和$q$-级数相联系；再运用作者与Takeyama、Tasaka的方法，在单位根处取代数极限与解析极限。

Result: 证明了Tasaka的猜想，即$Z(3)$确实与某递推式两解之商相关；通过$q$-递推与有限调和$q$-级数的联系，在单位根处分别得到代数极限$3Z(3)/4$和解析极限$3\zeta(3)/4$。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了Kaneko-Zagier对应框架下$Z(3)$的递推刻画，并揭示了有限调和$q$-级数与对称多重zeta值在单位根极限下的定量联系，为相关猜想与$q$-级数方法的应用提供了新证据。

Abstract: The Kaneko-Zagier conjecture predicts a correspondence between finite and symmetric multiple zeta values. Under this correspondence, $ζ(3)$ corresponds to an element $Z(3)$ defined by Bernoulli numbers. We prove a conjecture of Tasaka relating $Z(3)$ to the quotient of two solutions of a recurrence. A two-index $q$-recurrence connects this quotient to a finite harmonic $q$-series. Using a method of the author, Takeyama, and Tasaka, we obtain the algebraic and analytic limits $3Z(3)/4$ and $3ζ(3)/4$ at roots of unity.

</details>


### [206] [Graded Algebras of Modular Forms and Sign Changes of Fourier Coefficients for Eta Quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18339)
*Jianwen Gan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对N=8,12,16,20,32确定了Γ₀(N)在整权与半整权、带模N二次Dirichlet特征下的模形式分次代数的显式结构，给出eta商生成元及其关系，构造eta商基，并研究三个特定空间中Fourier系数符号的周期性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要显式掌握特定层次Γ₀(N)（N=8,12,16,20,32）的模形式代数结构，并利用eta商构造便于计算的基，以研究模形式Fourier系数符号的周期性问题。

Method: 对N=8,12,16,20,32，确定整权与半整权、带所有模N二次Dirichlet特征的模形式分次代数结构；给出显式eta商生成元并确定其关系；据此对给定权、层次和二次特征构造由eta商组成的基；再利用这些基推导三个特定空间中模形式Fourier系数符号周期性的充要条件。

Result: 得到了上述层次的模形式分次代数的显式结构及eta商生成元与关系；获得相应空间的eta商基；给出三个特定空间中Fourier系数符号周期性的充要条件；并确定了这些空间中所有Fourier系数符号具有周期性的eta商。

Conclusion: 该工作为特定层次的模形式提供了显式的eta商代数描述与可计算基，并完整刻画了相关空间中Fourier系数符号的周期性及其对应的eta商。

Abstract: In this paper, for $N=8,12,16,20,32$, we determine the explicit structure of the graded algebra of modular forms for $\varGamma_0(N)$ of both integral and half-integral weights, with all quadratic Dirichlet characters modulo $N$. We give explicit eta quotient generators and determine the relations among them. Using these structures, for a given weight, a level among those considered above, and a quadratic character, we obtain a basis for the corresponding space of modular forms consisting of eta quotients. Using these eta quotient bases, we obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for the periodicity of the signs of the Fourier coefficients of modular forms in three specific spaces. Finally, we determine all eta quotients in these spaces whose Fourier coefficient signs are periodic.

</details>


### [207] [Bounded intervals containing a given number of primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18414)
*Keiju Sono*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了满足#([n,n+f(n)]∩P)=m的整数n≤x的显式下界，推广了Mastrostefano与Freiberg的结果，并使Mastrostefano的下界显式化；同时证明当区间长度充分大时，存在无穷多个等长有界区间恰好包含指定数目的素数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于短区间内素数个数固定（等于m）的计数问题，Mastrostefano和Freiberg已给出相关结果，但Mastrostefano的下界是非显式的。作者希望给出显式下界，并推广已有结论。

Method: 设m为非负整数，f(x)为正的、非减的并满足一定条件的函数；对足够大的x，显式估计满足#([n,n+f(n)]∩P)=m的整数n≤x的个数下界。

Result: 得到了该计数函数的显式下界；使Mastrostefano的下界显式化，并推广了Mastrostefano与Freiberg的结果；进一步证明当区间长度充分大时，存在无穷多个相同长度的有界区间恰好包含指定数目的素数。

Conclusion: 本文在给定条件下建立了短区间素数计数为固定值m的显式下界，推广并强化了已有工作，并给出了关于等长有界区间素数数目无穷性的结论。

Abstract: Let $m$ be a non-negative integer and $f(x)$ a positive, non-decreasing function satisfying certain conditions. We give an explicit lower bound for the number of integers $n\leq x$ such that $\#([n, n+f(n)] \cap \mathbb{P})=m$ for sufficiently large $x$, where $\mathbb{P}$ denotes the set of prime numbers. This work extends the results of Mastrostefano and of Freiberg, and also makes the lower bound of Masrtrostefano explicit. In addition, we show that if the interval length is sufficiently large, then there exist infinitely many bounded intervals of the same length that contain exactly a prescribed number of primes.

</details>


### [208] [Extreme Values of Quadratic Dirichlet $L$-Functions over Monic Irreducible Polynomials in $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18452)
*Ahammad Mostafa Hossain*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上超椭圆曲线相关二次Dirichlet L-函数中心值的极值问题，在q固定、g→∞的渐近设定下给出新的Ω-结果，改进了Darbar-Maiti (2024)的界，并研究了中心线附近及σ∈(1/2,1)处L(σ,χ_P)的极值下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次Dirichlet L-函数中心值及极值问题是解析数论的核心问题之一。在函数域情形，Darbar-Maiti (2024)最近给出了中心值极值的下界，Lumley (2021)研究了L(σ,χ_P)的极值。本文旨在改进这些已有下界，并进一步考察中心线附近的极值行为，深化对该族L-函数极端取值的理解。

Method: 在q固定、g→∞的渐近框架下，考虑F_q[t]中次数为2g+1的首一不可约多项式集合P_{2g+1}，对中心值|L(1/2,χ_P)|建立新的Ω-结果；并推广至中心线附近及1/2<σ<1的L(σ,χ_P)极值，利用解析数论方法（如矩方法/共振法等）得到下界估计。

Result: 对任意ε∈(0,1/2)，证明 max_{P∈P_{2g+1}} |L(1/2,χ_P)| ≫ exp( (√((√q+1)/(√q-1)(1/2-ε))·ln q + o(1)) · √(g ln₂g/ln g) )，改进了Darbar-Maiti (2024)的结果；同时对1/2<σ<1及足够大的n，得到L(σ,χ_P)（P∈P_n）极值的改进下界，优于Lumley (2021)的结果。

Conclusion: 本文在函数域上给出了二次Dirichlet L-函数中心值极值的更强下界，并拓展至中心线附近及σ∈(1/2,1)的情形，统一改进并推广了Darbar-Maiti (2024)与Lumley (2021)的工作，为理解该族L-函数的极端取值提供了更精确的定量刻画。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a new $Ω$-result for the central values $|L(1/2,χ_P)|$ of quadratic Dirichlet $L$-functions, where $P$ ranges over monic irreducible polynomials associated with hyperelliptic curves of genus $g$ over a fixed finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$. We consider the asymptotic setting in which $q$ is fixed and $g\to\infty$. More precisely, for every $ε\in (0,1/2)$, we prove that \[
  \max_{P \in \mathcal{P}_{2g+1}} |L(1/2, χ_P)|
  \gg \exp \left( \left( \sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{q}+1}{\sqrt{q}-1} (1/2-ε)} \, \, \ln q + o(1) \right) \sqrt{\frac{g \ln_2 g}{\ln g}} \right),
  \] where $\mathcal{P}_{2g+1}$ is the set of all monic irreducible polynomials of degree $ 2g+1$ in $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$. Our result extends the recent work of Darbar and Maiti (2024) and yields an improved lower bound for the extreme values in this family. we also investigate the extreme values of these quadratic $L$-functions near the central line. In addition, for $1/2<σ<1$ and sufficiently large $n$, we study the extreme values of $L(σ,χ_P)$, where $P\in\mathcal{P}_n$, and obtain an improved lower bound compared with the result of Lumley (2021).

</details>


### [209] [Cyclotomic Prime Extractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18480)
*Joseph M. Shunia*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用分圆多项式值 $\Phi_n(2)$ 构造了显式的素数恢复公式：通过二进制整除模式检测重复素因子并确定无平方指数的最小素因子，结合对 $\log_2\Phi_n(2)$ 的小修正逐步恢复各相异素因子；并将该机制推广到无穷素数序列，由奇数原基（odd primorials）上分圆值的归一化极限定义一个实常数 $\Omega=0.25061403238015047218\ldots$，据其可通过递归取整规则恢复每个奇素数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建立分圆多项式值与素数分布之间的显式联系，将 $\Phi_n(2)$ 的算术/二进制结构转化为可直接恢复素数（素因子、最小素因子、给定整数之上的最小素数等）的具体公式，并把有限分解的机制推广为能编码全部奇素数的无穷序列常数。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 分析 $\Phi_n(2)$ 的二进制整除模式，用以检测重复素因子并识别无平方指数的最小素因子；(2) 对 $\log_2\Phi_n(2)$ 施加小修正，逐步恢复不同的素因子；(3) 通过对指数取特殊值导出素数乘积与给定整数之上最小素数的恒等式；(4) 沿奇数原基取分圆值的归一化极限，定义常数 $\Omega$ 并给出递归取整恢复规则。

Result: 得到若干显式素数恢复公式；对特定指数给出素数乘积与"给定整数之上最小素数"的恒等式；定义出实数常数 $\Omega=0.25061403238015047218\ldots$，并证明可由其通过递归取整规则恢复每一个奇素数。

Conclusion: 分圆多项式值可作为编码素数信息的载体，其二进制结构与对数尺度行为足以支撑对素因子的显式恢复；该机制可从有限分解自然延伸到无穷素数序列，表明全部奇素数的信息可被压缩进单一实常数 $\Omega$ 中。

Abstract: We develop explicit prime-recovery formulas from the values $Φ_n(2)$ of cyclotomic polynomials. Binary divisibility patterns detect repeated prime factors and identify the least prime divisor of a squarefree index, while small corrections to $\log_2Φ_n(2)$ allow successive recovery of the distinct prime factors. Specializing the index gives identities for prime products and the least prime above a given integer.
  The same mechanism extends from finite factorizations to an infinite prime sequence: a normalized limit of cyclotomic values along the odd primorials defines a real constant $Ω=0.25061403238015047218\ldots$, from which every odd prime can be recovered by a recursive rounding rule.

</details>


### [210] [Periodicity conjectures for all 2-sumfree sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18522)
*Daan van Berkel,Wieb Bosma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究递增2-无和序列S_{f,g}，提出可推出其最终周期性的精确猜想，并提供大量计算证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作聚焦3-无和序列并证明特定参数下S_{f,g}最终周期；本文转向2-无和序列，试图刻画更一般的最终周期性规律。

Method: 构造递增2-无和序列S_{f,g}：在任意初始段后，下一项为超过此前所有项、且不等于序列中任意两个不同元素之和的最小正整数；结合前作定理并给出精确猜想与计算验证。

Result: 对任意f≥1且f+1≤g<2f，S_{f,g}表现出最终周期行为；提出的猜想若成立则所有2-无和序列最终周期。大量计算数据支持这些猜想。

Conclusion: 2-无和序列可能普遍具有最终周期性，周期性的判定等价于一阶差分序列或特征序列的周期性；猜想尚待证明。

Abstract: Complementing an earlier paper, which focused on $3$-sumfree sequences, we here consider only $2$-sumfree sequences: starting with positive integers $f$ and $g>f$, the infinite, increasing 2-sumfree sequence $S_{f,g}$ is constructed as follows. After any initial segment, the next entry is the smallest positive integer exceeding all previous ones that differs from all sums of distinct pairs in the sequence. It follows from a theorem in the previous paper that for every $f\geq 1$ and all $f+1\leq g<2f$ the sequence $S_{f,g}$ exhibits ultimately periodic behaviour. In this paper we state precise conjectures that, if true, would imply that every $2$-sumfree sequence is ultimately periodic. Here periodicity of an increasing sequence is understood to mean periodicity of the sequence of first differences, or, equivalently, of its characteristic sequence. We supply much computational evidence to support the conjectures.

</details>


### [211] [Extreme values of Euler-Kronecker constants of cubic abelian fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18569)
*Yuichiro Toma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在广义黎曼假设下，给出了阿贝尔三次域与二次域上Euler-Kronecker常数的极值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euler-Kronecker常数是数域上Euler-Mascheroni常数的推广，刻画其极值行为有助于理解数域的不变量分布。

Method: 对Hecke L-函数假设广义黎曼假设（GRH）。

Result: 得到阿贝尔三次域Euler-Kronecker常数的极值，并证明二次域有类似结果。

Conclusion: Euler-Kronecker常数在数域上呈现与经典Euler-Mascheroni常数类似的极值现象。

Abstract: Euler-Kronecker constants analogues of the Euler-Mascheroni constant for number fields. Assuming the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis for Hecke $L$-functions, we obtain extreme values of Euler-Kronecker constants of abelian cubic fields. We also prove a similar result for quadratic fields.

</details>


### [212] [On Multiple Eisenstein Series in Positive Characteristic: Direct Sum Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18777)
*Chieh-Yu Chang,Song-Yun Chen,Fei-Jun Huang,Hung-Chun Tsui*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究正特征下的多重Eisenstein级数（MES），通过计算并分析其t-展开式确定系数的精确"权"，由此建立MES的分次代数结构，推广了[Cha14]关于Thakur多重zeta值的直和结果，并可作为Bachmann-Kanno[BK26]相应直和结果在函数域上的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重Eisenstein级数是多重zeta值的自然推广，在正特征（函数域）情形下的代数结构尚未充分刻画。作者希望将[Cha14]关于Thakur多重zeta值直和分解的结果以及Bachmann-Kanno[BK26]的相应结果推广到MES的框架中，因此需要先精确确定MES系数的权，以便建立分次代数结构。

Method: 计算并分析MES的t-展开式，据此确定其系数的精确"权"；利用该权框架建立MES的分次代数结构。

Result: 确定了MES系数的精确权，建立了MES的分次代数结构，从而将[Cha14]中Thakur多重zeta值的直和结果加以推广。

Conclusion: 本文的结果可视为Bachmann-Kanno[BK26]相应直和结果在函数域上的类比，表明MES在正特征下具有良好的分次代数结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multiple Eisenstein series (MES) in positive characteristic. By computing and analyzing the t-expansions of MES, we determine the precise "weights" of their coefficients. This framework enables us to establish a graded algebra structure for MES, extending the direct sum result for Thakur's multiple zeta values proved in [Cha14]. Our result may also be viewed as a function field analogue of the corresponding direct sum result of Bachmann and Kanno [BK26].

</details>


### [213] [On a conjecture of Browning and Sawin on random hypersurfaces with sign coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18879)
*Ken Ono,Ashvin Swaminathan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了Browning-Sawin猜想：随机的±1系数超曲面随次数增长几乎必然光滑，并给出定量界与绝对不可约性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Browning与Sawin关于随机符号系数超曲面光滑性概率趋于1的猜想，并给出定量化结果。

Method: 考虑n+1个变量、次数d的形式，其系数独立均匀取自{-1,1}，分析其定义的复超曲面的奇异性与不可约性。

Result: 含奇点的概率为O_n(d^{-1/2})；正维奇点轨迹出现概率指数小；n≥3时绝对不可约性失效也具指数界。结果在Lean中由AxiomProver形式化。

Conclusion: Browning-Sawin猜想成立，随机符号系数超曲面高概率光滑且（n≥3）绝对不可约，并已被形式化验证。

Abstract: Browning and Sawin conjectured that random hypersurfaces with sign coefficients are smooth with probability tending to one as the degree grows. We prove this conjecture and obtain a quantitative bound. For each $n\geq1$, a degree $d$ form in $n+1$ variables, with independent uniform coefficients in $\{-1,1\}$, defines a singular complex hypersurface with probability $O_n(d^{-1/2})$. The positive-dimensional singular loci occur with exponentially small probability. For $n\geq3$, the same exponential bound holds for failure of absolute irreducibility. These results have been formalized in Lean by AxiomProver assuming existing literature.

</details>


### [214] [A uniform effective André--Oort result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18934)
*Guy Fowler*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了由方程 a_1 x_1^m + ... + a_n x_n^m = b 定义的高维超曲面上的 André–Oort 猜想，并首次给出关于系数高度的有效且一致的证明，同时推广到了 Y(1)^n × G_m^l 中的超曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: André–Oort 猜想是该领域核心问题，但已有证明通常非有效，且缺乏对系数高度的一致性。作者希望针对一类具有任意维数、特殊轨迹非空的子簇，建立有效且一致的结果。

Method: 对形如 a_1 x_1^m + ... + a_n x_n^m = b 的高维超曲面 V ⊂ Y(1)^n ≅ A^n_C 证明 André–Oort 猜想，证明关于系数 a_1,...,a_n,b 的高度既有效又一致；并将方法推广到 Y(1)^n × G_m^l 中的类似超曲面。

Result: 证明了上述超曲面类的 André–Oort 猜想，且证明有效并关于系数高度一致；这是首个针对任意维数且特殊轨迹非空的子簇类的有效一致 André–Oort 结论。Y(1)^n × G_m^l 中的类似结果亦成立。

Conclusion: 结果为一大类高维、特殊轨迹非空的子簇提供了有效且一致的 André–Oort 证明，并拓展至混合情形，推动了猜想研究的定量化发展。

Abstract: We prove the André--Oort conjecture for hypersurfaces $V \subset Y(1)^n \cong \mathbb{A}^n_\mathbb{C}$ defined by an equation $a_1 x_1^m + \ldots + a_n x_n^m = b$, where $a_1, \ldots, a_n, b \in \overline{\mathbb{Q}}$ and $m \in \mathbb{Z}_{>0}$. Unlike previous proofs, our result is both effective and uniform in the height of the coefficients $a_1, \ldots, a_n, b$. This is the first effective proof of a uniform André--Oort statement for a class of subvarieties with arbitrary dimension and non-empty special locus. We also prove an analogous result for hypersurfaces $V \subset Y(1)^n \times \mathbb{G}_m^l$.

</details>


### [215] [An Omega result for partial sums of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18938)
*Qiyu Yang,Shengbo Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用长共振方法与光滑数的有效渐近公式，建立了Riemann zeta函数部分和在1-线上的Omega结果；对于充分长的部分和，该结果逼近Aistleitner、Mahatab与Munsch（2019）的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Riemann zeta函数在临界线（1-线）上部分和的振荡行为，改进/逼近已有最佳Omega下界结果。

Method: 长共振方法（long resonance method）结合光滑数的有效渐近公式。

Result: 对充分长的部分和得到新的Omega结果，其强度逼近Aistleitner–Mahatab–Munsch（2019）的结果。

Conclusion: 长共振方法与光滑数渐近公式可有效给出zeta部分和部分和的振荡下界，良好逼近现有最优结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an Omega result for partial sums of the Riemann zeta function on the \(1\)-line. Our work relies on the long resonance method and an effective asymptotic formula for smooth numbers. For sufficiently long partial sums, our result approaches the work obtained by Aistleitner, Mahatab and Munsch in 2019.

</details>


### [216] [$Ω$-results for the logarithmic derivative of $ζ(s)$ on vertical homogeneous progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18939)
*Shengbo Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在临界带及1-线上的垂直齐次等差数列上，建立了Riemann zeta函数对数导数的Ω-结果，表明离散情形与连续情形具有相似的量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与Yang（2023）的工作进行比较，探究在垂直齐次等差数列（离散情形）上Riemann zeta函数对数导数的下界估计，考察离散情形是否与连续情形具有相同量级。

Method: 采用解析数论方法，在1-线上及临界带内沿垂直齐次等差数列研究ζ′/ζ的Ω-结果（即下界型估计）。

Result: 得到了垂直齐次等差数列上ζ′/ζ的Ω-结果，并证明相较于Yang（2023）的结论，离散情形与连续情形在量级上相似。

Conclusion: 在垂直齐次等差数列上，Riemann zeta函数对数导数的离散估计与连续估计具有相似量级，拓展了Yang（2023）的相关结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish $Ω$-results for the logarithmic derivative of the Riemann zeta function on vertical homogeneous progressions on the 1-line and in the critical strip. Compared with Yang's work in 2023, our results show that the discrete case has a similar order of magnitude to the continuous case.

</details>


### [217] [Arithmetic Constraints and Limit Laws for Diagonal Rational Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18945)
*K. Srinivasa Raghava*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文研究将整数 m 分拆为分子分母不超过 N 的既约正分数（排除整数部分）的无序分拆计数 R_N(m)，给出了 log R_N(⌊ρN⌋) 在 N^{3/2}/log N 精度下的渐近公式，并分析了均匀分拆中素数分母块的结构、Ehrhart 分子及其极点与拟多项式系数的关系、留数分布的不对称性及高斯极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典整数分拆理论中，分拆块为整数；本文将其推广到既约分数块（分子分母有界），以刻画更一般的"分数分拆"计数问题，并借此联系格点距离积分、Ehrhart 理论及概率极限定理等工具。

Method: 通过将分拆块限定为分子分母不超过 N 的既约正分数，运用鞍点法/生成函数渐近分析得到 R_N 的对数渐近展开；利用格点距离积分表示显式给出 κ(ρ)；对固定 N 借助 Ehrhart 理论计算分子多项式并由极点分析拟多项式系数；进一步通过概率采样模型研究留数分布与矩，并证明高斯极限。

Result: 对 ρ 在紧正区间一致地有 log R_N(⌊ρN⌋)=√(2ρ)N^{3/2}−κ(ρ)N^{3/2}/log N+o(N^{3/2}/log N)，其中 κ 为正的连续可微函数，κ(1)≈0.00264713。均匀分拆中，区间 (n/2,n] 内除 o_P(n/log n) 个素数分母块外，其余块的总和为 2 或 3，取决于 p/n 是否低于或高于约 0.7522 的显式阈值。固定 N 时给出 Ehrhart 分子并确定其极点对拟多项式系数的控制；N≥4 时留数分布不对称，但在低于 ⌈N/2⌉ 阶的所有矩上与独立模型一致，并指出首个偏差并证明高斯极限；还得到两种采样规则下的联合分母与大小分布律。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了分数块分拆的精细渐近理论，揭示了 κ(ρ) 的显式格点积分结构、素数分母块的二/三值现象、Ehrhart 多项式的极点控制机制以及留数分布在高阶矩上与独立模型的偏离，为分拆理论的分数化推广提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Let $R_N(m)$ count unordered partitions of $m$ into reduced positive fractions whose numerators and denominators are at most $N$, excluding integer parts. Uniformly for $ρ$ in compact positive intervals, $\log R_N(\lfloorρN\rfloor)=\sqrt{2ρ}\,N^{3/2}-κ(ρ)N^{3/2}/\log N+o(N^{3/2}/\log N)$. The positive, continuously differentiable function $κ$ is an explicit sum of lattice-distance integrals, with $κ(1)\approx 0.00264713$. For a uniform partition of $n$, all but $o_{\mathbb{P}}(n/\log n)$ prime-denominator blocks in $(n/2,n]$ have total 2 or 3, according to whether $p/n$ lies below or above an explicit threshold near 0.7522. For fixed $N$, we give the Ehrhart numerator and determine how its poles control quasipolynomial coefficients. Its residue distribution is asymmetric for $N\ge 4$, but agrees with an independent model in every moment below order $\lceil N/2\rceil$. We identify the first discrepancy and prove a Gaussian limit. We also obtain joint denominator and size laws under two sampling rules.

</details>


### [218] [Finite Fourier Duality, Radial Values, and Arithmetic of a Mod Eight False Theta Quotient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18956)
*K. Srinivasa Raghava*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究一类模八的假theta矩商，给出其在单位根处的径向行为、精确Fourier闭合、函数方程与Dirichlet值的非零性，并证明系数分解、插值与Euler变换指数正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画模八假theta矩商在单位根处的径向渐近与有限值行为，建立其周期边界序列的精确Fourier闭合，并由此导出函数方程与相关周期Dirichlet值的非零性。

Method: 对周期边界序列作有限Fourier闭合分析；构造函数方程；利用精确边界估计与Möbius反演；建立系数级二进插值并定位公分母的唯一主导零点。

Result: 每个矩商或呈现通用的标准化阶乘渐近，或取有限非零径向值；有限值属于分圆域、满足Galois协变，并在主根处特化为带符号的奇数Springer数；得到精确系数整除性与最优系数级二进插值，识别公分母唯一主导零点并给出无条件的系数分解；证明分母形式Euler变换中每个指数为正。

Conclusion: 模八假theta矩商的径向行为由精确Fourier闭合控制，进而给出函数方程与Dirichlet值非零性，并统一了渐近、Galois协变、系数分解、二进插值与Euler变换正性等结构结果。

Abstract: We study a family of mod-eight false-theta moment quotients and determine their radial behaviour at every root of unity. The main structural result establishes an exact finite Fourier closure for the associated periodic boundary sequences. This closure yields a completed functional equation and proves the nonvanishing of the relevant periodic Dirichlet values at all negative odd integers. Consequently, each quotient exhibits either universal normalized factorial asymptotics or a finite nonzero radial value. The finite values belong to cyclotomic fields, satisfy Galois covariance, and specialize at the principal root to signed odd Springer numbers. We also determine exact coefficient divisibility, construct an optimal coefficientwise two-adic interpolation, and identify the unique dominant zero of the common denominator. This zero gives an unconditional coefficient decomposition. Finally, using exact boundary estimates and Möbius inversion, we prove positivity of every exponent in the formal Euler transform of the denominator.

</details>


### [219] [Transcendence criteria for the minimal word of the rational base $3/2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19007)
*Ralf Stephan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由递推 $x_{n}=\lceil 3x_{n-1}/2\rceil$ 生成的词 $w_{n}=2x_{n+1}-3x_{n}\in\{0,1\}$（对应有理数基 $3/2$ 数系的最小词 $g_{3/2}$）及其编码常数 $K=\lim_{n}x_{n}(2/3)^{n}$。作者证明：若 $w$ 是自动词，则 $K$ 是超越数（等价地，代数 $K$ 蕴含 $w$ 非自动）；并证明要么 $w$ 的复杂度超过任何线性界，要么 $K$ 是无理数。所有结果在 Lean 4 中形式化验证，基于三条公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常数 $K$（对 $x_0=1$ 即 $\omega_{3/2}\approx 1.6222705028$）的无理性自 1977 年以来一直是公开问题。该研究旨在建立常数 $K$ 的超越性与词 $w$ 自动性之间的联系，并借助自动词与线性复杂度等组合工具来推进关于 $K$ 的无理性/超越性研究。
